Create `$D/mig_T1_head.sql` with exactly (sections 1–3: the masker, captions, the pair marker and the held identity):

```sql
-- supabase/migrations/20261120100000_dating_v2_chat.sql
-- Spec 2 W6a (§4.9 W6a, §3.6, §4.3, §4.4, §4.5, §6.1, §7.1, §7.5 mutants 10-12;
-- carries 1-7): contact masking (one masker, the card's captions masked
-- instead of dropped); the pair marker (a one-way code for a pair that
-- passed, blocked or ended it, read by generation's never-introduced-before
-- filter, kept 2 years); the dating chat (messages, the chat signals, send,
-- read, the conversations list, swap, block and unmatch with fixed
-- answers); the kill switch reads both members of an open pair (carry 5);
-- act names the match it made (carry 7); open chats and first messages
-- read the messages (carry 6). Dating messages never enter public.messages,
-- relationship_message_counters or the couples safety outbox. No flag
-- changes. Grants: 20261120100010_dating_v2_chat_grants.sql.
BEGIN;
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';
SET LOCAL client_min_messages = warning;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1. Contact masking (§6.1; carry 3). private.dating_mask_contacts(text)
--    is the one masker: dating_messages.body_masked at insert, photo
--    captions on the card. supabase/functions/_shared/dating_mask.ts is its
--    line-by-line TypeScript mirror; both run
--    supabase/tests/fixtures/dating_mask_cases.json (mask_version 1).
--    Each span becomes "•••". The shadow folds each character once:
--    ASCII to lower case, anything else through Spec 1's own forms (I-17:
--    private.normalize_for_filter, then private.match_form while still not
--    ASCII), so there is no fold table here.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_mask_fold(p_c text)
RETURNS text
LANGUAGE plpgsql
IMMUTABLE PARALLEL SAFE
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v text;
BEGIN
  IF pg_catalog.ascii(p_c) < 128 THEN
    RETURN CASE WHEN p_c ~ '^[\t\n\v\f\r ]$' THEN ' ' ELSE pg_catalog.lower(p_c COLLATE "C") END;
  END IF;
  -- Read between two "0"s: a separator stays a space, a removed character
  -- leaves nothing.
  v := private.normalize_for_filter('0' || p_c || '0');
  v := pg_catalog.substr(v, 2, pg_catalog.char_length(v) - 2);
  IF v ~ '[^\u0001-\u007f]' THEN
    v := private.match_form('0' || v || '0');
    v := pg_catalog.substr(v, 2, pg_catalog.char_length(v) - 2);
  END IF;
  RETURN pg_catalog.regexp_replace(v, '[\U00010000-\U0010FFFF]', U&'\FFFD', 'g');
END;
$$;

-- The leetspeak view (one character for one): a digit, @ or $ next to a
-- letter read as that letter; 1 as [p_one] ('i' or 'l'); twice.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_mask_leet(p_s text, p_one text)
RETURNS text
LANGUAGE plpgsql
IMMUTABLE PARALLEL SAFE
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v text := p_s;
  r record;
BEGIN
  FOR i IN 1..2 LOOP
    FOR r IN SELECT d, l FROM (VALUES (1, '4', 'a'), (2, '3', 'e'), (3, '0', 'o'), (4, '5', 's'), (5, '7', 't'),
                                      (6, '@', 'a'), (7, '\$', 's'), (8, '1', p_one)) AS t(k, d, l) ORDER BY k LOOP
      v := pg_catalog.regexp_replace(v, '(?<=[a-z])' || r.d || '|' || r.d || '(?=[a-z])', r.l, 'g');
    END LOOP;
  END LOOP;
  RETURN v;
END;
$$;

-- The digits view (one character for one): calendar dates, year ranges and
-- times blanked to x; then o next to a digit as 0, l or i as 1; twice.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_mask_digits(p_s text)
RETURNS text
LANGUAGE plpgsql
IMMUTABLE PARALLEL SAFE
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v text := p_s;
  d text;
  a integer;
  m text;
BEGIN
  FOREACH d IN ARRAY ARRAY[
      '(?<![0-9])[0-9]{1,2}[/.-][0-9]{1,2}[/.-](?:[0-9]{4}|[0-9]{2})(?![0-9])',
      '(?<![0-9])[0-9]{4}[/.-][0-9]{1,2}[/.-][0-9]{1,2}(?![0-9])',
      '(?<![0-9])(?:19|20)[0-9]{2} ?[-/] ?(?:(?:19|20)[0-9]{2}|[0-9]{2})(?![0-9])',
      '(?<![0-9])[0-9]{1,2}:[0-5][0-9](?![0-9])'] LOOP
    a := 1;
    LOOP
      a := pg_catalog.regexp_instr(v, d, a);
      EXIT WHEN a = 0;
      m := pg_catalog.regexp_substr(v, d, a);
      v := overlay(v PLACING pg_catalog.repeat('x', pg_catalog.char_length(m)) FROM a FOR pg_catalog.char_length(m));
      a := a + pg_catalog.char_length(m);
    END LOOP;
  END LOOP;
  FOR i IN 1..2 LOOP
    v := pg_catalog.regexp_replace(v, '(?<=[0-9])o|o(?=[0-9])', '0', 'g');
    v := pg_catalog.regexp_replace(v, '(?<=[0-9])[li]|[li](?=[0-9])', '1', 'g');
  END LOOP;
  RETURN v;
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_mask_contacts(p_text text)
RETURNS text
LANGUAGE plpgsql
IMMUTABLE PARALLEL SAFE
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_stop constant text[] := pg_catalog.string_to_array('a about account again also am an and are at banned be blocked but call chat closed dead deleted different dm dms down for from gone handle here i id im in is it its later link locked me my name new no not now number of off ok okay old on or page please pls private public same so that the then there this to too up us user username video was we with yes you your', ' ');
  v_platform constant text[] := pg_catalog.string_to_array('snapchat telegram whatsapp watsapp instagram facebook onlyfans linkedin youtube cashapp tiktok paypal wechat whtsapp instgrm twitter discord snpcht wassap reddit tlgrm botim viber venmo whtsp fcbk momo kik thegram messenger threads signal insta snap line imo', ' ');
  v_after constant text := '^((?:[ :=@_-]+(?:is|its|id|handle|username|user|name|account|page)(?![a-z0-9]))*[ :=@_-]+(@?[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_.-]*[a-z0-9_])(?![a-z0-9]))';
  v_chars text[];
  v_cache jsonb := '{}'::jsonb;
  v_f text;
  v_x text;
  v_s text := '';
  v_chg text := '';
  v_pos integer[] := '{}';
  v_li text;
  v_ll text;
  v_dv text;
  v_view text;
  v_from integer;
  v_a integer;
  v_b integer;
  v_m text;
  v_h text;
  v_g text[];
  v_ok boolean;
  v_end integer;
  v_c text;
  v_n integer;
  v_word boolean;
  v_t text;
  v_sa integer[] := '{}';
  v_sb integer[] := '{}';
  v_out text := '';
  v_i integer := 1;
  v_last_a integer;
  v_last_b integer;
  r record;
  s record;
BEGIN
  IF p_text IS NULL THEN
    RETURN NULL;
  END IF;
  -- 1. The shadow, with each character's place in the text and whether
  --    folding changed it (a character outside ASCII).
  v_chars := pg_catalog.regexp_split_to_array(p_text, '');
  FOR i IN 1..coalesce(pg_catalog.array_length(v_chars, 1), 0) LOOP
    v_f := v_cache ->> v_chars[i];
    IF v_f IS NULL THEN
      v_f := private.dating_mask_fold(v_chars[i]);
      v_cache := v_cache || pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(v_chars[i], v_f);
    END IF;
    FOREACH v_x IN ARRAY pg_catalog.regexp_split_to_array(v_f, '') LOOP
      CONTINUE WHEN v_x = '';
      v_s := v_s || v_x;
      v_pos := v_pos || i;
      v_chg := v_chg || CASE WHEN pg_catalog.ascii(v_chars[i]) < 128 THEN '0' ELSE '1' END;
    END LOOP;
  END LOOP;
  -- 2. The views.
  v_li := private.dating_mask_leet(v_s, 'i');
  v_ll := private.dating_mask_leet(v_s, 'l');
  v_dv := private.dating_mask_digits(v_s);
  -- 3. The rules, in order; a stop word as the handle rejects a match.
  FOR r IN
    SELECT t.k, t.name, t.view, t.re, t.grp, t.chk FROM (VALUES
      (1, 'strong', 'li', '(?<![a-z0-9])(?:snap[ _-]?chat|tele[ _-]?gram|whats[ _-]?app|wats[ _-]?app|insta[ _-]?gram|face[ _-]?book|only[ _-]?fans|linked[ _-]?in|you[ _-]?tube|cash[ _-]?app|tik[ _-]?tok|pay[ _-]?pal|we[ _-]?chat|whtsapp|instgrm|twitter|discord|snpcht|wassap|reddit|tlgrm|botim|viber|venmo|whtsp|fcbk|momo|kik)(?![a-z0-9])(?:[ :=@_-]+(?:is|its|id|handle|username|user|name|account|page)(?![a-z0-9]))*[ :=@_-]+(@?[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_.-]*[a-z0-9_])(?![a-z0-9])', 1, 'names'),
      (2, 'strong', 'll', '(?<![a-z0-9])(?:snap[ _-]?chat|tele[ _-]?gram|whats[ _-]?app|wats[ _-]?app|insta[ _-]?gram|face[ _-]?book|only[ _-]?fans|linked[ _-]?in|you[ _-]?tube|cash[ _-]?app|tik[ _-]?tok|pay[ _-]?pal|we[ _-]?chat|whtsapp|instgrm|twitter|discord|snpcht|wassap|reddit|tlgrm|botim|viber|venmo|whtsp|fcbk|momo|kik)(?![a-z0-9])(?:[ :=@_-]+(?:is|its|id|handle|username|user|name|account|page)(?![a-z0-9]))*[ :=@_-]+(@?[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_.-]*[a-z0-9_])(?![a-z0-9])', 1, 'names'),
      (3, 'weak_said', 'li', '(?<![a-z0-9])(?:the[ _-]?gram|messenger|threads|signal|insta|snap|line|imo|sc|ig|fb|tg|yt|wa|x)(?![a-z0-9])(?: *[:=@] *| +(?:is|id|handle|username) *[:=]? *)(@?[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_.-]*[a-z0-9_])(?![a-z0-9])', 1, NULL),
      (4, 'weak_said', 'll', '(?<![a-z0-9])(?:the[ _-]?gram|messenger|threads|signal|insta|snap|line|imo|sc|ig|fb|tg|yt|wa|x)(?![a-z0-9])(?: *[:=@] *| +(?:is|id|handle|username) *[:=]? *)(@?[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_.-]*[a-z0-9_])(?![a-z0-9])', 1, NULL),
      (5, 'my_platform', 'li', '(?<![a-z0-9])(?:my|mine|our)(?: +|_)(?:snap[ _-]?chat|tele[ _-]?gram|whats[ _-]?app|wats[ _-]?app|insta[ _-]?gram|face[ _-]?book|only[ _-]?fans|linked[ _-]?in|you[ _-]?tube|cash[ _-]?app|tik[ _-]?tok|pay[ _-]?pal|we[ _-]?chat|whtsapp|instgrm|twitter|discord|snpcht|wassap|reddit|tlgrm|botim|viber|venmo|whtsp|fcbk|momo|kik|the[ _-]?gram|messenger|threads|signal|insta|snap|line|imo|sc|ig|fb|tg|yt|wa|x)(?![a-z0-9])(?:[ _]+(?:handle|id|name|username|account|page)(?![a-z0-9]))?(?:[ _]+is(?![a-z0-9]))?[ :=@_-]+(@?[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_.-]*[a-z0-9_])(?![a-z0-9])', 1, NULL),
      (6, 'weak_handle', 'li', '(?<![a-z0-9])(?:the[ _-]?gram|messenger|threads|signal|insta|snap|line|imo|sc|ig|fb|tg|yt|wa|x)(?![a-z0-9])[ :=_-]+(@?(?:[a-z0-9_.-]*[0-9_.][a-z0-9_.-]*[a-z0-9_]|[0-9]+))(?![a-z0-9])', 1, NULL),
      (7, 'evasive', 'li', '(?<![a-z0-9])(instagram|messenger|facebook|linkedin|onlyfans|snapchat|telegram|whatsapp|cashapp|discord|instgrm|thegram|threads|twitter|watsapp|whtsapp|youtube|paypal|reddit|signal|snpcht|tiktok|wassap|wechat|botim|insta|tlgrm|venmo|viber|whtsp|fcbk|line|momo|snap|imo|kik)(?![a-z0-9])', NULL, 'evasive'),
      (8, 'evasive', 'll', '(?<![a-z0-9])(instagram|messenger|facebook|linkedin|onlyfans|snapchat|telegram|whatsapp|cashapp|discord|instgrm|thegram|threads|twitter|watsapp|whtsapp|youtube|paypal|reddit|signal|snpcht|tiktok|wassap|wechat|botim|insta|tlgrm|venmo|viber|whtsp|fcbk|line|momo|snap|imo|kik)(?![a-z0-9])', NULL, 'evasive'),
      (9, 'spaced', 'li', '(?<![a-z0-9])[a-z](?:[ ._-][a-z]){2,}(?![a-z0-9])', NULL, 'spaced'),
      (10, 'on_platform', 'li', '(?<![a-z0-9])(@?[a-z0-9_.-]*[0-9_.][a-z0-9_.-]*[a-z0-9_]) +on +(?:snap[ _-]?chat|tele[ _-]?gram|whats[ _-]?app|wats[ _-]?app|insta[ _-]?gram|face[ _-]?book|only[ _-]?fans|linked[ _-]?in|you[ _-]?tube|cash[ _-]?app|tik[ _-]?tok|pay[ _-]?pal|we[ _-]?chat|whtsapp|instgrm|twitter|discord|snpcht|wassap|reddit|tlgrm|botim|viber|venmo|whtsp|fcbk|momo|kik|the[ _-]?gram|messenger|threads|signal|insta|snap|line|imo|sc|ig|fb|tg|yt|wa|x)(?![a-z0-9])', 1, NULL),
      (11, 'on_platform_alone', 'li', '^ *([a-z0-9][a-z0-9_.-]*[a-z0-9_]) +on +(?:snap[ _-]?chat|tele[ _-]?gram|whats[ _-]?app|wats[ _-]?app|insta[ _-]?gram|face[ _-]?book|only[ _-]?fans|linked[ _-]?in|you[ _-]?tube|cash[ _-]?app|tik[ _-]?tok|pay[ _-]?pal|we[ _-]?chat|whtsapp|instgrm|twitter|discord|snpcht|wassap|reddit|tlgrm|botim|viber|venmo|whtsp|fcbk|momo|kik|the[ _-]?gram|messenger|threads|signal|insta|snap|line|imo|sc|ig|fb|tg|yt|wa|x) *[.!]* *$', 1, NULL),
      (12, 'line_id', 'li', '(?<![a-z0-9])line[ _-]?id(?![a-z0-9])[ :=_-]*(@?[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_.-]*[a-z0-9_])(?![a-z0-9])', 1, NULL),
      (13, 'reddit', 's', '(?<![a-z0-9/])u/[a-z0-9_-]+', NULL, NULL),
      (14, 'handle_is', 'li', '(?<![a-z0-9])(?:handle|username|user[ _-]?name|user[ _-]?id)(?: +is)?[ :=@_-]+(@?[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_.-]*[a-z0-9_])(?![a-z0-9])', 1, NULL),
      (15, 'at_handle', 's', '(?<![a-z0-9_.@])@[a-z0-9_]+(?:\.[a-z0-9_]+)*', NULL, NULL),
      (16, 'email', 's', '[a-z0-9._%+-]*[a-z0-9]@ ?[a-z0-9-]+(?:\.[a-z0-9-]+)*', NULL, NULL),
      (17, 'email', 's', '[a-z0-9._%+-]*[a-z0-9] @[a-z0-9-]+(?:\.[a-z0-9-]+)*', NULL, NULL),
      (18, 'email', 's', '(?<![a-z0-9])[a-z0-9._%+-]*[a-z0-9] +@ +(?:protonmail|googlemail|hotmail|outlook|icloud|proton|yahoo|gmail|ymail|aol|gmx)(?![a-z0-9])(?:\.[a-z]+)*', NULL, NULL),
      (19, 'provider', 's', '(?<![a-z0-9])([a-z0-9._-]+) +(?:at +|\(at\) *|\[at\] *)?(?:protonmail|googlemail|hotmail|outlook|icloud|proton|yahoo|gmail|ymail|aol|gmx)(?![a-z0-9])(?: *(?:\.|dot(?![a-z0-9])|\(dot\)) *(?:com|net|co|uk)(?![a-z0-9]))*', 1, NULL),
      (20, 'provider', 's', '(?<![a-z0-9])(?:at +|\(at\) *|\[at\] *)?(?:protonmail|googlemail|hotmail|outlook|icloud|proton|yahoo|gmail|ymail|aol|gmx)(?![a-z0-9])(?: *(?:\.|dot(?![a-z0-9])|\(dot\)) *(?:com|net|co|uk)(?![a-z0-9]))*', NULL, NULL),
      (21, 'spelled_email', 's', '(?<![a-z0-9])[a-z0-9._-]+(?: dot [a-z0-9_-]+)* *(?: at |\(at\)|\[at\]) *[a-z0-9-]+(?: +[a-z0-9-]{2,})? *(?: dot |\(dot\)|\[dot\]| \. ) *(?:info|link|app|biz|com|edu|gov|net|org|xyz|co|gh|io|ly|me|ng|uk)(?![a-z0-9])(?:(?: dot |\.)(?:info|link|app|biz|com|edu|gov|net|org|xyz|co|gh|io|ly|me|ng|uk)(?![a-z0-9]))*', NULL, NULL),
      (22, 'spelled_domain', 's', '(?<![a-z0-9])[a-z0-9-]+ (?:dot|\.) (?:info|link|app|biz|com|edu|gov|net|org|xyz|co|gh|io|ly|me|ng|uk)(?![a-z0-9])(?: (?:dot|\.) (?:info|link|app|biz|com|edu|gov|net|org|xyz|co|gh|io|ly|me|ng|uk)(?![a-z0-9]))*', NULL, NULL),
      (23, 'url', 's', 'https?://[^ ]+|(?<![a-z0-9])www\.[^ ]+', NULL, NULL),
      (24, 'domain', 's', '(?<![a-z0-9])[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*(?:\.[a-z0-9-]+)*\.(?:info|link|app|biz|com|edu|gov|net|org|xyz|co|gh|io|ly|me|ng|uk)(?![a-z0-9])(?:/[^ ]*)?', NULL, NULL),
      (25, 'domain_path', 's', '(?<![a-z0-9])[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*\.[a-z]{2,}/[^ ]+', NULL, NULL),
      (26, 'cashtag', 's', '(?<![a-z0-9$])\$[a-z][a-z0-9_]*', NULL, NULL),
      (27, 'crypto', 's', '(?<![a-z0-9])(?:0x[0-9a-f]{40}|bc1[0-9a-z]{25,62}|t[1-9a-z]{33})(?![a-z0-9])', NULL, NULL),
      (28, 'phone', 'dv', '(?<![0-9])\+?\(?[0-9](?:[ ._/()-]{0,3}[0-9]){6,}\)?', NULL, NULL),
      (29, 'number_words', 's', '(?<![a-z0-9])(?:mmiensa|mmienu|nwotwe|triple|double|baako|seven|three|eight|nkron|hwee|enan|enum|four|five|nine|nsia|nson|zero|one|six|two|oh|o|[0-9]+)(?![a-z0-9])(?:[ ,.-]+(?:mmiensa|mmienu|nwotwe|triple|double|baako|seven|three|eight|nkron|hwee|enan|enum|four|five|nine|nsia|nson|zero|one|six|two|oh|o|[0-9]+)(?![a-z0-9]))+', NULL, 'number_words')
    ) AS t(k, name, view, re, grp, chk) ORDER BY t.k
  LOOP
    v_view := CASE r.view WHEN 's' THEN v_s WHEN 'li' THEN v_li WHEN 'll' THEN v_ll ELSE v_dv END;
    v_from := 1;
    LOOP
      EXIT WHEN v_from > pg_catalog.char_length(v_view) + 1;
      v_a := pg_catalog.regexp_instr(v_view, r.re, v_from);
      EXIT WHEN v_a = 0;
      v_m := pg_catalog.regexp_substr(v_view, r.re, v_a);
      v_b := v_a + pg_catalog.char_length(v_m);
      v_ok := pg_catalog.char_length(v_m) > 0;
      IF v_ok AND r.grp IS NOT NULL THEN
        v_h := pg_catalog.ltrim(coalesce(pg_catalog.regexp_substr(v_view, r.re, v_a, 1, '', r.grp), ''), '@');
        IF v_h = ANY (v_stop) THEN
          v_ok := false;
        END IF;
      END IF;
      IF v_ok AND r.chk = 'evasive' THEN
        v_ok := pg_catalog.substr(v_s, v_a, v_b - v_a) <> pg_catalog.substr(v_view, v_a, v_b - v_a)
             OR pg_catalog.strpos(pg_catalog.substr(v_chg, v_a, v_b - v_a), '1') > 0;
      END IF;
      IF v_ok AND r.chk = 'spaced' THEN
        v_ok := pg_catalog.translate(v_m, ' ._-', '') = ANY (v_platform);
      END IF;
      IF v_ok AND r.chk IN ('evasive', 'spaced') THEN
        -- its handle, if one follows
        v_g := pg_catalog.regexp_match(pg_catalog.substr(v_view, v_b), v_after);
        IF v_g IS NOT NULL AND NOT (pg_catalog.ltrim(v_g[2], '@') = ANY (v_stop)) THEN
          v_b := v_b + pg_catalog.char_length(v_g[1]);
        END IF;
      END IF;
      IF v_ok AND r.chk = 'number_words' THEN
        v_n := 0;
        v_word := false;
        FOREACH v_t IN ARRAY pg_catalog.regexp_split_to_array(v_m, '[ ,.-]+') LOOP
          CONTINUE WHEN v_t = '';
          IF v_t ~ '^[0-9]+$' THEN
            v_n := v_n + pg_catalog.char_length(v_t);
          ELSE
            v_n := v_n + 1;
            v_word := true;
          END IF;
        END LOOP;
        v_ok := v_word AND v_n >= 7;
      END IF;
      IF v_ok AND r.chk = 'names' THEN
        -- "facebook Ama Boateng": up to two more capitalised words
        v_end := v_b;
        FOR j IN 1..2 LOOP
          v_g := pg_catalog.regexp_match(pg_catalog.substr(v_view, v_end), '^( +([a-z][a-z''-]*[a-z])(?![a-z0-9]))');
          EXIT WHEN v_g IS NULL;
          v_c := v_chars[v_pos[v_end + pg_catalog.char_length(v_g[1]) - pg_catalog.char_length(v_g[2])]];
          EXIT WHEN v_c IS NULL OR v_c = pg_catalog.lower(v_c COLLATE "pg_c_utf8") OR v_g[2] = ANY (v_stop);
          v_end := v_end + pg_catalog.char_length(v_g[1]);
        END LOOP;
        v_b := v_end;
      END IF;
      IF v_ok THEN
        v_sa := v_sa || v_pos[v_a];
        v_sb := v_sb || (v_pos[v_b - 1] + 1);
        v_from := v_b;
      ELSE
        v_from := v_a + 1;
      END IF;
    END LOOP;
  END LOOP;
  -- 4. The spans, merged, each "•••".
  FOR s IN SELECT u.a, u.b FROM unnest(v_sa, v_sb) AS u(a, b) ORDER BY u.a, u.b LOOP
    IF v_last_a IS NOT NULL AND s.a <= v_last_b THEN
      v_last_b := greatest(v_last_b, s.b);
    ELSE
      IF v_last_a IS NOT NULL THEN
        v_out := v_out || pg_catalog.array_to_string(v_chars[v_i:v_last_a - 1], '') || '•••';
        v_i := v_last_b;
      END IF;
      v_last_a := s.a;
      v_last_b := s.b;
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
  IF v_last_a IS NOT NULL THEN
    v_out := v_out || pg_catalog.array_to_string(v_chars[v_i:v_last_a - 1], '') || '•••';
    v_i := v_last_b;
  END IF;
  RETURN v_out || coalesce(pg_catalog.array_to_string(v_chars[v_i:pg_catalog.array_length(v_chars, 1)], ''), '');
END;
$$;

-- Fail closed for presentation (§6.1): if the masker raises, every digit
-- run and every @, / or URL-like token is masked; delivery never waits.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_mask_contacts_safe(p_text text)
RETURNS text
LANGUAGE plpgsql
IMMUTABLE PARALLEL SAFE
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
BEGIN
  RETURN private.dating_mask_contacts(p_text);
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
  RETURN pg_catalog.regexp_replace(p_text, '[0-9]+|[^[:space:]]*[@/.$][^[:space:]]*', '•••', 'g');
END;
$$;

-- Captions only: an invitation to move off the app with nothing to mask
-- ("DM me", "add me on snap"); the card drops such a caption.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_caption_invites(p_text text)
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE plpgsql
IMMUTABLE PARALLEL SAFE
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_s text := '';
  v_li text;
  v_ll text;
  v_c text;
  v_re text;
BEGIN
  IF p_text IS NULL THEN
    RETURN false;
  END IF;
  FOREACH v_c IN ARRAY pg_catalog.regexp_split_to_array(p_text, '') LOOP
    v_s := v_s || private.dating_mask_fold(v_c);
  END LOOP;
  v_li := private.dating_mask_leet(v_s, 'i');
  v_ll := private.dating_mask_leet(v_s, 'l');
  FOREACH v_re IN ARRAY ARRAY[
      '(?<![a-z0-9])(?:add|find|message|contact|hit|dm|text|call|ping|inbox|follow) +(?:me|us)(?![a-z0-9])',
      '(?<![a-z0-9])(?:slide|come) +into +my +dms?(?![a-z0-9])',
      '(?<![a-z0-9])(?:snap[ _-]?chat|tele[ _-]?gram|whats[ _-]?app|wats[ _-]?app|insta[ _-]?gram|face[ _-]?book|only[ _-]?fans|linked[ _-]?in|you[ _-]?tube|cash[ _-]?app|tik[ _-]?tok|pay[ _-]?pal|we[ _-]?chat|whtsapp|instgrm|twitter|discord|snpcht|wassap|reddit|tlgrm|botim|viber|venmo|whtsp|fcbk|momo|kik|the[ _-]?gram|messenger|threads|signal|insta|snap|line|imo|sc|ig|fb|tg|yt|wa|x) +(?:me|us)(?![a-z0-9])',
      '(?<![a-z0-9])of +link(?![a-z0-9])',
      '(?<![a-z0-9])link +in +(?:my +)?bio(?![a-z0-9])'] LOOP
    IF v_li ~ v_re OR v_ll ~ v_re THEN
      RETURN true;
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
  RETURN false;
END;
$$;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2. Captions (carry 3, owner 2026-11-20 "Mask contacts on card"): each
--    caption is masked once, when it is written, so the card reads it
--    without masking per view; a caption that only invites someone off
--    the app ("DM me") stays off the card, as one the content filter
--    flags as unkind does.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
ALTER TABLE public.dating_profile_photos
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS caption_masked text,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS caption_invites boolean;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_photo_caption_mask()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
BEGIN
  NEW.caption_masked := CASE WHEN NEW.caption IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE private.dating_mask_contacts_safe(NEW.caption) END;
  NEW.caption_invites := CASE WHEN NEW.caption IS NULL THEN false ELSE coalesce(private.dating_caption_invites(NEW.caption), true) END;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS dating_photo_caption_mask ON public.dating_profile_photos;
CREATE TRIGGER dating_photo_caption_mask
  BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF caption ON public.dating_profile_photos
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION private.dating_photo_caption_mask();

-- Every caption there is today (the trigger fires on the caption column).
UPDATE public.dating_profile_photos SET caption = caption WHERE caption IS NOT NULL;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2b. Carry 5: the identity a person held before a move, encrypted, read
--     only by the kill switch to judge their open pairs (see section 4).
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
ALTER TABLE public.dating_profile_sensitive ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS held_identity_enc bytea;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3. The pair marker (owner decision 2026-11-20, carry 1). When a pair
--    passes ("Not for me"), blocks, unmatches or closes kindly, a one-way
--    code of the two phones' HMACs is kept: no name, no id, no phone, not
--    even which of them acted. It outlives leaving Dating and deleting
--    either account (it is keyed to the numbers, not the accounts), so the
--    pair is never introduced again after one of them leaves and comes
--    back; generation's never-introduced-before filter reads it. Kept 2
--    years (purge_after), then deleted by purge_dating_retention (W6c).
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.dating_pair_markers (
  pair_code text PRIMARY KEY CONSTRAINT dating_pair_markers_code_check CHECK (pair_code ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  purge_after timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now() + interval '2 years'
);
ALTER TABLE public.dating_pair_markers ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS dating_pair_markers_purge_idx ON public.dating_pair_markers (purge_after);

-- The code of the pair with these two phone HMACs: keyed with the
-- exclusion key (an HMAC of the two HMACs in order), so it can be checked
-- only for two numbers already in hand, and never read back as either.
-- NULL without the key or either HMAC (nothing is kept then).
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_pair_code(p_ha text, p_hb text)
RETURNS text
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_key constant text := public.app_setting('dating_exclusion_key');
BEGIN
  IF p_ha IS NULL OR p_hb IS NULL OR p_ha = p_hb OR coalesce(v_key, '') = '' THEN
    RETURN NULL;
  END IF;
  RETURN pg_catalog.encode(extensions.hmac('dating_pair_marker|' || least(p_ha, p_hb) || '|' || greatest(p_ha, p_hb),
                                           v_key, 'sha256'), 'hex');
END;
$$;

-- Keeps the marker of the pair [p_a], [p_b]; never raises: a pair it
-- can't code (no key, a member with no phone) is counted, and the caller
-- goes on.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_mark_pair(p_a uuid, p_b uuid)
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_code text;
BEGIN
  IF p_a IS NULL OR p_b IS NULL OR p_a = p_b THEN
    RETURN false;
  END IF;
  v_code := private.dating_pair_code(public.dating_phone_hmac_for_user(p_a), public.dating_phone_hmac_for_user(p_b));
  IF v_code IS NULL THEN
    PERFORM private.bump_metric_internal('dating.pair_marker.unwritten');
    RETURN false;
  END IF;
  INSERT INTO public.dating_pair_markers (pair_code) VALUES (v_code) ON CONFLICT (pair_code) DO NOTHING;
  RETURN true;
END;
$$;

-- §5.2 14 v2: never introduced before, and no marker for the pair's
-- numbers (the people's HMACs, as generation holds them).
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_pair_new(p_a uuid, p_b uuid, p_ha text, p_hb text)
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_introductions AS i WHERE i.pair_key = public.dating_pair_key(p_a, p_b))
     AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_matches AS m WHERE m.pair_key = public.dating_pair_key(p_a, p_b))
     AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_pair_markers AS k WHERE k.pair_code = private.dating_pair_code(p_ha, p_hb));
$$;

-- The pairs that already passed, blocked or unmatched (development data;
-- production has none). Only with the key.
DO $$
BEGIN
  IF coalesce(public.app_setting('dating_exclusion_key'), '') <> '' THEN
    INSERT INTO public.dating_pair_markers (pair_code)
    SELECT private.dating_pair_code(public.dating_phone_hmac_for_user(i.user_low_id), public.dating_phone_hmac_for_user(i.user_high_id))
      FROM public.dating_introductions AS i
     WHERE (i.low_action = 'passed' OR i.high_action = 'passed' OR i.state = 'passed')
    UNION
    SELECT private.dating_pair_code(public.dating_phone_hmac_for_user(m.user_low_id), public.dating_phone_hmac_for_user(m.user_high_id))
      FROM public.dating_matches AS m
     WHERE m.state IN ('unmatched', 'blocked')
    UNION
    SELECT private.dating_pair_code(h.blocker_hmac, h.blocked_hmac) FROM public.dating_block_hmacs AS h
    EXCEPT SELECT NULL
    ON CONFLICT (pair_code) DO NOTHING;
  END IF;
END $$;
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Spec 2 W6a: builds supabase/migrations/20261120100000_dating_v2_chat.sql
# from mig_T1_head.sql (masking, captions, the pair marker), the functions
# below re-created from their LIVE bodies (saved to before_w6a/ before the
# migration is applied) with only the lines marked "-- Spec 2 W6a"
# changed (Task 2's mig_T2_chat.sql, when it exists, goes before them, and
# edits_T2.py adds its edits), and mig_T1_tail.sql (the drops, COMMIT). Every anchor must occur exactly once.
import pathlib, runpy

D = pathlib.Path('.superpowers/sdd/2026-11-20-spec2-w6')
BEFORE = D / 'before_w6a'

EDITS = {
  # 1. Carry 3: the card reads the caption masked at write time; a caption
  #    that only invites someone off the app, or that the filter flags as
  #    unkind, stays off the card (contact details no longer drop it).
  'private.dating_card(uuid,uuid,dating_introductions)': [
    ("""             -- W6a: replace with dating_mask_contacts span masking
             'caption', CASE WHEN private.content_filter_verdict(f.caption, NULL) IS NULL
                              AND coalesce(private.dating_caption_has_contact(f.caption), true) IS FALSE THEN f.caption END) -- fix round 2 (I-3)""",
     """             -- Spec 2 W6a (carry 3): masked once when written, never dropped for a contact detail
             'caption', CASE WHEN private.content_filter_verdict(f.caption, ARRAY['slur', 'explicit']) IS NULL -- Spec 2 W6a
                              AND f.caption_invites IS FALSE THEN f.caption_masked END) -- Spec 2 W6a"""),
  ],
  # 2. Carry 1: the single-pair predicate reads the pair marker.
  'public.dating_pair_passes_hard_filters(uuid,uuid)': [
    ("""                  AND private.dating_pair_new(p_a, p_b), false);""",
     """                  AND private.dating_pair_new(p_a, p_b, v_a.hmac, v_b.hmac), false); -- Spec 2 W6a (carry 1): the pair marker"""),
  ],
  # 3. Carry 1: generation reads it too.
  'private.dating_generate_partition(text,text,text,date,timestamp with time zone,text,jsonb)': [
    ("""             AND private.dating_pair_new(v_u.user_id, a.user_id)""",
     """             AND private.dating_pair_new(v_u.user_id, a.user_id, (v_u.p).hmac, (a.p).hmac) -- Spec 2 W6a (carry 1)"""),
  ],
  # 4. Carry 1: "Not for me" keeps the pair's marker; carry 7: an answer
  #    that made (or found) the match names it.
  'public.act_on_dating_introduction(text,uuid,text)': [
    ("""          'matched', EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_interest_actions AS a
                              JOIN public.dating_matches AS m ON m.introduction_id = a.introduction_id
                             WHERE a.actor_user_id = v_user AND a.idempotency_key = p_idempotency_key));""",
     """          'matched', EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_interest_actions AS a
                              JOIN public.dating_matches AS m ON m.introduction_id = a.introduction_id
                             WHERE a.actor_user_id = v_user AND a.idempotency_key = p_idempotency_key)) -- Spec 2 W6a
          || coalesce((SELECT jsonb_build_object('match_id', m.id) FROM public.dating_interest_actions AS a -- Spec 2 W6a (carry 7)
                         JOIN public.dating_matches AS m ON m.introduction_id = a.introduction_id -- Spec 2 W6a
                        WHERE a.actor_user_id = v_user AND a.idempotency_key = p_idempotency_key), '{}'::jsonb); -- Spec 2 W6a"""),
    ("""        RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'action', v_mine, 'matched', v_i.state = 'matched');""",
     """        RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'action', v_mine, 'matched', v_i.state = 'matched') -- Spec 2 W6a
          || coalesce((SELECT jsonb_build_object('match_id', m.id) FROM public.dating_matches AS m WHERE m.introduction_id = v_i.id), '{}'::jsonb); -- Spec 2 W6a (carry 7)"""),
    ("""      IF p_action = 'interested' AND v_other = 'interested' THEN""",
     """      IF p_action = 'passed' THEN -- Spec 2 W6a (carry 1): never introduced again, even after leaving
        PERFORM private.dating_mark_pair(v_i.user_low_id, v_i.user_high_id); -- Spec 2 W6a
      END IF; -- Spec 2 W6a
      IF p_action = 'interested' AND v_other = 'interested' THEN"""),
    ("""      RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'action', p_action, 'matched', p_action = 'interested' AND v_other IS NOT DISTINCT FROM 'interested');""",
     """      RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'action', p_action, 'matched', p_action = 'interested' AND v_other IS NOT DISTINCT FROM 'interested') -- Spec 2 W6a
        || CASE WHEN p_action = 'interested' AND v_other IS NOT DISTINCT FROM 'interested' -- Spec 2 W6a (carry 7): the match it made
                THEN jsonb_build_object('match_id', coalesce(v_match, (SELECT m.id FROM public.dating_matches AS m WHERE m.introduction_id = v_i.id))) -- Spec 2 W6a
                ELSE '{}'::jsonb END; -- Spec 2 W6a"""),
  ],
  # 5. Carry 1: every block keeps the pair's marker (the card's, the
  #    chat's, any other).
  'public.block_dating_user(text,uuid)': [
    ("""  UPDATE public.dating_introductions
  SET state = 'invalidated'
  WHERE pair_key = v_pair_key""",
     """  PERFORM private.dating_mark_pair(v_user_id, p_target_user_id); -- Spec 2 W6a (carry 1)
  UPDATE public.dating_introductions
  SET state = 'invalidated'
  WHERE pair_key = v_pair_key"""),
  ],
  # 6. Carry 5: a person who moves country is asked for gender and
  #    who-to-meet again; until then the identity they held is kept,
  #    encrypted, for one use: the kill switch judges their open pairs by
  #    it (a man-woman pair was closed as "not known to be man-woman").
  #    Not kept when the move itself found it not allowed there; cleared
  #    when they answer again, by a switch in their country, and by leaving.
  'public.save_dating_location(text,text,text,text,text)': [
    ("""       SET identity_enc = NULL, seeking_enc = NULL, updated_at = now()""",
     """       SET held_identity_enc = CASE WHEN v_restrict THEN NULL ELSE coalesce(s.identity_enc, s.held_identity_enc) END, -- Spec 2 W6a (carry 5)
           identity_enc = NULL, seeking_enc = NULL, updated_at = now() -- Spec 2 W6a"""),
  ],
  'public.save_dating_identity(text,text,text,text,text[],boolean)': [
    ("""       SET identity_enc = private.dating_field_encrypt(v_new),""",
     """       SET identity_enc = private.dating_field_encrypt(v_new),
           held_identity_enc = NULL, -- Spec 2 W6a (carry 5): answered again"""),
  ],
  'private.dating_switch_identity(uuid,jsonb)': [
    ("""                     CROSS JOIN LATERAL private.dating_switch_read(s.identity_enc) AS d""",
     """                     CROSS JOIN LATERAL private.dating_switch_read(coalesce(s.identity_enc, s.held_identity_enc)) AS d -- Spec 2 W6a (carry 5)"""),
  ],
  'public.apply_same_gender_switch(text)': [
    ("""   CROSS JOIN LATERAL private.dating_switch_read(s.identity_enc) AS d""",
     """   CROSS JOIN LATERAL private.dating_switch_read(coalesce(s.identity_enc, s.held_identity_enc)) AS d -- Spec 2 W6a (carry 5)"""),
    ("""           seeking_enc = CASE WHEN v_bad_seeking THEN NULL ELSE s.seeking_enc END,""",
     """           seeking_enc = CASE WHEN v_bad_seeking THEN NULL ELSE s.seeking_enc END,
           held_identity_enc = NULL, -- Spec 2 W6a (carry 5): nothing held in a switched country"""),
  ],
}

extra = D / 'edits_T2.py'
if extra.exists():
    for sig, pairs in runpy.run_path(str(extra))['EDITS'].items():
        EDITS.setdefault(sig, []).extend(pairs)

out = [(D / 'mig_T1_head.sql').read_text().rstrip('\n'), '']
t2 = D / 'mig_T2_chat.sql'
if t2.exists():
    out += [t2.read_text().rstrip('\n'), '']
out += ['-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------',
       '-- 5. Functions re-created from their live bodies; only the lines marked',
       '--    "-- Spec 2 W6a" change.',
       '-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------']
for sig, pairs in EDITS.items():
    body = (BEFORE / f'{sig}.sql').read_text().rstrip('\n')
    for old, new in pairs:
        n = body.count(old)
        assert n == 1, f'{sig}: anchor occurs {n} times: {old[:80]!r}'
        body = body.replace(old, new)
    out += ['', body + ';']
out += ['', (D / 'mig_T1_tail.sql').read_text().rstrip('\n'), '']
pathlib.Path('supabase/migrations/20261120100000_dating_v2_chat.sql').write_text('\n'.join(out))
print(f'20261120100000_dating_v2_chat.sql: {len(EDITS)} functions re-created' + (', the chat section' if t2.exists() else ''))
```

Create `supabase/migrations/20261120100010_dating_v2_chat_grants.sql` with exactly (Task 2 adds the chat's grants):

```sql
-- Spec 2 W6a: grants for 20261120100000_dating_v2_chat.sql. The masker,
-- the caption trigger and the pair marker are the database's own (no API
-- role's, the service role included: a marker is a keyed code of two
-- numbers, and the masker is presentation the server applies). The chat's
-- RPCs are the app's (authenticated only); its tables are no API role's
-- but the chat signals, which a signed-in person reads for their own rows
-- (realtime). dating_messages is revoked from the service role too (S-5;
-- W6b's worker reads bodies only through its claim function).
BEGIN;
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';
REVOKE ALL ON TABLE public.dating_pair_markers FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_mask_fold(text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_mask_leet(text, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_mask_digits(text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_mask_contacts(text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_mask_contacts_safe(text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_caption_invites(text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_photo_caption_mask() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_pair_code(text, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_mark_pair(uuid, uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_pair_new(uuid, uuid, text, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
-- Re-created (CREATE OR REPLACE keeps their grants; re-asserted).
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_card(uuid, uuid, public.dating_introductions) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_generate_partition(text, text, text, date, timestamptz, text, jsonb) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.act_on_dating_introduction(text, uuid, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.act_on_dating_introduction(text, uuid, text) TO authenticated;
COMMIT;
```

```python
     OR v ->> 'match_id' IS DISTINCT FROM (SELECT id::text FROM public.dating_matches WHERE introduction_id = v_wm) -- Spec 2 W6a
     OR (SELECT count(*) FROM public.dating_matches WHERE introduction_id = v_wm AND state = 'active') <> 1"""),

       IS DISTINCT FROM '{"action": "interested", "matched": true, "ok": true}'::jsonb::text""",
   """  IF pg_temp.call(m, pg_temp.dev('42'), format('SELECT public.act_on_dating_introduction(%L, %L, ''interested'')', md5('k-m'), v_wm))
       IS DISTINCT FROM v::text -- Spec 2 W6a (carry 7): the replay names the same match"""),

   """  IF v_card #>> '{photos,1,caption}' IS DISTINCT FROM 'mail me at •••' OR v_card::text ~* 'gmail' -- Spec 2 W6a (carry 3): masked, shown
     OR v_card #>> '{photos,0,caption}' IS DISTINCT FROM 'Me at the beach' THEN"""),
```

```sql
-- =====================================================================
-- F. (§3.6, §4.3; chat spec §2.3, §11) A dating message never enters the
--    couples chat: no row in public.messages, relationship_message_counters,
--    message_safety_outbox or message_notification_outbox; it is masked
--    at insert (mask_version 1, counted) and the recipient's chat signal
--    is bumped (no content in it), never the sender's.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  a constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('30');
  b constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('31', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"]}');
  v_m uuid;
  v_before bigint[];
  v_hits bigint := pg_temp.metric('dating.mask_hit');
  v jsonb;
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (user_low_id, user_high_id, state) VALUES (least(a, b), greatest(a, b), 'active') RETURNING id INTO v_m;
  v_before := ARRAY[(SELECT count(*) FROM public.messages), (SELECT count(*) FROM public.relationship_message_counters),
                    (SELECT count(*) FROM public.message_safety_outbox), (SELECT count(*) FROM public.message_notification_outbox)];
  v := pg_temp.j(a, '30', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''Call me on 0244 123 456'')', pg_temp.key('send'), v_m));
  IF v ->> 'ok' IS DISTINCT FROM 'true' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a member could not send: %', v;
  END IF;
  IF ARRAY[(SELECT count(*) FROM public.messages), (SELECT count(*) FROM public.relationship_message_counters),
           (SELECT count(*) FROM public.message_safety_outbox), (SELECT count(*) FROM public.message_notification_outbox)]
     IS DISTINCT FROM v_before THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a dating message reached the couples chat''s tables';
  END IF;
  IF (SELECT d.body || '|' || d.body_masked || '|' || d.mask_version || '|' || d.sender_side || '|' || d.kind
        FROM public.dating_messages AS d WHERE d.match_id = v_m)
     IS DISTINCT FROM ('Call me on 0244 123 456|Call me on •••|1|' || (CASE WHEN a < b THEN 'low' ELSE 'high' END) || '|text')
     OR pg_temp.metric('dating.mask_hit') <> v_hits + 1 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the message was not stored masked at insert, or the hit was not counted';
  END IF;
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_chat_signals WHERE user_id = b AND match_id = v_m)
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_chat_signals WHERE user_id = a)
     OR (SELECT array_agg(column_name::text ORDER BY ordinal_position) FROM information_schema.columns
          WHERE table_schema = 'public' AND table_name = 'dating_chat_signals') IS DISTINCT FROM ARRAY['user_id', 'match_id', 'bumped_at'] THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the recipient''s signal was not bumped, the sender''s was, or a signal holds more than a bump';
  END IF;
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_publication_tables WHERE pubname = 'supabase_realtime' AND tablename = 'dating_chat_signals')
     AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_publication WHERE pubname = 'supabase_realtime') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the chat signals are not in the realtime publication';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- G. (§3.6, §4.4; Fable C1, H2) Send's refusals, each a fixed answer:
--    invalid input; another phone; an outsider and a match that doesn't
--    exist (one answer); a sender not in good standing; a replay answers the first
--    message and stores none.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  a constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('32');
  b constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('33', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"]}');
  o constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('34');
  v_m uuid;
  k constant text := pg_temp.key('replay');
  v1 jsonb;
  v2 jsonb;
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (user_low_id, user_high_id, state) VALUES (least(a, b), greatest(a, b), 'active') RETURNING id INTO v_m;
  IF pg_temp.j(a, '32', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''   '')', pg_temp.key('blank'), v_m)) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'invalid'
     OR pg_temp.j(a, '32', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, %L)', pg_temp.key('long'), v_m, repeat('x', 1001))) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'invalid'
     OR pg_temp.j(a, '32', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(''short'', %L, ''hi'')', v_m)) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'invalid'
     OR pg_temp.j(a, '32', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''hi'', ''close_kindly'')', pg_temp.key('kind'), v_m)) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'invalid'
     OR pg_temp.call(a, pg_temp.dev('99'), format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''hi'')', pg_temp.key('phone'), v_m))::jsonb ->> 'reason'
        IS DISTINCT FROM 'reverify_device' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: send took invalid input or another phone';
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.j(o, '34', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''hi'')', pg_temp.key('o'), v_m))
     IS DISTINCT FROM pg_temp.j(o, '34', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''hi'')', pg_temp.key('n'), gen_random_uuid()))
     OR pg_temp.j(o, '34', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''hi'')', pg_temp.key('o'), v_m)) ->> 'reason'
        IS DISTINCT FROM 'match_unavailable' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an outsider''s send answered differently from a match that doesn''t exist';
  END IF;
  UPDATE auth.users SET banned_until = now() + interval '1 day' WHERE id = a; -- not in good standing (W7a's holds keep the match open)
  IF pg_temp.j(a, '32', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''hi'')', pg_temp.key('held'), v_m)) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'restricted'
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_messages WHERE match_id = v_m) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a person not in good standing could send';
  END IF;
  UPDATE auth.users SET banned_until = NULL WHERE id = a;
  v1 := pg_temp.j(b, '33', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''Hello there'')', k, v_m));
  v2 := pg_temp.j(b, '33', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''Hello again'')', k, v_m));
  IF v1 ->> 'ok' IS DISTINCT FROM 'true' OR v2 IS DISTINCT FROM v1
     OR (SELECT count(*) FROM public.dating_messages WHERE match_id = v_m) <> 1 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a replay sent a second message or answered differently (% then %)', v1, v2;
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- H. (§3.6, §4.4, §4.5; chat spec §4.4; mutant 12) A blocked or unmatched
--    pair can't send through a stale client: the chat still open, the
--    next send answers closed and stores nothing (a block by account, by
--    phone HMAC alone, an unmatch); no API role can insert a row, and were
--    INSERT ever granted the policy refuses the blocked pair.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  a constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('35');
  b constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('36', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"]}');
  c constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('37');
  e constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('38', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"]}');
  f constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('39');
  g constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('40', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"]}');
  v_ab uuid;
  v_ce uuid;
  v_fg uuid;
  v jsonb;
  v_err text;
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (user_low_id, user_high_id, state) VALUES (least(a, b), greatest(a, b), 'active') RETURNING id INTO v_ab;
  INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (user_low_id, user_high_id, state) VALUES (least(c, e), greatest(c, e), 'active') RETURNING id INTO v_ce;
  INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (user_low_id, user_high_id, state) VALUES (least(f, g), greatest(f, g), 'active') RETURNING id INTO v_fg;
  -- a blocks b from the chat; b's stale screen sends.
  v := pg_temp.j(a, '35', format('SELECT public.block_dating_match(%L, %L)', pg_temp.key('block'), v_ab));
  IF v IS DISTINCT FROM '{"ok": true}'::jsonb OR NOT pg_temp.marked(a, b)
     OR (SELECT state || '/' || closed_reason FROM public.dating_matches WHERE id = v_ab) IS DISTINCT FROM 'blocked/blocked'
     OR NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_chat_signals WHERE user_id = b AND match_id = v_ab) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the chat''s Block answered % (or kept no marker, did not close, did not tell b''s chat)', v;
  END IF;
  -- A block by phone HMAC alone (the match left open, as after a
  -- re-registration): the send still refuses.
  INSERT INTO public.dating_block_hmacs (blocker_hmac, blocked_hmac)
  VALUES (public.dating_phone_hmac_for_user(f), public.dating_phone_hmac_for_user(g));
  v := pg_temp.j(e, '38', format('SELECT public.unmatch_dating_match(%L, %L)', pg_temp.key('unmatch'), v_ce));
  IF v IS DISTINCT FROM '{"ok": true}'::jsonb OR NOT pg_temp.marked(c, e)
     OR (SELECT state || '/' || closed_reason || '/' || (evidence_until > now() + interval '29 days')::text FROM public.dating_matches WHERE id = v_ce)
        IS DISTINCT FROM 'unmatched/unmatched/true' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: unmatch answered % (or kept no marker, or no 30-day evidence window)', v;
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.j(b, '36', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''still there?'')', pg_temp.key('stale1'), v_ab)) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'closed'
     OR pg_temp.j(c, '37', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''still there?'')', pg_temp.key('stale2'), v_ce)) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'closed'
     OR pg_temp.j(g, '40', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''still there?'')', pg_temp.key('stale3'), v_fg)) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'closed'
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_messages WHERE match_id IN (v_ab, v_ce, v_fg)) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a blocked or unmatched pair sent through a stale client (mutant 12)';
  END IF;
  -- Fixed answers: an outsider's and a missing match's block, and a
  -- second unmatch, never raise.
  IF pg_temp.j(c, '37', format('SELECT public.block_dating_match(%L, %L)', pg_temp.key('ob'), v_fg)) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'match_unavailable'
     OR pg_temp.j(c, '37', format('SELECT public.block_dating_match(%L, %L)', pg_temp.key('nb'), gen_random_uuid())) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'match_unavailable'
     OR pg_temp.j(c, '37', format('SELECT public.unmatch_dating_match(%L, %L)', pg_temp.key('again'), v_ce)) IS DISTINCT FROM '{"ok": true}'::jsonb
     OR pg_temp.j(c, '37', 'SELECT public.block_dating_match(''short'', NULL)') ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'invalid' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: block or unmatch raised or answered loosely';
  END IF;
  -- No API role inserts; with INSERT granted (only here) the policy refuses.
  v_err := pg_temp.call(g, pg_temp.dev('40'), format(
    'WITH x AS (INSERT INTO public.dating_messages (match_id, sender_side, sender_user_id, body, body_masked, mask_version, idempotency_key)
     VALUES (%L, %L, %L, ''x'', ''x'', 1, ''k-direct'') RETURNING 1) SELECT count(*)::text FROM x', v_fg, CASE WHEN g < f THEN 'low' ELSE 'high' END, g));
  IF v_err NOT LIKE '42501:permission denied%' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a signed-in person could insert a dating message directly: %', v_err;
  END IF;
  GRANT INSERT ON public.dating_messages TO authenticated;
  v_err := pg_temp.call(g, pg_temp.dev('40'), format(
    'WITH x AS (INSERT INTO public.dating_messages (match_id, sender_side, sender_user_id, body, body_masked, mask_version, idempotency_key)
     VALUES (%L, %L, %L, ''x'', ''x'', 1, ''k-direct'') RETURNING 1) SELECT count(*)::text FROM x', v_fg, CASE WHEN g < f THEN 'low' ELSE 'high' END, g));
  REVOKE INSERT ON public.dating_messages FROM authenticated;
  IF v_err NOT LIKE '42501:new row violates row-level security policy%' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the INSERT policy let a blocked pair write: %', v_err;
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- I. (§3.6, §6.1; mutants 10, 11) The mask holds until both swap: one
--    side's offer unmasks nothing; withdrawn, still nothing; both: the
--    words as written, for old and new messages; time alone never lifts
--    it; swap refused before 72 hours (with the time it opens); after
--    both, it can't be withdrawn; an ended chat can't swap.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  a constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('41');
  b constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('42', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"]}');
  v_m uuid;
  v jsonb;
  v_text text;
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (user_low_id, user_high_id, state, matched_at)
  VALUES (least(a, b), greatest(a, b), 'active', now() - interval '71 hours') RETURNING id INTO v_m;
  PERFORM pg_temp.j(a, '41', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''my snapchat is amab'')', pg_temp.key('s1'), v_m));
  v := pg_temp.j(a, '41', format('SELECT public.offer_dating_contact_swap(%L)', v_m));
  IF v ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'too_early'
     OR (v ->> 'from')::timestamptz IS DISTINCT FROM (SELECT matched_at + interval '72 hours' FROM public.dating_matches WHERE id = v_m) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: swap was not refused before 72 hours, with the time it opens (mutant 11): %', v;
  END IF;
  UPDATE public.dating_matches SET matched_at = now() - interval '72 hours' WHERE id = v_m;
  v := pg_temp.j(a, '41', format('SELECT public.offer_dating_contact_swap(%L)', v_m));
  v_text := pg_temp.j(b, '42', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_m)) #>> '{messages,0,text}';
  IF v IS DISTINCT FROM '{"ok": true, "swap": {"both": false, "mine": true, "theirs": false}}'::jsonb OR v_text IS DISTINCT FROM '•••' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: one side''s offer unmasked the chat (mutant 10): % / %', v, v_text;
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.j(b, '42', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_m)) #> '{chat,swap}'
     IS DISTINCT FROM jsonb_build_object('mine', false, 'theirs', true, 'both', false,
                                         'from', (SELECT matched_at + interval '72 hours' FROM public.dating_matches WHERE id = v_m)) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the other side does not see the offer';
  END IF;
  v := pg_temp.j(a, '41', format('SELECT public.withdraw_dating_contact_swap(%L)', v_m));
  PERFORM pg_temp.j(b, '42', format('SELECT public.offer_dating_contact_swap(%L)', v_m));
  IF v IS DISTINCT FROM '{"ok": true, "swap": {"both": false, "mine": false, "theirs": false}}'::jsonb
     OR pg_temp.j(a, '41', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_m)) #>> '{messages,0,text}' IS DISTINCT FROM '•••' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a withdrawn offer, then the other''s, unmasked the chat: %', v;
  END IF;
  -- Time alone never lifts it.
  UPDATE public.dating_matches SET matched_at = now() - interval '400 days' WHERE id = v_m;
  IF pg_temp.j(a, '41', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_m)) #>> '{messages,0,text}' IS DISTINCT FROM '•••' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the mask lifted by itself';
  END IF;
  PERFORM pg_temp.j(a, '41', format('SELECT public.offer_dating_contact_swap(%L)', v_m));
  PERFORM pg_temp.j(b, '42', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''mine is 0244 123 456'')', pg_temp.key('s2'), v_m));
  v := pg_temp.j(a, '41', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_m));
  IF (SELECT array_agg(x ->> 'text' ORDER BY x ->> 'at') FROM jsonb_array_elements(v -> 'messages') AS x)
     IS DISTINCT FROM ARRAY['my snapchat is amab', 'mine is 0244 123 456']
     OR v #>> '{chat,swap,both}' IS DISTINCT FROM 'true' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: after both swapped the words are not as written: %', v;
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.j(a, '41', format('SELECT public.withdraw_dating_contact_swap(%L)', v_m)) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'already_swapped' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a swap both made could be undone';
  END IF;
  UPDATE public.dating_matches SET state = 'unmatched', closed_reason = 'unmatched', evidence_until = now() + interval '30 days' WHERE id = v_m;
  IF pg_temp.j(a, '41', format('SELECT public.offer_dating_contact_swap(%L)', v_m)) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'closed' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an ended chat could swap';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- J. (§3.5, §3.6) The reads: messages newest first with a page bound and
--    keyset paging; an ended chat is read-only for its 30-day window,
--    then gone; the conversations list: newest activity first, the last
--    message as the reader reads it, a dot (never a count) until read,
--    the ended section on request; another phone reads nothing; an
--    outsider reads nothing (one answer with a missing match).
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  a constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('43');
  b constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('44', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"], "name": "Kofi"}');
  c constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('45', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"], "name": "Yaw"}');
  o constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('46');
  v_ab uuid;
  v_ac uuid;
  v jsonb;
  v_page jsonb;
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (user_low_id, user_high_id, state, matched_at)
  VALUES (least(a, b), greatest(a, b), 'active', now() - interval '2 days') RETURNING id INTO v_ab;
  INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (user_low_id, user_high_id, state, matched_at)
  VALUES (least(a, c), greatest(a, c), 'active', now() - interval '1 day') RETURNING id INTO v_ac;
  FOR i IN 1..3 LOOP
    PERFORM pg_temp.j(b, '44', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, %L)', pg_temp.key('m' || i), v_ab, 'message ' || i));
  END LOOP;
  v := pg_temp.j(a, '43', 'SELECT public.get_my_dating_matches()');
  IF (SELECT array_agg(x ->> 'display_name' || ':' || (x ->> 'new') || ':' || coalesce(x #>> '{last,text}', '-') ORDER BY ord)
        FROM jsonb_array_elements(v -> 'conversations') WITH ORDINALITY AS t(x, ord))
     IS DISTINCT FROM ARRAY['Kofi:true:message 3', 'Yaw:false:-'] OR v ? 'ended' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the conversations list is %', v;
  END IF;
  v_page := pg_temp.j(a, '43', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L, NULL, 2)', v_ab));
  IF (SELECT array_agg(x ->> 'text' ORDER BY ord) FROM jsonb_array_elements(v_page -> 'messages') WITH ORDINALITY AS t(x, ord))
     IS DISTINCT FROM ARRAY['message 3', 'message 2'] OR v_page ->> 'more' IS DISTINCT FROM 'true'
     OR v_page #>> '{chat,display_name}' IS DISTINCT FROM 'Kofi' OR v_page #>> '{chat,state}' IS DISTINCT FROM 'active'
     OR (v_page #>> '{messages,0,mine}')::boolean THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the newest page is %', v_page;
  END IF;
  v := pg_temp.j(a, '43', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L, %L, 2)', v_ab, v_page #>> '{messages,1,at}'));
  IF (SELECT array_agg(x ->> 'text') FROM jsonb_array_elements(v -> 'messages') AS x) IS DISTINCT FROM ARRAY['message 1']
     OR v ->> 'more' IS DISTINCT FROM 'false' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the next page is %', v;
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.j(a, '43', 'SELECT public.get_my_dating_matches()') #>> '{conversations,0,new}' IS DISTINCT FROM 'false' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: reading the chat did not clear the dot';
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.call(a, pg_temp.dev('99'), 'SELECT public.get_my_dating_matches()')::jsonb IS DISTINCT FROM '{"ok": false, "reason": "reverify_device"}'::jsonb
     OR pg_temp.call(a, pg_temp.dev('99'), format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_ab))::jsonb ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'reverify_device'
     OR pg_temp.j(o, '46', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_ab))
        IS DISTINCT FROM pg_temp.j(o, '46', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', gen_random_uuid()))
     OR pg_temp.j(o, '46', 'SELECT public.get_my_dating_matches(true)') IS DISTINCT FROM '{"ok": true, "ended": [], "conversations": []}'::jsonb
     OR pg_temp.j(a, '43', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L, NULL, 101)', v_ab)) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'invalid' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: another phone or an outsider read the chat, or a page over 100 was taken';
  END IF;
  -- Ended: read-only for its window, listed under ended, then gone.
  PERFORM pg_temp.j(a, '43', format('SELECT public.unmatch_dating_match(%L, %L)', pg_temp.key('u'), v_ab));
  v := pg_temp.j(a, '43', 'SELECT public.get_my_dating_matches(true)');
  IF jsonb_array_length(v -> 'conversations') <> 1 OR v #>> '{ended,0,display_name}' IS DISTINCT FROM 'Kofi'
     OR pg_temp.j(b, '44', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_ab)) #>> '{chat,state}' IS DISTINCT FROM 'ended' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an ended chat is not listed as ended, or not readable as ended: %', v;
  END IF;
  UPDATE public.dating_matches SET evidence_until = now() - interval '1 minute' WHERE id = v_ab;
  IF pg_temp.j(b, '44', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_ab)) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'match_unavailable'
     OR jsonb_array_length(pg_temp.j(a, '43', 'SELECT public.get_my_dating_matches(true)') -> 'ended') <> 0 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an ended chat is still readable after its evidence window';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- K. (§2.3; W5 final review I-1) The chat's reads refresh the phone's
--    last use as PostgREST calls them (a STABLE RPC runs READ ONLY there),
--    so each is VOLATILE.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  u constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('47');
  v_fn text;
  v_ro boolean;
  v_seen timestamptz;
  v_bad text[] := '{}';
BEGIN
  FOREACH v_fn IN ARRAY ARRAY['public.get_my_dating_matches(boolean)', 'public.get_dating_messages(uuid,timestamp with time zone,integer)'] LOOP
    UPDATE public.dating_trusted_devices SET last_seen_at = now() - interval '2 days' WHERE user_id = u;
    SELECT p.provolatile <> 'v' INTO v_ro FROM pg_catalog.pg_proc AS p WHERE p.oid = v_fn::regprocedure;
    v_seen := NULL;
    BEGIN
      IF v_ro THEN
        PERFORM set_config('transaction_read_only', 'on', true);
      END IF;
      PERFORM pg_temp.call(u, pg_temp.dev('47'), CASE WHEN v_fn LIKE '%messages(uuid%' THEN 'SELECT public.get_dating_messages(gen_random_uuid())::text'
                                                       ELSE 'SELECT public.get_my_dating_matches()::text' END);
      SELECT d.last_seen_at INTO v_seen FROM public.dating_trusted_devices AS d WHERE d.user_id = u;
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'w6:postgrest_undo';
    EXCEPTION WHEN raise_exception THEN
      IF SQLERRM IS DISTINCT FROM 'w6:postgrest_undo' THEN
        RAISE;
      END IF;
    END;
    IF v_seen IS DISTINCT FROM now() THEN
      v_bad := v_bad || (v_fn || CASE WHEN v_ro THEN ' (runs READ ONLY)' ELSE '' END);
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
  IF cardinality(v_bad) > 0 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a chat read, called as PostgREST calls it, never refreshes the phone''s last use: %', v_bad;
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- L. (§5.3, §5.10; carry 6) Open chats count a match under 7 days old or
--    one with a message in the last 14 days; the dragnet's first-message
--    input counts the matches the person wrote in.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  a constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('48');
  b constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('49', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"]}');
  c constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('50', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"]}');
  v_ab uuid;
  v_ac uuid;
  v_i uuid;
BEGIN
  v_i := pg_temp.intro(a, b);
  INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (introduction_id, user_low_id, user_high_id, state, matched_at)
  VALUES (v_i, least(a, b), greatest(a, b), 'active', now() - interval '20 days') RETURNING id INTO v_ab;
  v_i := pg_temp.intro(a, c);
  INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (introduction_id, user_low_id, user_high_id, state, matched_at)
  VALUES (v_i, least(a, c), greatest(a, c), 'active', now() - interval '20 days') RETURNING id INTO v_ac;
  IF private.dating_open_chats(a, now()) <> 0 OR private.dating_first_messages(a, now() - interval '1 day') <> 0 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: two quiet 20-day-old matches count as open chats or as written in';
  END IF;
  PERFORM pg_temp.j(a, '48', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''hello'')', pg_temp.key('l1'), v_ab));
  PERFORM pg_temp.j(c, '50', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''hello'')', pg_temp.key('l2'), v_ac));
  IF private.dating_open_chats(a, now()) <> 2 OR private.dating_first_messages(a, now() - interval '1 day') <> 1
     OR private.dating_first_messages(c, now() - interval '1 day') <> 1 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: open chats (%) or first messages (%) don''t read the messages',
      private.dating_open_chats(a, now()), private.dating_first_messages(a, now() - interval '1 day');
  END IF;
  UPDATE public.dating_messages SET created_at = now() - interval '15 days' WHERE match_id IN (v_ab, v_ac);
  IF private.dating_open_chats(a, now()) <> 0 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a chat quiet for 15 days still counts as open';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- @@W6B@@
```
