- [ ] **Step 3: Write the chat**

Create `$D/mig_T2_chat.sql` with exactly (section 4: the chat's tables, policy, send, reads, swap, Block and Unmatch; the builder puts it before the re-created functions):

```sql
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4. The dating chat (§3.6, §4.3, §4.4, §4.5, §6.1). Its own table, never
--    public.messages (no relationship, no couples trigger, no counter, no
--    couples safety outbox). Text only, 1-1,000 characters. Each message
--    is masked at insert (body_masked, mask_version); a reader gets the
--    masked text until both have swapped (low_swap_at and high_swap_at),
--    then the text as written; nothing lifts the mask by itself. No read
--    receipts, typing or presence: the per-side seen marks below only
--    decide the conversations list's dot for the reader, never what the
--    other person sees.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
ALTER TABLE public.dating_matches
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS chat_started_at timestamptz,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS low_swap_at timestamptz,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS high_swap_at timestamptz,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS low_seen_at timestamptz,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS high_seen_at timestamptz;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.dating_messages (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  match_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.dating_matches (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  sender_side text NOT NULL CONSTRAINT dating_messages_side_check CHECK (sender_side IN ('low', 'high')),
  sender_user_id uuid REFERENCES public.users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  kind text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'text' CONSTRAINT dating_messages_kind_check CHECK (kind IN ('text', 'guided_answer', 'close_kindly')),
  question_id text CONSTRAINT dating_messages_question_check CHECK (question_id IS NULL OR question_id ~ '^[a-z0-9_]{1,40}$'),
  body text NOT NULL CONSTRAINT dating_messages_body_check CHECK (char_length(body) BETWEEN 1 AND 1000),
  body_masked text NOT NULL,
  mask_version smallint NOT NULL,
  idempotency_key text NOT NULL,
  -- clock time, so messages sent in one transaction keep their order
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT clock_timestamp(),
  CONSTRAINT dating_messages_key_unique UNIQUE (match_id, sender_side, idempotency_key)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS dating_messages_match_idx ON public.dating_messages (match_id, created_at DESC, id DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS dating_messages_sender_idx ON public.dating_messages (sender_user_id, created_at DESC);
ALTER TABLE public.dating_messages ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- A recipient's "something changed" bump, read over realtime: the person's
-- own rows only, no content (§4.3).
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.dating_chat_signals (
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.users (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  match_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.dating_matches (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  bumped_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id, match_id)
);
ALTER TABLE public.dating_chat_signals ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS dating_chat_signals_own ON public.dating_chat_signals;
CREATE POLICY dating_chat_signals_own ON public.dating_chat_signals
  FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (user_id = (SELECT auth.uid()));
DO $$
BEGIN
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_catalog.pg_publication WHERE pubname = 'supabase_realtime')
     AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_catalog.pg_publication_tables
                      WHERE pubname = 'supabase_realtime' AND schemaname = 'public' AND tablename = 'dating_chat_signals') THEN
    ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE public.dating_chat_signals;
  END IF;
END $$;

-- Bumps [p_user]'s signal for [p_match] (never raises; nobody to bump is
-- nothing).
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_bump_chat(p_user uuid, p_match uuid)
RETURNS void
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  INSERT INTO public.dating_chat_signals (user_id, match_id, bumped_at)
  SELECT p_user, p_match, now() WHERE p_user IS NOT NULL AND p_match IS NOT NULL
  ON CONFLICT (user_id, match_id) DO UPDATE SET bumped_at = now();
$$;

-- §4.4 chat spec §4.4: may the caller send in [p_match_id] now? A member
-- of an active match with both members, no block either way (by account
-- or by phone HMAC), and no live restriction or hold. Used by
-- send_dating_message and by the INSERT policy below; false for anyone
-- else's match or none (one answer).
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.dating_can_send(p_match_id uuid)
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT coalesce((
    SELECT m.state = 'active' AND m.user_low_id IS NOT NULL AND m.user_high_id IS NOT NULL
       AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_blocks AS b
                        WHERE (b.blocker_user_id = m.user_low_id AND b.blocked_user_id = m.user_high_id)
                           OR (b.blocker_user_id = m.user_high_id AND b.blocked_user_id = m.user_low_id))
       AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_block_hmacs AS h,
                              (SELECT public.dating_phone_hmac_for_user(m.user_low_id) AS lo,
                                      public.dating_phone_hmac_for_user(m.user_high_id) AS hi) AS x
                        WHERE (h.blocker_hmac = x.lo AND h.blocked_hmac = x.hi) OR (h.blocker_hmac = x.hi AND h.blocked_hmac = x.lo))
       AND public.dating_account_in_good_standing(auth.uid())
      FROM public.dating_matches AS m
     WHERE m.id = p_match_id AND auth.uid() IN (m.user_low_id, m.user_high_id)), false);
$$;

-- Defence in depth (§4.5): no API role may insert (the grant is revoked),
-- and were one ever granted, a row must still be the caller's own, in a
-- match they may send in.
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS dating_messages_insert_member ON public.dating_messages;
CREATE POLICY dating_messages_insert_member ON public.dating_messages
  FOR INSERT TO authenticated
  WITH CHECK (sender_user_id = (SELECT auth.uid()) AND public.dating_can_send(match_id));

-- One message as [p_side] reads it: the masked text until both swapped.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_message_json(p_m public.dating_messages, p_side text, p_both boolean)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE sql
IMMUTABLE
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('id', p_m.id, 'mine', p_m.sender_side = p_side, 'kind', p_m.kind,
           'question_id', p_m.question_id, 'text', CASE WHEN p_both THEN p_m.body ELSE p_m.body_masked END,
           'at', p_m.created_at);
$$;

-- The idempotency key rule of act v2 and block (16-128, [A-Za-z0-9_:.-]).
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_key_ok(p_key text)
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql
IMMUTABLE
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT p_key IS NOT NULL AND pg_catalog.char_length(p_key) BETWEEN 16 AND 128 AND p_key ~ '^[A-Za-z0-9_:.-]+$';
$$;

-- §3.6 send. Fixed answers, never a raise but the unsigned caller:
-- {ok, message} | {ok:false, reason: invalid | reverify_device |
-- match_unavailable | closed | restricted | rate_limited | busy}. A replay
-- (the same key in the same match) answers the first message. The match
-- row is locked first (FOR NO KEY UPDATE), so a block, an unmatch or a
-- leave that commits first is seen (the stale-client rule) and a send
-- that commits first is never undone by one. W6a sends text; W6c adds
-- the guided answers and Close kindly.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.send_dating_message(p_idempotency_key text, p_match_id uuid, p_body text,
                                                      p_kind text DEFAULT 'text', p_question_id text DEFAULT NULL)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
SET lock_timeout = '2s'
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_user constant uuid := auth.uid();
  v_m public.dating_matches;
  v_side text;
  v_other uuid;
  v_msg public.dating_messages;
  v_masked text;
  v_try integer;
BEGIN
  IF v_user IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'not_authenticated' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;
  IF p_match_id IS NULL OR NOT private.dating_key_ok(p_idempotency_key)
     OR p_kind IS DISTINCT FROM 'text' OR p_question_id IS NOT NULL
     OR p_body IS NULL OR pg_catalog.btrim(p_body) = '' OR pg_catalog.char_length(p_body) > 1000 THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'invalid');
  END IF;
  IF public.dating_require_trusted_device() IS NOT TRUE THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'reverify_device');
  END IF;
  FOR v_try IN 1..3 LOOP
    BEGIN
      SELECT * INTO v_m FROM public.dating_matches AS m
       WHERE m.id = p_match_id AND v_user IN (m.user_low_id, m.user_high_id)
       FOR NO KEY UPDATE;
      IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'match_unavailable');
      END IF;
      v_side := CASE WHEN v_m.user_low_id = v_user THEN 'low' ELSE 'high' END;
      v_other := CASE WHEN v_side = 'low' THEN v_m.user_high_id ELSE v_m.user_low_id END;
      -- A replay answers as the first did, whatever happened since.
      SELECT * INTO v_msg FROM public.dating_messages AS d
       WHERE d.match_id = p_match_id AND d.sender_side = v_side AND d.idempotency_key = p_idempotency_key;
      IF FOUND THEN
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'message',
          private.dating_message_json(v_msg, v_side, v_m.low_swap_at IS NOT NULL AND v_m.high_swap_at IS NOT NULL));
      END IF;
      IF v_m.state <> 'active' OR v_other IS NULL OR public.dating_can_send(p_match_id) IS NOT TRUE THEN
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason',
          CASE WHEN v_m.state = 'active' AND v_other IS NOT NULL AND public.dating_account_in_good_standing(v_user) IS NOT TRUE
               THEN 'restricted' ELSE 'closed' END);
      END IF;
      BEGIN
        PERFORM public.check_dating_rate_limit('dating_message', 500, interval '1 day');
      EXCEPTION WHEN invalid_parameter_value THEN
        IF SQLERRM IS DISTINCT FROM 'rate_limited' THEN
          RAISE;
        END IF;
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'rate_limited');
      END;
      v_masked := private.dating_mask_contacts_safe(p_body);
      INSERT INTO public.dating_messages (match_id, sender_side, sender_user_id, kind, question_id, body, body_masked,
                                          mask_version, idempotency_key)
      VALUES (p_match_id, v_side, v_user, 'text', NULL, p_body, v_masked, 1, p_idempotency_key)
      RETURNING * INTO v_msg;
      UPDATE public.dating_matches AS m
         SET chat_started_at = coalesce(m.chat_started_at, now()),
             low_seen_at = CASE WHEN v_side = 'low' THEN clock_timestamp() ELSE m.low_seen_at END,
             high_seen_at = CASE WHEN v_side = 'high' THEN clock_timestamp() ELSE m.high_seen_at END
       WHERE m.id = p_match_id;
      PERFORM private.dating_bump_chat(v_other, p_match_id);
      IF v_masked IS DISTINCT FROM p_body THEN
        PERFORM private.bump_metric_internal('dating.mask_hit');
      END IF;
      RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'message',
        private.dating_message_json(v_msg, v_side, v_m.low_swap_at IS NOT NULL AND v_m.high_swap_at IS NOT NULL));
    EXCEPTION WHEN deadlock_detected OR lock_not_available OR serialization_failure THEN
      IF v_try = 3 THEN
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'busy');
      END IF;
    END;
  END LOOP;
  RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'busy');
END;
$$;

-- The chat's header as [p_side] sees it.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_chat_header(p_m public.dating_matches, p_side text)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_other constant uuid := CASE WHEN p_side = 'low' THEN p_m.user_high_id ELSE p_m.user_low_id END;
  v_mine constant timestamptz := CASE WHEN p_side = 'low' THEN p_m.low_swap_at ELSE p_m.high_swap_at END;
  v_theirs constant timestamptz := CASE WHEN p_side = 'low' THEN p_m.high_swap_at ELSE p_m.low_swap_at END;
BEGIN
  RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
    'match_id', p_m.id,
    'display_name', (SELECT p.display_name FROM public.dating_profiles AS p WHERE p.user_id = v_other),
    'age', CASE WHEN v_other IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE public.account_age(v_other) END,
    'state', CASE WHEN p_m.state = 'active' AND v_other IS NOT NULL THEN 'active' ELSE 'ended' END,
    'swap', pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('mine', v_mine IS NOT NULL, 'theirs', v_theirs IS NOT NULL,
                                          'both', v_mine IS NOT NULL AND v_theirs IS NOT NULL,
                                          'from', p_m.matched_at + interval '72 hours'));
END;
$$;

-- §3.6 read: the newest [p_limit] (1-100, default 50) messages before
-- [p_before] (none: the newest), newest first, and the header; the
-- caller's own matches only, an ended one for its 30-day evidence window
-- (read-only). Reading the newest page marks the conversation seen for
-- the list's dot. VOLATILE: it writes the mark and the phone's last use,
-- and PostgREST runs a STABLE RPC read-only (W5 final I-1).
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_dating_messages(p_match_id uuid, p_before timestamptz DEFAULT NULL, p_limit integer DEFAULT 50)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
VOLATILE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_user constant uuid := auth.uid();
  v_m public.dating_matches;
  v_side text;
  v_both boolean;
  v_limit integer;
  v_rows jsonb;
  v_more boolean;
BEGIN
  IF v_user IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'not_authenticated' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;
  IF p_match_id IS NULL OR (p_limit IS NOT NULL AND p_limit NOT BETWEEN 1 AND 100) THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'invalid');
  END IF;
  IF public.dating_require_trusted_device() IS NOT TRUE THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'reverify_device');
  END IF;
  v_limit := coalesce(p_limit, 50);
  SELECT * INTO v_m FROM public.dating_matches AS m
   WHERE m.id = p_match_id AND v_user IN (m.user_low_id, m.user_high_id)
     AND (m.state = 'active' OR m.evidence_until > now());
  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'match_unavailable');
  END IF;
  v_side := CASE WHEN v_m.user_low_id = v_user THEN 'low' ELSE 'high' END;
  v_both := v_m.low_swap_at IS NOT NULL AND v_m.high_swap_at IS NOT NULL;
  SELECT coalesce(pg_catalog.jsonb_agg(private.dating_message_json(x, v_side, v_both) ORDER BY x.created_at DESC, x.id DESC), '[]'::jsonb),
         count(*) > v_limit
    INTO v_rows, v_more
    FROM (SELECT d.* FROM public.dating_messages AS d
           WHERE d.match_id = p_match_id AND (p_before IS NULL OR d.created_at < p_before)
           ORDER BY d.created_at DESC, d.id DESC
           LIMIT v_limit + 1) AS x;
  IF v_more THEN
    v_rows := v_rows - v_limit;
  END IF;
  IF p_before IS NULL THEN
    UPDATE public.dating_matches AS m
       SET low_seen_at = CASE WHEN v_side = 'low' THEN clock_timestamp() ELSE m.low_seen_at END,
           high_seen_at = CASE WHEN v_side = 'high' THEN clock_timestamp() ELSE m.high_seen_at END
     WHERE m.id = p_match_id
       AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_messages AS d
                    WHERE d.match_id = m.id AND d.sender_side <> v_side
                      AND d.created_at > coalesce(CASE WHEN v_side = 'low' THEN m.low_seen_at ELSE m.high_seen_at END, '-infinity'));
  END IF;
  RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'chat', private.dating_chat_header(v_m, v_side),
                                       'messages', v_rows, 'more', v_more);
END;
$$;

-- §3.5 the conversations list: the caller's active matches (newest
-- activity first) with the other person's first name and age, the last
-- message as the caller reads it, and a dot (never a count) for
-- something new; with [p_include_ended], the matches that ended in the
-- last 30 days (their evidence window). VOLATILE (the phone's last use).
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_my_dating_matches(p_include_ended boolean DEFAULT false)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
VOLATILE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_user constant uuid := auth.uid();
  v_rows jsonb;
  v_ended jsonb;
BEGIN
  IF v_user IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'not_authenticated' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;
  IF public.dating_require_trusted_device() IS NOT TRUE THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'reverify_device');
  END IF;
  SELECT coalesce(pg_catalog.jsonb_agg(c.row ORDER BY c.at DESC, c.id), '[]'::jsonb) INTO v_rows
    FROM (SELECT m.id, coalesce(l.created_at, m.matched_at) AS at,
                 pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
                   'match_id', m.id,
                   'display_name', (SELECT p.display_name FROM public.dating_profiles AS p WHERE p.user_id = s.other),
                   'age', public.account_age(s.other),
                   'last', CASE WHEN l.id IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE
                             private.dating_message_json(l, s.side, m.low_swap_at IS NOT NULL AND m.high_swap_at IS NOT NULL) END,
                   'new', EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_messages AS d
                                   WHERE d.match_id = m.id AND d.sender_side <> s.side
                                     AND d.created_at > coalesce(s.seen, '-infinity'))) AS row
            FROM public.dating_matches AS m
           CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT CASE WHEN m.user_low_id = v_user THEN 'low' ELSE 'high' END AS side,
                                      CASE WHEN m.user_low_id = v_user THEN m.user_high_id ELSE m.user_low_id END AS other,
                                      CASE WHEN m.user_low_id = v_user THEN m.low_seen_at ELSE m.high_seen_at END AS seen) AS s
            LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT d.* FROM public.dating_messages AS d WHERE d.match_id = m.id
                                ORDER BY d.created_at DESC, d.id DESC LIMIT 1) AS l ON true
           WHERE v_user IN (m.user_low_id, m.user_high_id) AND m.state = 'active' AND s.other IS NOT NULL) AS c;
  IF coalesce(p_include_ended, false) THEN
    SELECT coalesce(pg_catalog.jsonb_agg(pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
             'match_id', m.id,
             'display_name', (SELECT p.display_name FROM public.dating_profiles AS p
                               WHERE p.user_id = CASE WHEN m.user_low_id = v_user THEN m.user_high_id ELSE m.user_low_id END),
             'ended_at', coalesce(m.closed_at, m.matched_at)) ORDER BY coalesce(m.closed_at, m.matched_at) DESC, m.id), '[]'::jsonb)
      INTO v_ended
      FROM public.dating_matches AS m
     WHERE v_user IN (m.user_low_id, m.user_high_id)
       AND (m.state <> 'active' OR m.user_low_id IS NULL OR m.user_high_id IS NULL)
       AND m.evidence_until > now();
  END IF;
  RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'conversations', v_rows)
      || CASE WHEN coalesce(p_include_ended, false) THEN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ended', v_ended) ELSE '{}'::jsonb END;
END;
$$;

-- §3.6, §6.1 swap. Offered from 72 hours after the match; either may
-- withdraw an offer until both have swapped; after both, it can't be
-- undone. Both answer {ok, swap:{mine, theirs, both}} | {ok:false,
-- reason: invalid | reverify_device | match_unavailable | closed |
-- too_early (with from) | already_swapped | busy}.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_swap_write(p_match_id uuid, p_offer boolean)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
SET lock_timeout = '2s'
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_user constant uuid := auth.uid();
  v_m public.dating_matches;
  v_side text;
  v_mine timestamptz;
  v_theirs timestamptz;
  v_try integer;
BEGIN
  IF v_user IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'not_authenticated' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;
  IF p_match_id IS NULL THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'invalid');
  END IF;
  IF public.dating_require_trusted_device() IS NOT TRUE THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'reverify_device');
  END IF;
  FOR v_try IN 1..3 LOOP
    BEGIN
      SELECT * INTO v_m FROM public.dating_matches AS m
       WHERE m.id = p_match_id AND v_user IN (m.user_low_id, m.user_high_id)
       FOR NO KEY UPDATE;
      IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'match_unavailable');
      END IF;
      v_side := CASE WHEN v_m.user_low_id = v_user THEN 'low' ELSE 'high' END;
      v_mine := CASE WHEN v_side = 'low' THEN v_m.low_swap_at ELSE v_m.high_swap_at END;
      v_theirs := CASE WHEN v_side = 'low' THEN v_m.high_swap_at ELSE v_m.low_swap_at END;
      IF v_m.state <> 'active' OR v_m.user_low_id IS NULL OR v_m.user_high_id IS NULL THEN
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'closed');
      END IF;
      IF p_offer AND now() < v_m.matched_at + interval '72 hours' THEN
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'too_early', 'from', v_m.matched_at + interval '72 hours');
      END IF;
      IF NOT p_offer AND v_mine IS NOT NULL AND v_theirs IS NOT NULL THEN
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'already_swapped');
      END IF;
      v_mine := CASE WHEN p_offer THEN coalesce(v_mine, now()) ELSE NULL END;
      UPDATE public.dating_matches AS m
         SET low_swap_at = CASE WHEN v_side = 'low' THEN v_mine ELSE m.low_swap_at END,
             high_swap_at = CASE WHEN v_side = 'high' THEN v_mine ELSE m.high_swap_at END
       WHERE m.id = p_match_id;
      PERFORM private.dating_bump_chat(CASE WHEN v_side = 'low' THEN v_m.user_high_id ELSE v_m.user_low_id END, p_match_id);
      IF p_offer AND v_theirs IS NOT NULL
         AND (CASE WHEN v_side = 'low' THEN v_m.low_swap_at ELSE v_m.high_swap_at END) IS NULL THEN
        PERFORM private.bump_metric_internal('dating.swap');
      END IF;
      RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'swap', pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
        'mine', v_mine IS NOT NULL, 'theirs', v_theirs IS NOT NULL, 'both', v_mine IS NOT NULL AND v_theirs IS NOT NULL));
    EXCEPTION WHEN deadlock_detected OR lock_not_available OR serialization_failure THEN
      IF v_try = 3 THEN
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'busy');
      END IF;
    END;
  END LOOP;
  RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'busy');
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.offer_dating_contact_swap(p_match_id uuid)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT private.dating_swap_write(p_match_id, true);
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.withdraw_dating_contact_swap(p_match_id uuid)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT private.dating_swap_write(p_match_id, false);
$$;

-- §3.6 Block and Unmatch from the chat (carry 4: fixed answers, never a
-- raise but the unsigned caller; the lock order of exit and of the card's
-- Block: the setup lock, then the match row, then its introduction's).
-- Whole replacements: both returned void and raised before. Neither is
-- gated by the phone or by eligibility (they reduce exposure; Ruling
-- R-36); a member of the match only. {ok} | {ok:false, reason: invalid |
-- match_unavailable | rate_limited | busy}.
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.block_dating_match(text, uuid);
CREATE FUNCTION public.block_dating_match(p_idempotency_key text, p_match_id uuid)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
SET lock_timeout = '2s'
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_user constant uuid := auth.uid();
  v_m public.dating_matches;
  v_other uuid;
  v_try integer;
BEGIN
  IF v_user IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'not_authenticated' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;
  IF p_match_id IS NULL OR NOT private.dating_key_ok(p_idempotency_key) THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'invalid');
  END IF;
  FOR v_try IN 1..3 LOOP
    BEGIN
      PERFORM private.dating_lock_setup(v_user);
      IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_rpc_idempotency AS k
                  WHERE k.user_id = v_user AND k.operation = 'block_dating_match' AND k.idempotency_key = p_idempotency_key) THEN
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
      END IF;
      SELECT * INTO v_m FROM public.dating_matches AS m
       WHERE m.id = p_match_id AND v_user IN (m.user_low_id, m.user_high_id)
       FOR UPDATE;
      v_other := CASE WHEN v_m.user_low_id = v_user THEN v_m.user_high_id ELSE v_m.user_low_id END;
      IF NOT FOUND OR v_other IS NULL THEN
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'match_unavailable');
      END IF;
      PERFORM 1 FROM public.dating_introductions AS i WHERE i.id = v_m.introduction_id FOR UPDATE;
      BEGIN
        PERFORM public.check_dating_rate_limit('block', 20, interval '1 day');
      EXCEPTION WHEN invalid_parameter_value THEN
        IF SQLERRM IS DISTINCT FROM 'rate_limited' THEN
          RAISE;
        END IF;
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'rate_limited');
      END;
      PERFORM public.claim_dating_idempotency('block_dating_match', p_idempotency_key);
      PERFORM public.block_dating_user(p_idempotency_key, v_other);
      PERFORM private.bump_metric_internal('dating.block');
      RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
    EXCEPTION WHEN deadlock_detected OR lock_not_available OR serialization_failure THEN
      IF v_try = 3 THEN
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'busy');
      END IF;
    END;
  END LOOP;
  RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'busy');
END;
$$;

DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.unmatch_dating_match(text, uuid);
CREATE FUNCTION public.unmatch_dating_match(p_idempotency_key text, p_match_id uuid)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
SET lock_timeout = '2s'
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_user constant uuid := auth.uid();
  v_m public.dating_matches;
  v_other uuid;
  v_try integer;
BEGIN
  IF v_user IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'not_authenticated' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;
  IF p_match_id IS NULL OR NOT private.dating_key_ok(p_idempotency_key) THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'invalid');
  END IF;
  FOR v_try IN 1..3 LOOP
    BEGIN
      PERFORM private.dating_lock_setup(v_user);
      IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_rpc_idempotency AS k
                  WHERE k.user_id = v_user AND k.operation = 'unmatch_dating_match' AND k.idempotency_key = p_idempotency_key) THEN
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
      END IF;
      SELECT * INTO v_m FROM public.dating_matches AS m
       WHERE m.id = p_match_id AND v_user IN (m.user_low_id, m.user_high_id)
       FOR UPDATE;
      IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'match_unavailable');
      END IF;
      v_other := CASE WHEN v_m.user_low_id = v_user THEN v_m.user_high_id ELSE v_m.user_low_id END;
      PERFORM 1 FROM public.dating_introductions AS i WHERE i.id = v_m.introduction_id FOR UPDATE;
      PERFORM public.claim_dating_idempotency('unmatch_dating_match', p_idempotency_key);
      -- Already ended (by either side or anything else): nothing more to do.
      IF v_m.state = 'active' THEN
        UPDATE public.dating_matches AS m
           SET state = 'unmatched', closed_reason = 'unmatched', closed_at = coalesce(m.closed_at, now()),
               evidence_until = now() + interval '30 days'
         WHERE m.id = p_match_id;
        -- §3.6: "You won't be introduced again."
        PERFORM private.dating_mark_pair(v_m.user_low_id, v_m.user_high_id);
        PERFORM private.dating_bump_chat(v_other, p_match_id);
        PERFORM private.bump_metric_internal('dating.unmatch');
      END IF;
      RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
    EXCEPTION WHEN deadlock_detected OR lock_not_available OR serialization_failure THEN
      IF v_try = 3 THEN
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'busy');
      END IF;
    END;
  END LOOP;
  RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'busy');
END;
$$;
```

Create `$D/edits_T2.py` with exactly (the builder's edits to the two bodies saved in Step 1):

```python
# Spec 2 W6a Task 2: build_mig_T1.py's extra edits (the chat's), on the
# live bodies saved to before_w6a/ (two more by Task 2 Step 1).
EDITS = {
  # §5.3 open chats: an active match under 7 days old, or one with a
  # message in the last 14 days (W6c adds "neither said We met").
  'private.dating_open_chats(uuid,timestamp with time zone)': [
    ("""     AND m.matched_at > p_at - interval '7 days';""",
     """     AND (m.matched_at > p_at - interval '7 days' -- Spec 2 W6a (§5.3): or a message in the last 14 days
          OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_messages AS d -- Spec 2 W6a
                      WHERE d.match_id = m.id AND d.created_at > p_at - interval '14 days')); -- Spec 2 W6a"""),
  ],
  # §5.10 the dragnet's third input (carry 6, Ruling R-14): of the matches
  # from the window's introductions, those the person wrote in.
  'private.dating_first_messages(uuid,timestamp with time zone)': [
    ("""  SELECT NULL::integer;""",
     """  SELECT count(*)::integer -- Spec 2 W6a (carry 6): the matches the person wrote in
    FROM public.dating_matches AS m JOIN public.dating_introductions AS i ON i.id = m.introduction_id -- Spec 2 W6a
   WHERE p_user IN (i.user_low_id, i.user_high_id) AND i.created_at > p_since -- Spec 2 W6a
     AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_messages AS d WHERE d.match_id = m.id AND d.sender_user_id = p_user); -- Spec 2 W6a"""),
  ],
  # §3.6: a block shows the other person's open chat that it ended (the
  # same "This conversation has ended." an unmatch shows; never that it
  # was a block).
  'public.block_dating_user(text,uuid)': [
    ("""  WHERE public.dating_pair_key(user_low_id, user_high_id) = v_pair_key
    AND state = 'active';""",
     """  WHERE public.dating_pair_key(user_low_id, user_high_id) = v_pair_key
    AND state = 'active';
  PERFORM private.dating_bump_chat(p_target_user_id, m.id) FROM public.dating_matches AS m WHERE m.pair_key = v_pair_key; -- Spec 2 W6a"""),

}
```

Create `supabase/migrations/20261120100010_dating_v2_chat_grants.sql` with exactly (Task 1's, with the chat's grants):

```sql
-- Spec 2 W6a: grants for 20261120100000_dating_v2_chat.sql. The masker,
-- the caption trigger and the pair marker are the database's own (no API
-- role's, the service role included: a marker is a keyed code of two
-- numbers, and the masker is presentation the server applies). The chat's
-- RPCs are the app's (authenticated only); its tables are no API role's
-- but the chat signals, which a signed-in person reads for their own rows
-- (realtime). dating_messages is revoked from the service role too (S-5;
-- W6b's worker reads bodies only through its claim function).
BEGIN;
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';
REVOKE ALL ON TABLE public.dating_pair_markers FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_mask_fold(text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_mask_leet(text, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_mask_digits(text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_mask_contacts(text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_mask_contacts_safe(text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_caption_invites(text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_photo_caption_mask() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_pair_code(text, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_mark_pair(uuid, uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_pair_new(uuid, uuid, text, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
-- Re-created (CREATE OR REPLACE keeps their grants; re-asserted).
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_card(uuid, uuid, public.dating_introductions) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_generate_partition(text, text, text, date, timestamptz, text, jsonb) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.act_on_dating_introduction(text, uuid, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.act_on_dating_introduction(text, uuid, text) TO authenticated;
-- The chat (Task 2).
REVOKE ALL ON TABLE public.dating_messages FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON TABLE public.dating_chat_signals FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
GRANT SELECT ON TABLE public.dating_chat_signals TO authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_bump_chat(uuid, uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_message_json(public.dating_messages, text, boolean) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_key_ok(text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_chat_header(public.dating_matches, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_swap_write(uuid, boolean) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_open_chats(uuid, timestamptz) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_first_messages(uuid, timestamptz) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
-- The app's: the chat RPCs; dating_can_send also backs the INSERT policy.
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.dating_can_send(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.dating_can_send(uuid) TO authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.send_dating_message(text, uuid, text, text, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.send_dating_message(text, uuid, text, text, text) TO authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.get_dating_messages(uuid, timestamptz, integer) FROM PUBLIC, anon, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.get_dating_messages(uuid, timestamptz, integer) TO authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.get_my_dating_matches(boolean) FROM PUBLIC, anon, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.get_my_dating_matches(boolean) TO authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.offer_dating_contact_swap(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.offer_dating_contact_swap(uuid) TO authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.withdraw_dating_contact_swap(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.withdraw_dating_contact_swap(uuid) TO authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.block_dating_match(text, uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.block_dating_match(text, uuid) TO authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.unmatch_dating_match(text, uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.unmatch_dating_match(text, uuid) TO authenticated;
COMMIT;
```

```python
edit('scripts/concurrency/dating_kill_switch_races.sh', [
  ("""#   block vs leaving on a matched pair (Spec 2 W5 final, M-4): the block
#     takes the match's row before the introduction's, exit's order, so it
#     waits on the leave instead of deadlocking with it; the leave
#     completes and the block finds the card gone.
""", """#   block vs leaving on a matched pair (Spec 2 W5 final, M-4): the block
#     takes the match's row before the introduction's, exit's order, so it
#     waits on the leave instead of deadlocking with it; the leave
#     completes and the block finds the card gone.
#   the chat's Block vs leaving (Spec 2 W6a, carry 4): the same order from
#     the chat (the setup lock, the match row, its introduction's); the
#     block waits on the leave and still lands.
#   a send vs a block (Spec 2 W6a, §4.4 stale client): the send waits on
#     the match row the block holds, then answers closed; no message is
#     stored after a block.
"""),
  ("""  DELETE FROM public.dating_rpc_idempotency WHERE user_id::text LIKE '5e2f%';
""", """  DELETE FROM public.dating_rpc_idempotency WHERE user_id::text LIKE '5e2f%';
  IF to_regclass('public.dating_pair_markers') IS NOT NULL THEN -- Spec 2 W6a: a scratch database's markers (keyed to the race phones)
    EXECUTE 'DELETE FROM public.dating_pair_markers';
  END IF;
"""),
  ("""$P -c "SELECT race.reset(); UPDATE public.feature_flags SET enabled = false WHERE key = 'dating_candidate_generation';" > /dev/null 2>&1
exit $fail""", """# Spec 2 W6a (carry 4): the chat's Block while the other leaves. B (42)
# leaves: exit closes the match (and sleeps in that update, holding the
# match's row). A (41) blocks from the chat: A's setup lock, then the
# match's row, so A waits on the leave, then blocks the person (the match
# already ended by the leave). No deadlock, the leave completes, the block
# answers ok and is kept.
FX_CHAT_BLOCK_EXIT="SELECT race.reset(); ALTER SEQUENCE race.s1 RESTART;
SELECT race.ready('41', 'woman', ARRAY['man']), race.ready('42', 'man', ARRAY['woman']);
INSERT INTO public.dating_introductions (pair_key, user_low_id, user_high_id, state, algorithm_version, visible_from_low, visible_from_high, low_action, high_action)
VALUES (public.dating_pair_key(race.id('41'), race.id('42')), race.id('41'), race.id('42'),
        'matched', 'introductions_v2', now() - interval '1 hour', now() - interval '1 hour', 'interested', 'interested');
INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (introduction_id, user_low_id, user_high_id, state, matched_at)
SELECT i.id, i.user_low_id, i.user_high_id, 'active', now() FROM public.dating_introductions AS i
 WHERE i.pair_key = public.dating_pair_key(race.id('41'), race.id('42'));
INSERT INTO race.outcome SELECT 'cb_match', m.id::text FROM public.dating_matches AS m
 WHERE m.pair_key = public.dating_pair_key(race.id('41'), race.id('42'));"
CB_MATCH="(SELECT result::uuid FROM race.outcome WHERE name = 'cb_match')"
run "the chat's Block while the other leaves: no deadlock" "$FX_CHAT_BLOCK_EXIT" \\
  "SET race.sleep_dating_matches_update = '1.5:race.s1';
   INSERT INTO race.outcome VALUES ('cb_exit',
     race.call(race.id('42'), format('SELECT to_jsonb(public.exit_dating_mode(%L))', race.key('cb-exit'))) ->> 'state');" \\
  "SELECT pg_sleep(0.5);
   INSERT INTO race.outcome SELECT 'cb_overlap', ((SELECT is_called FROM race.s1) AND (SELECT state FROM public.dating_matches WHERE id = $CB_MATCH) = 'active')::text;
   SELECT clock_timestamp() AS tb \\\\gset
   INSERT INTO race.outcome VALUES ('cb_block', race.call(race.id('41'), format('SELECT public.block_dating_match(%L, %L)', race.key('cb-block'), $CB_MATCH))::text);
   INSERT INTO race.outcome VALUES ('cb_block_s', round(extract(epoch FROM clock_timestamp() - :'tb'::timestamptz), 2)::text);" \\
  "SELECT CASE WHEN NOT (SELECT is_called FROM race.s1) THEN 'the leave never reached the match row (no race)'
               WHEN (SELECT result FROM race.outcome WHERE name = 'cb_exit') IS DISTINCT FROM 'exited' THEN 'leave lost'
               WHEN (SELECT result::jsonb FROM race.outcome WHERE name = 'cb_block') IS DISTINCT FROM '{\\"ok\\": true}'::jsonb
               THEN 'the block answered ' || coalesce((SELECT result FROM race.outcome WHERE name = 'cb_block'), 'none')
               WHEN NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_blocks WHERE blocker_user_id = race.id('41') AND blocked_user_id = race.id('42'))
               THEN 'no block row'
               WHEN (SELECT state || '/' || closed_reason FROM public.dating_matches WHERE id = $CB_MATCH) IS DISTINCT FROM 'closed/exit'
               THEN 'match: ' || coalesce((SELECT state || '/' || closed_reason FROM public.dating_matches WHERE id = $CB_MATCH), 'none')
               WHEN (SELECT result FROM race.outcome WHERE name = 'cb_overlap') IS DISTINCT FROM 'true'
                 OR coalesce((SELECT result::numeric FROM race.outcome WHERE name = 'cb_block_s'), 0) < 0.5
               THEN 'the block did not wait on the leave (no race)'
               ELSE 'ok' END;" serialised

# Spec 2 W6a (§4.4, chat spec §4.4): a send while the other blocks. B (44)
# blocks from the chat and sleeps in the match's update (holding its row);
# A (43) sends from a stale screen: the send waits on the match's row,
# then reads it blocked and answers closed. Nothing is stored after the
# block; no deadlock.
FX_SEND_BLOCK="SELECT race.reset(); ALTER SEQUENCE race.s1 RESTART;
SELECT race.ready('43', 'woman', ARRAY['man']), race.ready('44', 'man', ARRAY['woman']);
INSERT INTO race.outcome SELECT 'sb_match', race.match(race.id('43'), race.id('44'))::text;"
SB_MATCH="(SELECT result::uuid FROM race.outcome WHERE name = 'sb_match')"
run "a send while the other blocks: nothing after the block" "$FX_SEND_BLOCK" \\
  "SET race.sleep_dating_matches_update = '1.5:race.s1';
   INSERT INTO race.outcome VALUES ('sb_block', race.call(race.id('44'), format('SELECT public.block_dating_match(%L, %L)', race.key('sb-block'), $SB_MATCH))::text);" \\
  "SELECT pg_sleep(0.5);
   INSERT INTO race.outcome SELECT 'sb_overlap', ((SELECT is_called FROM race.s1) AND (SELECT state FROM public.dating_matches WHERE id = $SB_MATCH) = 'active')::text;
   SELECT clock_timestamp() AS tb \\\\gset
   INSERT INTO race.outcome VALUES ('sb_send', race.call(race.id('43'), format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''are you there?'')', race.key('sb-send'), $SB_MATCH))::text);
   INSERT INTO race.outcome VALUES ('sb_send_s', round(extract(epoch FROM clock_timestamp() - :'tb'::timestamptz), 2)::text);" \\
  "SELECT CASE WHEN NOT (SELECT is_called FROM race.s1) THEN 'the block never reached the match row (no race)'
               WHEN (SELECT result::jsonb FROM race.outcome WHERE name = 'sb_block') IS DISTINCT FROM '{\\"ok\\": true}'::jsonb
               THEN 'the block answered ' || coalesce((SELECT result FROM race.outcome WHERE name = 'sb_block'), 'none')
               WHEN (SELECT result::jsonb FROM race.outcome WHERE name = 'sb_send') IS DISTINCT FROM '{\\"ok\\": false, \\"reason\\": \\"closed\\"}'::jsonb
               THEN 'the send answered ' || coalesce((SELECT result FROM race.outcome WHERE name = 'sb_send'), 'none')
               WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_messages WHERE match_id = $SB_MATCH) THEN 'a message was stored after the block'
               WHEN (SELECT result FROM race.outcome WHERE name = 'sb_overlap') IS DISTINCT FROM 'true'
                 OR coalesce((SELECT result::numeric FROM race.outcome WHERE name = 'sb_send_s'), 0) < 0.5
               THEN 'the send did not wait on the block (no race)'
               ELSE 'ok' END;" serialised

$P -c "SELECT race.reset(); UPDATE public.feature_flags SET enabled = false WHERE key = 'dating_candidate_generation';" > /dev/null 2>&1
exit $fail"""),
], 'races: the chat block and send')
```

```python
                        """        PERFORM private.dating_bump_chat(v_other, p_match_id);"""),
  'unmatch_no_window': ("""           SET state = 'unmatched', closed_reason = 'unmatched', closed_at = coalesce(m.closed_at, now()),
               evidence_until = now() + interval '30 days'""",
                        """           SET state = 'unmatched', closed_reason = 'unmatched', closed_at = coalesce(m.closed_at, now())"""),
  'block_chat_untold': ("""  PERFORM private.dating_bump_chat(p_target_user_id, m.id) FROM public.dating_matches AS m WHERE m.pair_key = v_pair_key; -- Spec 2 W6a""",
                        """  -- Spec 2 W6a"""),
}
```

```dart
// Spec 2 W6a (§3.5, §3.6, §6.1; D8, D20): the chat's data. Every answer is
// read defensively (a malformed message dropped, an unknown refusal
// failed, never a server's text); the repository calls the chat RPCs with
// their parameters through the Dating RPC client; a busy or failing call
// answers a fixed code; the list is one entry per account (the next
// account's starts loading, nothing of the last); each chat signal is a
// new count.
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat_repository.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/data/dating_rpc.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

import '../support/dating_chat_fakes.dart';

void main() {
  test('a page reads defensively: masked parts, a malformed message dropped, '
      'an unknown refusal is failed', () {
    final page = DatingChatPage.fromJson({
      ...datingPageJson(),
      'messages': [
        datingMessageJson('d2', 'Call me on •••, or ••• later', minute: 2),
        {'id': 'bad', 'text': 7},
        datingMessageJson('d1', 'Hi!', mine: true, minute: 1),
      ],
    });
    expect(page.ok, isTrue);
    expect(page.header!.displayName, 'Kofi');
    expect(page.header!.age, 31);
    expect(page.header!.ended, isFalse);
    expect([for (final m in page.messages) m.id], ['d2', 'd1']);
    expect(datingMaskedParts(page.messages.first.text), [
      ('Call me on ', false),
      ('•••', true),
      (', or ', false),
      ('•••', true),
      (' later', false),
    ]);
    expect(
      DatingChatPage.fromJson({
        'ok': false,
        'reason': 'Kofi blocked you',
      }).reason,
      'failed',
    );
    expect(
      DatingChatPage.fromJson({
        'ok': false,
        'reason': 'match_unavailable',
      }).reason,
      'match_unavailable',
    );
    expect(DatingChatPage.fromJson('nope').reason, 'failed');
    final list = DatingConversations.fromJson(datingConversationsJson());
    expect([for (final c in list.conversations) c.matchId], ['m1', 'm2']);
    expect(list.conversations.first.isNew, isTrue);
    expect(list.conversations.last.last, isNull);
    final swap = DatingChatAnswer.fromJson({
      'ok': false,
      'reason': 'too_early',
      'from': '2026-11-23T18:00:00Z',
    });
    expect(swap.reason, 'too_early');
    expect(swap.from, DateTime.utc(2026, 11, 23, 18));
  });

  test('the repository calls the chat RPCs with their parameters', () async {
    final calls = <(String, Map<String, dynamic>?)>[];
    final repo = DatingChatRepository(
      DatingRpc.withCall((fn, params) async {
        calls.add((fn, params));
        return fn == 'get_my_dating_matches'
            ? datingConversationsJson()
            : fn == 'get_dating_messages'
            ? datingPageJson()
            : fn == 'send_dating_message'
            ? {'ok': true, 'message': datingMessageJson('s1', 'Hi', mine: true)}
            : {'ok': true};
      }),
      null,
    );
    await repo.conversations(ended: true);
    await repo.messages('m1');
    await repo.messages('m1', before: DateTime.utc(2026, 11, 20, 18));
    final sent = await repo.send('key-0123456789abcdef', 'm1', 'Hi');
    await repo.offerSwap('m1');
    await repo.withdrawSwap('m1');
    await repo.block('key-block-0123456789', 'm1');
    await repo.unmatch('key-unmatch-0123456', 'm1');
    expect(sent.ok, isTrue);
    expect(sent.message!.mine, isTrue);
    expect(
      [for (final (fn, params) in calls) '$fn ${jsonEncode(params)}'],
      [
        'get_my_dating_matches {"p_include_ended":true}',
        'get_dating_messages {"p_match_id":"m1","p_before":null,"p_limit":50}',
        'get_dating_messages {"p_match_id":"m1","p_before":"2026-11-20T18:00:00.000Z","p_limit":50}',
        'send_dating_message {"p_idempotency_key":"key-0123456789abcdef","p_match_id":"m1","p_body":"Hi"}',
        'offer_dating_contact_swap {"p_match_id":"m1"}',
        'withdraw_dating_contact_swap {"p_match_id":"m1"}',
        'block_dating_match {"p_idempotency_key":"key-block-0123456789","p_match_id":"m1"}',
        'unmatch_dating_match {"p_idempotency_key":"key-unmatch-0123456","p_match_id":"m1"}',
      ],
    );
    expect(await repo.signals('ama').isEmpty, isTrue);
  });

  test('a busy server and any failure answer fixed codes', () async {
    final busy = DatingChatRepository(
      DatingRpc.withCall(
        (fn, params) async =>
            throw const PostgrestException(message: 'dating:busy'),
      ),
      null,
    );
    expect((await busy.messages('m1')).reason, 'busy');
    expect((await busy.send('key-0123456789abcdef', 'm1', 'x')).reason, 'busy');
    expect((await busy.conversations()).reason, 'busy');
    final broken = DatingChatRepository(
      DatingRpc.withCall((fn, params) async => throw StateError('socket')),
      null,
    );
    expect((await broken.messages('m1')).reason, 'failed');
    expect((await broken.offerSwap('m1')).reason, 'failed');
    expect(
      (await broken.unmatch('key-0123456789abcdef', 'm1')).reason,
      'failed',
    );
  });

  test('D8: the list is one entry per account; the next account\'s starts '
      'loading and holds nothing of the last', () async {
    final fake = FakeDatingChat();
    final container = ProviderContainer(overrides: datingChatOverrides(fake));
    addTearDown(container.dispose);
    final sub = container.listen(
      datingConversationsProvider('ama'),
      (_, __) {},
    );
    addTearDown(sub.close);
    final ama = await container.read(datingConversationsProvider('ama').future);
    expect(ama.conversations.first.displayName, 'Kofi');
    fake.listHold = Completer<DatingConversations>();
    final next = container.listen(
      datingConversationsProvider('esi'),
      (_, __) {},
    );
    addTearDown(next.close);
    final esi = container.read(datingConversationsProvider('esi'));
    expect(esi.isLoading, isTrue);
    expect(esi.valueOrNull, isNull);
    expect(
      container.read(datingConversationsProvider(null).future),
      completion(predicate<DatingConversations>((d) => !d.ok)),
    );
  });

  test(
    'each chat signal is a new count; nothing listens while signed out',
    () async {
      final fake = FakeDatingChat();
      final container = ProviderContainer(overrides: datingChatOverrides(fake));
      addTearDown(container.dispose);
      final seen = <int>[];
      final sub = container.listen(
        datingChatSignalsProvider('ama'),
        (_, next) => next.whenData(seen.add),
      );
      addTearDown(sub.close);
      await Future<void>.delayed(Duration.zero);
      fake.signalEvents.add(null);
      fake.signalEvents.add(null);
      await Future<void>.delayed(Duration.zero);
      expect(seen, [1, 2]);
      expect(fake.signalAccounts, ['ama']);
      final none = container.listen(
        datingChatSignalsProvider(null),
        (_, __) {},
      );
      addTearDown(none.close);
      await Future<void>.delayed(Duration.zero);
      expect(fake.signalAccounts, ['ama']);
    },
  );
}
```

Create `test/features/dating/support/dating_chat_fakes.dart` with exactly (the fake every chat test uses; it grows with Tasks 7, 9 and 10):

```dart
// Spec 2 W6a: the chat faked for widget tests. Every answer is the test's;
// every call is recorded; a read or a send can be held open (D8 tests);
// the signals are a stream the test drives.
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat_repository.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

final datingChatT0 = DateTime.utc(2026, 11, 20, 18);

/// A message as the server sends it.
Map<String, Object?> datingMessageJson(
  String id,
  String text, {
  bool mine = false,
  int minute = 0,
}) => {
  'id': id,
  'mine': mine,
  'kind': 'text',
  'question_id': null,
  'text': text,
  'at': datingChatT0.add(Duration(minutes: minute)).toIso8601String(),
};

/// A page as the server sends it (newest first), [over] on top.
Map<String, Object?> datingPageJson({
  String matchId = 'm1',
  String name = 'Kofi',
  int? age = 31,
  bool ended = false,
  Map<String, Object?> swap = const {
    'mine': false,
    'theirs': false,
    'both': false,
  },
  DateTime? swapFrom,
  List<Map<String, Object?>>? messages,
  bool more = false,
}) => {
  'ok': true,
  'chat': {
    'match_id': matchId,
    'display_name': name,
    'age': age,
    'state': ended ? 'ended' : 'active',
    'swap': {
      ...swap,
      'from':
          (swapFrom ?? datingChatT0.subtract(const Duration(days: 1)))
              .toIso8601String(),
    },
  },
  'messages':
      messages ??
      [
        datingMessageJson('d2', 'Call me on •••', minute: 2),
        datingMessageJson('d1', 'Hi Ama!', mine: true, minute: 1),
      ],
  'more': more,
};

Map<String, Object?> datingConversationsJson({
  List<Map<String, Object?>>? rows,
  List<Map<String, Object?>> ended = const [],
}) => {
  'ok': true,
  'conversations':
      rows ??
      [
        {
          'match_id': 'm1',
          'display_name': 'Kofi',
          'age': 31,
          'last': datingMessageJson('d2', 'Call me on •••', minute: 2),
          'new': true,
        },
        {
          'match_id': 'm2',
          'display_name': 'Yaw',
          'age': 34,
          'last': null,
          'new': false,
        },
      ],
  'ended': ended,
};

class FakeDatingChat implements DatingChatGateway {
  FakeDatingChat({Map<String, Object?>? page, Map<String, Object?>? list})
    : pageJson = page ?? datingPageJson(),
      listJson = list ?? datingConversationsJson();

  Map<String, Object?> pageJson;
  Map<String, Object?> listJson;
  Completer<DatingChatPage>? hold;
  Completer<DatingConversations>? listHold;
  Completer<DatingSendAnswer>? sendHold;
  DatingSendAnswer Function(String text)? sendAnswer;
  DatingChatAnswer swapAnswer = const DatingChatAnswer(
    ok: true,
    swap: DatingSwap(mine: true),
  );
  DatingChatAnswer endAnswer = const DatingChatAnswer(ok: true);
  final signalEvents = StreamController<void>.broadcast();

  final reads = <(String, DateTime?)>[];
  int listReads = 0;
  final sends = <(String key, String matchId, String text)>[];
  final swaps = <(String matchId, bool offer)>[];
  final blocks = <(String key, String matchId)>[];
  final unmatches = <(String key, String matchId)>[];
  final signalAccounts = <String>[];

  @override
  Future<DatingConversations> conversations({bool ended = false}) async {
    listReads++;
    final h = listHold;
    if (h != null) return h.future;
    return DatingConversations.fromJson(listJson);
  }

  @override
  Future<DatingChatPage> messages(String matchId, {DateTime? before}) async {
    reads.add((matchId, before));
    final h = hold;
    if (h != null) return h.future;
    return DatingChatPage.fromJson(pageJson);
  }

  @override
  Future<DatingSendAnswer> send(String key, String matchId, String text) async {
    sends.add((key, matchId, text));
    final h = sendHold;
    if (h != null) return h.future;
    final a = sendAnswer;
    if (a != null) return a(text);
    return DatingSendAnswer.fromJson({
      'ok': true,
      'message': datingMessageJson(
        's${sends.length}',
        text,
        mine: true,
        minute: 9,
      ),
    });
  }

  @override
  Future<DatingChatAnswer> offerSwap(String matchId) async {
    swaps.add((matchId, true));
    return swapAnswer;
  }

  @override
  Future<DatingChatAnswer> withdrawSwap(String matchId) async {
    swaps.add((matchId, false));
    return swapAnswer;
  }

  @override
  Future<DatingChatAnswer> block(String key, String matchId) async {
    blocks.add((key, matchId));
    return endAnswer;
  }

  @override
  Future<DatingChatAnswer> unmatch(String key, String matchId) async {
    unmatches.add((key, matchId));
    return endAnswer;
  }

  @override
  Stream<void> signals(String account) {
    signalAccounts.add(account);
    return signalEvents.stream;
  }
}

List<Override> datingChatOverrides(FakeDatingChat fake) => [
  datingChatGatewayProvider.overrideWithValue(fake),
];
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the data layer**

Create `lib/features/dating/chat/dating_chat.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 2 W6a (§3.5, §3.6, §6.1): the dating chat as the server sends it.
// Every field is read defensively (a malformed message is dropped, an
// unknown refusal is failed, never a server's text). The text a message
// carries is the server's view of it: masked ("•••") until both people
// have swapped. Memory only (D20): nothing here is written to disk.

/// The chat RPCs' fixed refusals.
const datingChatReasons = {
  'invalid',
  'reverify_device',
  'match_unavailable',
  'closed',
  'restricted',
  'rate_limited',
  'too_early',
  'already_swapped',
  'busy',
};

/// The span every hidden contact detail becomes (§6.1).
const kDatingMask = '•••';

String _fixed(Object? reason) =>
    reason is String && datingChatReasons.contains(reason) ? reason : 'failed';

DateTime? _time(Object? raw) =>
    raw is String ? DateTime.tryParse(raw)?.toUtc() : null;

String? _text(Object? raw) => raw is String ? raw : null;

/// One message as this person reads it.
class DatingChatMessage {
  const DatingChatMessage({
    required this.id,
    required this.mine,
    required this.text,
    required this.at,
    this.kind = 'text',
    this.questionId,
  });

  final String id;
  final bool mine;
  final String kind;
  final String? questionId;
  final String text;
  final DateTime at;

  static DatingChatMessage? fromJson(Object? raw) {
    if (raw is! Map) return null;
    final id = raw['id'];
    final text = raw['text'];
    final at = _time(raw['at']);
    if (id is! String || text is! String || at == null) return null;
    return DatingChatMessage(
      id: id,
      mine: raw['mine'] == true,
      kind: _text(raw['kind']) ?? 'text',
      questionId: _text(raw['question_id']),
      text: text,
      at: at,
    );
  }
}

/// The text in order, each part a plain run or a hidden contact detail.
List<(String, bool)> datingMaskedParts(String text) {
  final parts = <(String, bool)>[];
  var rest = text;
  while (rest.isNotEmpty) {
    final i = rest.indexOf(kDatingMask);
    if (i < 0) {
      parts.add((rest, false));
      break;
    }
    if (i > 0) parts.add((rest.substring(0, i), false));
    parts.add((kDatingMask, true));
    rest = rest.substring(i + kDatingMask.length);
  }
  return parts;
}

/// Swap contacts: each side's offer, and from when it can be offered.
class DatingSwap {
  const DatingSwap({
    this.mine = false,
    this.theirs = false,
    this.both = false,
    this.from,
  });

  final bool mine;
  final bool theirs;
  final bool both;
  final DateTime? from;

  factory DatingSwap.fromJson(Object? raw, {DateTime? from}) {
    if (raw is! Map) return DatingSwap(from: from);
    return DatingSwap(
      mine: raw['mine'] == true,
      theirs: raw['theirs'] == true,
      both: raw['both'] == true,
      from: _time(raw['from']) ?? from,
    );
  }

  /// Whether Swap can be offered at [now].
  bool open(DateTime now) => from == null || !now.isBefore(from!);
}

/// The chat's header: the other person's first name and age, and whether
/// the conversation has ended.
class DatingChatHeader {
  const DatingChatHeader({
    required this.matchId,
    this.displayName = '',
    this.age,
    this.ended = false,
    this.swap = const DatingSwap(),
  });

  final String matchId;
  final String displayName;
  final int? age;
  final bool ended;
  final DatingSwap swap;

  static DatingChatHeader? fromJson(Object? raw) {
    if (raw is! Map || raw['match_id'] is! String) return null;
    return DatingChatHeader(
      matchId: raw['match_id'] as String,
      displayName: (_text(raw['display_name']) ?? '').trim(),
      age: raw['age'] is int ? raw['age'] as int : null,
      ended: raw['state'] != 'active',
      swap: DatingSwap.fromJson(raw['swap']),
    );
  }
}

/// One page of the chat, newest first.
class DatingChatPage {
  const DatingChatPage({
    this.ok = false,
    this.reason,
    this.header,
    this.messages = const [],
    this.more = false,
  });

  static const failed = DatingChatPage(reason: 'failed');
  static const busy = DatingChatPage(reason: 'busy');

  final bool ok;
  final String? reason;
  final DatingChatHeader? header;
  final List<DatingChatMessage> messages;
  final bool more;

  factory DatingChatPage.fromJson(Object? raw) {
    if (raw is! Map) return failed;
    if (raw['ok'] != true) return DatingChatPage(reason: _fixed(raw['reason']));
    final header = DatingChatHeader.fromJson(raw['chat']);
    final list = raw['messages'];
    if (header == null || list is! List) return failed;
    return DatingChatPage(
      ok: true,
      header: header,
      messages: [
        for (final m in list.map(DatingChatMessage.fromJson))
          if (m != null) m,
      ],
      more: raw['more'] == true,
    );
  }
}

/// send_dating_message()'s answer.
class DatingSendAnswer {
  const DatingSendAnswer({required this.ok, this.reason, this.message});

  static const busy = DatingSendAnswer(ok: false, reason: 'busy');
  static const failed = DatingSendAnswer(ok: false, reason: 'failed');

  final bool ok;
  final String? reason;
  final DatingChatMessage? message;

  factory DatingSendAnswer.fromJson(Object? raw) {
    if (raw is! Map) return failed;
    if (raw['ok'] != true) {
      return DatingSendAnswer(ok: false, reason: _fixed(raw['reason']));
    }
    final m = DatingChatMessage.fromJson(raw['message']);
    return m == null ? failed : DatingSendAnswer(ok: true, message: m);
  }
}

/// An answer that only says ok or why not (swap, block, unmatch).
class DatingChatAnswer {
  const DatingChatAnswer({required this.ok, this.reason, this.swap, this.from});

  static const busy = DatingChatAnswer(ok: false, reason: 'busy');
  static const failed = DatingChatAnswer(ok: false, reason: 'failed');

  final bool ok;
  final String? reason;
  final DatingSwap? swap;

  /// too_early: when Swap opens.
  final DateTime? from;

  factory DatingChatAnswer.fromJson(Object? raw) {
    if (raw is! Map) return failed;
    if (raw['ok'] != true) {
      return DatingChatAnswer(
        ok: false,
        reason: _fixed(raw['reason']),
        from: _time(raw['from']),
      );
    }
    return DatingChatAnswer(
      ok: true,
      swap: raw['swap'] is Map ? DatingSwap.fromJson(raw['swap']) : null,
    );
  }
}

/// One row of the conversations list.
class DatingConversation {
  const DatingConversation({
    required this.matchId,
    this.displayName = '',
    this.age,
    this.last,
    this.isNew = false,
  });

  final String matchId;
  final String displayName;
  final int? age;
  final DatingChatMessage? last;
  final bool isNew;
}

/// A conversation that ended in the last 30 days.
class DatingEndedConversation {
  const DatingEndedConversation({required this.matchId, this.displayName = ''});

  final String matchId;
  final String displayName;
}

class DatingConversations {
  const DatingConversations({
    this.ok = false,
    this.reason,
    this.conversations = const [],
    this.ended = const [],
  });

  static const failed = DatingConversations(reason: 'failed');
  static const busy = DatingConversations(reason: 'busy');

  final bool ok;
  final String? reason;
  final List<DatingConversation> conversations;
  final List<DatingEndedConversation> ended;

  factory DatingConversations.fromJson(Object? raw) {
    if (raw is! Map) return failed;
    if (raw['ok'] != true) {
      return DatingConversations(reason: _fixed(raw['reason']));
    }
    final rows = raw['conversations'];
    if (rows is! List) return failed;
    final ended = raw['ended'];
    return DatingConversations(
      ok: true,
      conversations: [
        for (final r in rows.whereType<Map>())
          if (r['match_id'] is String)
            DatingConversation(
              matchId: r['match_id'] as String,
              displayName: (_text(r['display_name']) ?? '').trim(),
              age: r['age'] is int ? r['age'] as int : null,
              last: DatingChatMessage.fromJson(r['last']),
              isNew: r['new'] == true,
            ),
      ],
      ended: [
        if (ended is List)
          for (final r in ended.whereType<Map>())
            if (r['match_id'] is String)
              DatingEndedConversation(
                matchId: r['match_id'] as String,
                displayName: (_text(r['display_name']) ?? '').trim(),
              ),
      ],
    );
  }
}
```

Create `lib/features/dating/chat/dating_chat_repository.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 2 W6a (§3.5, §3.6, §4.4): the chat's calls, all through the Dating
// RPC client (its device header), and its "something changed" signal
// through the same Dating-scoped client's realtime (the person's own
// dating_chat_signals rows; no content). Every call is bounded; a failure
// answers failed or busy, never the error's text.
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/data/dating_rpc.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

abstract class DatingChatGateway {
  /// The conversations list; with [ended], the last 30 days' ended ones.
  Future<DatingConversations> conversations({bool ended = false});

  /// The newest page of [matchId], or the page before [before].
  Future<DatingChatPage> messages(String matchId, {DateTime? before});

  Future<DatingSendAnswer> send(String key, String matchId, String text);

  Future<DatingChatAnswer> offerSwap(String matchId);

  Future<DatingChatAnswer> withdrawSwap(String matchId);

  Future<DatingChatAnswer> block(String key, String matchId);

  Future<DatingChatAnswer> unmatch(String key, String matchId);

  /// One event each time something changed in one of [account]'s chats.
  Stream<void> signals(String account);
}

class DatingChatRepository implements DatingChatGateway {
  DatingChatRepository(this._rpc, this._client);

  final DatingRpc _rpc;
  final SupabaseClient? _client;

  Future<T> _guard<T>(
    Future<dynamic> Function() call,
    T Function(Object? raw) parse,
    T busy,
    T failed,
  ) async {
    try {
      return parse(await call());
    } on DatingBusy {
      return busy;
    } catch (_) {
      return failed;
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<DatingConversations> conversations({bool ended = false}) => _guard(
    () => _rpc.call('get_my_dating_matches', {'p_include_ended': ended}),
    DatingConversations.fromJson,
    DatingConversations.busy,
    DatingConversations.failed,
  );

  @override
  Future<DatingChatPage> messages(String matchId, {DateTime? before}) => _guard(
    () => _rpc.call('get_dating_messages', {
      'p_match_id': matchId,
      'p_before': before?.toUtc().toIso8601String(),
      'p_limit': 50,
    }),
    DatingChatPage.fromJson,
    DatingChatPage.busy,
    DatingChatPage.failed,
  );

  @override
  Future<DatingSendAnswer> send(String key, String matchId, String text) =>
      _guard(
        () => _rpc.call('send_dating_message', {
          'p_idempotency_key': key,
          'p_match_id': matchId,
          'p_body': text,
        }),
        DatingSendAnswer.fromJson,
        DatingSendAnswer.busy,
        DatingSendAnswer.failed,
      );

  Future<DatingChatAnswer> _answer(String fn, Map<String, dynamic> params) =>
      _guard(
        () => _rpc.call(fn, params),
        DatingChatAnswer.fromJson,
        DatingChatAnswer.busy,
        DatingChatAnswer.failed,
      );

  @override
  Future<DatingChatAnswer> offerSwap(String matchId) =>
      _answer('offer_dating_contact_swap', {'p_match_id': matchId});

  @override
  Future<DatingChatAnswer> withdrawSwap(String matchId) =>
      _answer('withdraw_dating_contact_swap', {'p_match_id': matchId});

  @override
  Future<DatingChatAnswer> block(String key, String matchId) => _answer(
    'block_dating_match',
    {'p_idempotency_key': key, 'p_match_id': matchId},
  );

  @override
  Future<DatingChatAnswer> unmatch(String key, String matchId) => _answer(
    'unmatch_dating_match',
    {'p_idempotency_key': key, 'p_match_id': matchId},
  );

  @override
  Stream<void> signals(String account) {
    final client = _client;
    if (client == null) return const Stream.empty();
    late final RealtimeChannel channel;
    final events = StreamController<void>(
      onCancel: () => unawaited(client.removeChannel(channel)),
    );
    channel =
        client
            .channel('dating-chat:${const Uuid().v4()}')
            .onPostgresChanges(
              event: PostgresChangeEvent.all,
              schema: 'public',
              table: 'dating_chat_signals',
              filter: PostgresChangeFilter(
                type: PostgresChangeFilterType.eq,
                column: 'user_id',
                value: account,
              ),
              callback: (_) {
                if (!events.isClosed) events.add(null);
              },
            )
            .subscribe();
    return events.stream;
  }
}
```

Create `lib/features/dating/chat/dating_chat_providers.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 2 W6a (D8, D20): the chat's reads, one entry per account (the next
// account's entry starts as a plain loading value, never holding the last
// one's conversations), memory only, disposed when nothing shows them.
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat_repository.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/data/dating_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/data/dating_supabase_client.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

final datingChatGatewayProvider = Provider<DatingChatGateway>(
  (ref) => DatingChatRepository(
    ref.watch(datingRpcProvider),
    ref.watch(datingSupabaseClientProvider),
  ),
);

/// The conversations list (with the ended ones) for [account].
final datingConversationsProvider = FutureProvider.autoDispose
    .family<DatingConversations, String?>((ref, account) async {
      if (account == null) return DatingConversations.failed;
      return ref.read(datingChatGatewayProvider).conversations(ended: true);
    });

/// A count of [account]'s chat signals while something listens: each new
/// value means "read again". Never an error (a dropped connection is
/// silence; the screens read again when they open and on resume).
final datingChatSignalsProvider = StreamProvider.autoDispose
    .family<int, String?>((ref, account) async* {
      if (account == null) return;
      var n = 0;
      try {
        await for (final _ in ref
            .read(datingChatGatewayProvider)
            .signals(account)) {
          yield ++n;
        }
      } catch (_) {
        // silence
      }
    });
```

```dart
// Spec 2 W6a (§3.5, §3.6, §3.15 Chat, §6.1, §7.4; D8): the conversations
// list and the chat. Rows read the name, the last message as the reader
// reads it and a dot for something new; the chat reads with sender names,
// a hidden span says why on a tap and reads "hidden contact detail"; a
// send shows "Sending…", then nothing, or "Not sent. Tap to try again."
// (the same request again); offline nothing is sent and the words stay;
// Swap from 72 hours, then each state; Block and Unmatch from the Safety
// sheet; an ended chat is read-only; a chat signal reads again; another
// account sees nothing of this chat and nothing typed is sent as it; the
// moment's Say hello opens the chat.
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/app/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/conversations_screen.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat.dart';

import '../support/dating_shell.dart';
import '../support/dating_today_fakes.dart';

Future<void> tap(WidgetTester t, Finder finder) async {
  await tapInReach(t, finder);
  await t.pump();
  await t.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 300));
}

void main() {
  final loc = AppLocalizationsEn();
  final now = datingChatT0.add(const Duration(minutes: 30));

  Future<DatingShell> open(
    WidgetTester t,
    FakeDatingChat fake, {
    bool online = true,

  }) => pumpDatingShell(
    t,

    online: online,
    textScale: textScale,
    overrides: datingChatOverrides(fake),
  );

  group('the list', () {
    testWidgets('rows: name and age, the last message as read, a dot for '
        'something new, the ended ones below; a row opens its chat', (t) async {
      final fake = FakeDatingChat(
        list: datingConversationsJson(

            {'match_id': 'm9', 'display_name': 'Esi'},

      expect(find.text(loc.dating_chatsTitle), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('Kofi, 31'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('Call me on •••'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text(loc.dating_chatsNoMessage), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_chats_dot')), findsOneWidget);

        find.bySemanticsLabel(

        ),

      );
      expect(find.text(loc.dating_chatsEnded), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('Esi'), findsOneWidget);
      await tap(t, find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_chats_row_m1')));
      await t.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.byType(DatingChatScreen), findsOneWidget);
    });

    testWidgets('empty: the spec\'s line', (t) async {
      final fake = FakeDatingChat(
        list: datingConversationsJson(rows: const []),
      );
      await open(t, fake, initial: DatingPaths.chats);
      expect(find.text(loc.dating_chatsEmpty), findsOneWidget);
    });
  });

  group('the chat', () {
    testWidgets('the header, messages with sender names, a hidden span read '
        'as hidden and explained on a tap; no receipts', (t) async {
      final fake = FakeDatingChat();
      await open(t, fake);
      expect(find.text('Kofi, 31'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(
        find.bySemanticsLabel('Kofi: Call me on ${loc.dating_chatMaskedLabel}'),
        findsOneWidget,
      );
      expect(
        find.bySemanticsLabel('${loc.dating_chatYou}: Hi Ama!'),
        findsOneWidget,
      );
      expect(find.bySemanticsLabel(loc.dating_chatSafetyLabel), findsWidgets);
      for (final word in ['Seen', 'Read', 'typing', 'Online', 'Last seen']) {
        expect(find.textContaining(word), findsNothing, reason: word);
      }
      await tap(t, find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_chat_masked')));
      await t.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.text(loc.dating_chatMaskedExplain), findsOneWidget);
    });

    testWidgets('a send shows Sending…, then nothing; a failure says so and a '
        'tap tries the same request again', (t) async {
      final fake = FakeDatingChat();
      final hold = fake.sendHold = Completer<DatingSendAnswer>();
      await open(t, fake);
      await t.enterText(
        find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_chat_composer')),
        'Hello Kofi',
      );
      await tap(t, find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_chat_send')));
      expect(find.text(loc.dating_chatSending), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('Hello Kofi'), findsOneWidget);
      hold.complete(DatingSendAnswer.failed);
      await t.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.text(loc.dating_chatNotSent), findsOneWidget);
      fake.sendHold = null;
      await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_chatNotSent));
      await t.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(fake.sends.length, 2);
      expect(fake.sends[1].$1, fake.sends[0].$1);
      expect(fake.sends[1].$3, 'Hello Kofi');
      expect(find.text(loc.dating_chatSending), findsNothing);
      expect(find.text(loc.dating_chatNotSent), findsNothing);
      expect(fake.reads.length, greaterThanOrEqualTo(2));
    });

    testWidgets('offline: nothing is sent, the words stay, the spec\'s line', (
      t,
    ) async {
      final fake = FakeDatingChat();

      await t.enterText(

      expect(fake.sends, isEmpty);
      expect(find.text(loc.dating_chatOffline), findsOneWidget);

    testWidgets('Swap: disabled before 72 hours with its time; open: the sheet '

        t,
        initial: '/dating/chat/m1',
        overrides: datingChatOverrides(fake),
      );
      expect(
        find.textContaining('${loc.dating_chatSwap} (from'),

      );
      final chip = t.widget<ActionChip>(
        find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_chat_swap')),
      );
      expect(chip.onPressed, isNull);

      fake.pageJson = datingPageJson();
      await pumpDatingShell(

      await tap(t, find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_chat_swap')));
      await t.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.text(loc.dating_chatSwapSheetBody), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text(loc.dating_chatSwapCard), findsOneWidget);
      await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_chatSwapConfirm));
      await t.pumpAndSettle();

      await pumpDatingShell(
        t,
        initial: '/dating/chat/m1',
        overrides: datingChatOverrides(fake),
      );
      await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_chatSwapCancel));
      await t.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(fake.swaps.last, ('m1', false));
    });

    testWidgets(
      'Block and Unmatch from the Safety sheet, each confirmed once',
      (t) async {
        final fake = FakeDatingChat();
        await open(t, fake);
        await tap(t, find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_chat_safety')));
        await t.pumpAndSettle();
        expect(find.text(loc.dating_blockRow('Kofi')), findsOneWidget);
        expect(find.text(loc.dating_chatSafetyUnmatch), findsOneWidget);
        expect(find.text(loc.dating_chatSafetyResources), findsOneWidget);
        await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_chatSafetyUnmatch));
        await t.pumpAndSettle();
        expect(find.text(loc.dating_unmatchTitle('Kofi')), findsOneWidget);
        expect(find.text(loc.dating_unmatchBody('Kofi')), findsOneWidget);
        fake.pageJson = datingPageJson(ended: true);
        await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_unmatchConfirm));
        await t.pumpAndSettle();
        expect(fake.unmatches.length, 1);
        expect(find.text(loc.dating_chatEnded), findsOneWidget);
        expect(
          find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_chat_composer')),
          findsNothing,
        );

        fake.pageJson = datingPageJson();
        await open(t, fake);
        await tap(t, find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_chat_safety')));
        await t.pumpAndSettle();
        await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_blockRow('Kofi')));
        await t.pumpAndSettle();
        await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_cardBlock));
        await t.pumpAndSettle();
        expect(fake.blocks.length, 1);
        expect(find.text(loc.dating_blockDone('Kofi')), findsOneWidget);
        expect(
          find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_chat_composer')),
          findsNothing,
        );
      },
    );

    testWidgets('a chat signal reads again', (t) async {
      final fake = FakeDatingChat();
      await open(t, fake);
      final before = fake.reads.length;
      fake.pageJson = datingPageJson(
        messages: [
          datingMessageJson('d3', 'Are you free Saturday?', minute: 3),
        ],
      );
      fake.signalEvents.add(null);
      await t.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(fake.reads.length, before + 1);
      expect(find.text('Are you free Saturday?'), findsOneWidget);
    });

    testWidgets('D8: another account sees nothing of this chat, and nothing '
        'typed is sent as it', (t) async {
      final fake = FakeDatingChat();
      final s = await open(t, fake);
      expect(find.text('Kofi, 31'), findsOneWidget);
      await t.enterText(
        find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_chat_composer')),
```
