```dart
      s.container.read(datingTestAccount.notifier).state = 'esi';
      await t.pump();
      expect(find.text('Kofi, 31'), findsNothing);
      expect(find.text('Call me on •••'), findsNothing);
      expect(find.text('Hello Kofi'), findsNothing);
      // The next account's own chat (a test stands it in under the same id).
      fake.hold!.complete(DatingChatPage.fromJson(datingPageJson(name: 'Yaw')));
      await t.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.text('Yaw, 31'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(
        t
            .widget<TextField>(
              find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_chat_composer')),
            )
            .controller!
            .text,
        isEmpty,
      );
      await tap(t, find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_chat_send')));
      expect(fake.sends, isEmpty);
    });

    testWidgets('the moment\'s Say hello opens the chat', (t) async {
      final fake = FakeDatingChat();
      final s = await pumpDatingShell(
        t,
        initial: '/dating/match/m1',
        overrides: [

      expect(find.byType(DatingChatScreen), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('Kofi, 31'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(s.router.canPop(), isFalse);
    });
  });

  testWidgets('goldens, guidelines and a dark render', (t) async {
    for (final (name, screen) in [
      ('dating_chat', () => DatingChatScreen(matchId: 'm1', now: () => now)),
      ('dating_chats', () => const DatingConversationsScreen()),

      await pumpFlowScreen(

        screen,
        overrides: [
          ...datingShellOverrides(),
          ...datingChatOverrides(FakeDatingChat()),

      );
      await expectFlowGuidelines(t);
      await pumpFlowScreen(
        t,
        () => Theme(data: AppTheme.darkTheme, child: screen()),
        overrides: [
          ...datingShellOverrides(),
          ...datingChatOverrides(FakeDatingChat()),
        ],
      );
      expect(t.takeException(), isNull);
    }
  });
}
```

Create `$D/arb_fragment_T4.txt` with exactly:

```text
  "dating_chatsTitle": "Conversations",
```

Create `lib/features/dating/chat/chat_sheets.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 2 W6a (§3.6; D8): the chat's sheets. The Safety sheet (Block,
// Unmatch, Safety resources; W6c and W7a add their rows), Unmatch's
// confirm, Swap contacts with its one-time card, and what a hidden span
// means. Each answers once (a second tap does nothing), shows nothing of
// the last account once the signed-in account changes and then closes
// choosing nothing, and carries a Dating sheet's route name (the unlock
// removes it). None of them is ever animated beyond the sheet itself.
import 'package:attune/app/theme/design_tokens.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/ui/text/allow_large_text.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/widgets/info_row_widget.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_buttons.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/data/dating_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/gentle_wait/gentle_wait_providers.dart'
    show datingOnlineProvider;
import 'package:attune/features/dating/lock/dating_sheets.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/section/dating_calm_line.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

/// What the Safety sheet's person chose.
enum DatingSafetyChoice { block, unmatch, resources }

Future<T?> _show<T>(
  BuildContext context,
  String name,
  String? account,
  Widget Function(BuildContext context, void Function(T?) close) builder,
) => showModalBottomSheet<T>(
  context: context,
  routeSettings: datingSheetSettings(name),
  isScrollControlled: true,
  builder:
      (sheet) => DatingSheetTracker(
        child: AllowLargeText(
          child: _OnceSheet<T>(account: account, builder: builder),
        ),
      ),
);

class _OnceSheet<T> extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const _OnceSheet({required this.account, required this.builder});

  final String? account;
  final Widget Function(BuildContext context, void Function(T?) close) builder;

  @override
  ConsumerState<_OnceSheet<T>> createState() => _OnceSheetState<T>();
}

class _OnceSheetState<T> extends ConsumerState<_OnceSheet<T>> {
  bool _closed = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    ref.listenManual<String?>(datingAccountProvider, (previous, next) {
      if (next == widget.account) return;
      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => _close(null));
    });
  }

  void _close(T? chosen) {
    if (_closed || !mounted) return;
    _closed = true;
    final route = ModalRoute.of(context);
    if (route == null || !route.isActive) return;
    if (route.isCurrent) {
      Navigator.of(context).pop(chosen);
    } else {
      route.navigator?.removeRoute(route);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (ref.watch(datingAccountProvider) != widget.account) {
      return const SizedBox.shrink();
    }
    return widget.builder(context, _close);
  }
}

Widget _frame(BuildContext context, String title, List<Widget> children) =>
    SafeArea(
      child: SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Semantics(
              header: true,
              child: Text(title, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
            ...children,
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );

/// §3.6 the Safety sheet, opened as [account] for [name]'s chat. W6a's
/// rows: Block, Unmatch (while the chat is open) and Safety resources.
Future<DatingSafetyChoice?> showDatingSafetySheet(
  BuildContext context, {
  required String? account,
  required String name,
  required bool ended,
}) => _show<DatingSafetyChoice>(context, 'chat-safety', account, (
  context,
  close,
) {
  final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
  Widget row(String title, IconData icon, DatingSafetyChoice choice) =>
      InfoRowWidget(
        title: title,
        subtitle: '',
        icon: icon,
        showAvatar: false,
        disableTrailing: true,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: Spacing.xs),
        onTap: () => close(choice),
      );
  return _frame(context, loc.dating_chatSafety, [
    row(
      name.isEmpty ? loc.dating_blockRowNoName : loc.dating_blockRow(name),
      Icons.block,
      DatingSafetyChoice.block,
    ),
    if (!ended)
      row(
        loc.dating_chatSafetyUnmatch,
        Icons.person_remove_outlined,
        DatingSafetyChoice.unmatch,
      ),
    row(
      loc.dating_chatSafetyResources,
      Icons.health_and_safety_outlined,
      DatingSafetyChoice.resources,
    ),
  ]);
});

/// §3.6 Unmatch's confirm: true when the person chose Unmatch. Offline,
/// Unmatch waits (nothing is queued).
Future<bool> showDatingUnmatchSheet(
  BuildContext context, {
  required String? account,
  required String name,
}) async =>
    await _show<bool>(context, 'chat-unmatch', account, (context, close) {
      return Consumer(
        builder: (context, ref, _) {
          final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
          final online = ref.watch(datingOnlineProvider).valueOrNull ?? true;
          return _frame(
            context,
            name.isEmpty
                ? loc.dating_unmatchTitleNoName
                : loc.dating_unmatchTitle(name),
            [
              Text(
                name.isEmpty
                    ? loc.dating_unmatchBodyNoName
                    : loc.dating_unmatchBody(name),
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: Spacing.md),
              if (!online) DatingCalmLine(loc.dating_offline),
              FlowPrimaryButton(
                label: loc.dating_unmatchConfirm,
                onPressed: online ? () => close(true) : null,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
              FlowSecondaryButton(
                label: loc.dating_blockCancel,
                onPressed: () => close(false),
              ),
            ],
          );
        },
      );
    }) ==
    true;

/// §3.6 Swap contacts: what swapping means and the one-time card (shown
/// each time the sheet opens: nothing is stored to remember it, D20).
/// True when the person chose Swap.
Future<bool> showDatingSwapSheet(
  BuildContext context, {
  required String? account,
}) async =>
    await _show<bool>(context, 'chat-swap', account, (context, close) {
      final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
      final theme = Theme.of(context);
      return _frame(context, loc.dating_chatSwap, [
        Text(loc.dating_chatSwapSheetBody, style: theme.textTheme.bodyLarge),
        const SizedBox(height: Spacing.md),
        Semantics(
          container: true,
          child: Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: theme.colorScheme.secondaryContainer,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
            child: Row(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Icon(
                  Icons.shield_outlined,
                  color: theme.colorScheme.onSecondaryContainer,
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: Spacing.sm),
                Expanded(
                  child: Text(
                    loc.dating_chatSwapCard,
                    style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                      color: theme.colorScheme.onSecondaryContainer,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: Spacing.md),
        FlowPrimaryButton(
          label: loc.dating_chatSwapConfirm,
          onPressed: () => close(true),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
        FlowSecondaryButton(
          label: loc.dating_chatSwapNotYet,
          onPressed: () => close(false),
        ),
      ]);
    }) ==
    true;

/// §3.6: what a hidden span is.
Future<void> showDatingMaskedSheet(
  BuildContext context, {
  required String? account,
}) => _show<void>(context, 'chat-masked', account, (context, close) {
  final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
  return _frame(context, loc.dating_chatMaskedTitle, [
    Text(
      loc.dating_chatMaskedExplain,
      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge,
    ),
    const SizedBox(height: Spacing.md),
    FlowSecondaryButton(label: loc.dating_chatOk, onPressed: () => close(null)),
  ]);
});
```

Create `lib/features/dating/chat/conversations_screen.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 2 W6a (§3.5 the conversations list, §3.15): each row the other
// person's first name and age, the last message as this person reads it
// (hidden spans "•••"), and a dot (never a count) for something new; the
// conversations that ended in the last 30 days below. A standard list,
// read again on a chat signal and on the way back from a chat. D8: one
// entry per account; memory only.
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/app/theme/design_tokens.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/ui/text/allow_large_text.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_buttons.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_text_field.dart'
    show FlowErrorLine;
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/data/dating_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/gentle_wait/gentle_wait_providers.dart'
    show datingOnlineProvider;
import 'package:attune/features/dating/routes/dating_routes.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/section/dating_calm_line.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/verify/dating_reverify_line.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

class DatingConversationsScreen extends ConsumerWidget {
  const DatingConversationsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final account = ref.watch(datingAccountProvider);
    ref.listen(datingChatSignalsProvider(account), (previous, next) {
      if (next.hasValue && next.value != previous?.value) {
        ref.invalidate(datingConversationsProvider(account));
      }
    });
    final read = ref.watch(datingConversationsProvider(account));
    final online = ref.watch(datingOnlineProvider).valueOrNull ?? true;
    void reread() => ref.invalidate(datingConversationsProvider(account));
    return AllowLargeText(
      child: Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(title: Text(loc.dating_chatsTitle)),
        body: SafeArea(
          child: switch (read) {
            AsyncData(value: final d) when d.ok => _list(
              context,
              loc,
              d,
              reread,
            ),
            AsyncData(value: final d) => ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
              children: [_refusal(loc, d.reason, online, reread)],
            ),
            AsyncError() => ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
              children: [_refusal(loc, 'failed', online, reread)],
            ),
            _ => const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _refusal(
    AppLocalizations loc,
    String? reason,
    bool online,
    VoidCallback reread,
  ) {
    final retry = FlowSecondaryButton(
      label: loc.dating_tryAgain,
      onPressed: reread,
    );
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: switch (reason) {
        _ when !online => [DatingCalmLine(loc.dating_offline), retry],
        'reverify_device' => [const DatingReverifyLine()],
        'busy' => [DatingCalmLine(loc.dating_busy), retry],
        _ => [
          FlowErrorLine(text: loc.dating_errorGeneric, liveRegion: true),
          retry,
        ],
      },
    );
  }

  String _spoken(AppLocalizations loc, String text) =>
      [
        for (final (part, masked) in datingMaskedParts(text))
          masked ? ' ${loc.dating_chatMaskedLabel} ' : part,
      ].join().replaceAll(RegExp(' {2,}'), ' ').trim();

  Widget _list(
    BuildContext context,
    AppLocalizations loc,
    DatingConversations d,
    VoidCallback reread,
  ) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    void open(String matchId) =>
        unawaited(context.push(DatingPaths.chat(matchId)).whenComplete(reread));
    String named(String name, int? age) =>
        name.isEmpty
            ? loc.dating_chatTitleNoName
            : age == null
            ? name
            : loc.dating_chatTitle(name, age);
    return ListView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: Spacing.sm),
      children: [
        if (d.conversations.isEmpty)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
            child: Text(
              loc.dating_chatsEmpty,
              style: theme.textTheme.bodyLarge,
            ),
          ),
        for (final c in d.conversations)
          Builder(
            builder: (context) {
              final title = named(c.displayName, c.age);
              final last = c.last;
              final preview =
                  last == null
                      ? loc.dating_chatsNoMessage
                      : last.mine
                      ? loc.dating_chatsYouSaid(last.text)
                      : last.text;
              final spoken = [
                title,
                last == null
                    ? loc.dating_chatsNoMessage
                    : last.mine
                    ? loc.dating_chatsYouSaid(_spoken(loc, last.text))
                    : _spoken(loc, last.text),
                if (c.isNew) loc.dating_chatsNew,
              ].join(', ');
              return Semantics(
                button: true,
                label: spoken,
                excludeSemantics: true,
                onTap: () => open(c.matchId),
                child: ListTile(
                  key: ValueKey('dating_chats_row_${c.matchId}'),
                  minVerticalPadding: Spacing.sm,
                  title: Text(title),
                  subtitle: Text(
                    preview,
                    maxLines: 2,
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  ),
                  trailing:
                      c.isNew
                          ? Container(
                            key: const ValueKey('dating_chats_dot'),
                            width: 10,
                            height: 10,
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: theme.colorScheme.primary,
                              shape: BoxShape.circle,
                            ),
                          )
                          : null,
                  onTap: () => open(c.matchId),
                ),
              );
            },
          ),
        if (d.ended.isNotEmpty) ...[
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
              Spacing.md,
              Spacing.lg,
              Spacing.md,
              Spacing.xs,
            ),
            child: Semantics(
              header: true,
              child: Text(
                loc.dating_chatsEnded,
                style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          for (final e in d.ended)
            Semantics(
              container: true,
              child: ListTile(
                key: ValueKey('dating_chats_ended_${e.matchId}'),
                title: Text(
                  e.displayName.isEmpty
                      ? loc.dating_chatTitleNoName
                      : e.displayName,
                ),
                subtitle: Text(loc.dating_chatEnded),
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ],
    );
  }
}
```

Create `lib/features/dating/chat/dating_chat_screen.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 2 W6a (§3.6, §3.15 Chat, §6.1): the dating chat, its own screen and
// widgets (nothing from lib/features/chat or games: an architecture test
// pins it). "{Name}, {age}" and the Safety button; Swap contacts from 72
// hours, then its states; the messages as a list read with sender names,
// hidden spans "•••" read "hidden contact detail" (a tap says why); text
// only, 1-1,000 characters, no attachment of any kind; own messages show
// "Sending…", then nothing, or "Not sent. Tap to try again."; no read
// receipts, typing or presence. New messages appear without animation.
// An ended chat is read-only under "This conversation has ended." It reads
// again on a chat signal, on resume and on the way back from a sheet.
// D8: another account sees nothing of this chat, and nothing typed is
// sent as the next person. Memory only (D20): nothing typed is kept when
// the screen closes.
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/app/routing/app_router.dart' show RouteNames;
import 'package:attune/app/theme/design_tokens.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/ui/text/allow_large_text.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/utils/date_formatter.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_buttons.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_text_field.dart'
    show FlowErrorLine;
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/chat_sheets.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat_repository.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/data/dating_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/data/dating_rpc.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/gentle_wait/gentle_wait_providers.dart'
    show datingOnlineProvider;
import 'package:attune/features/dating/section/dating_calm_line.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/today/block_sheet.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/verify/dating_reverify_line.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

/// §3.6: at most this many characters in a message.
const kDatingMessageMax = 1000;

/// One of this person's messages on its way, or not sent.
class _Outgoing {
  _Outgoing(this.key, this.text);

  final String key;
  final String text;
  bool failed = false;
  String? reason;

  /// Sent: the id the server gave it (it goes when a read lists it).
  String? sentId;
}

class DatingChatScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const DatingChatScreen({super.key, required this.matchId, this.now});

  final String matchId;

  /// The clock (tests pin it).
  final DateTime Function()? now;

  @override
  ConsumerState<DatingChatScreen> createState() => _DatingChatScreenState();
}

class _DatingChatScreenState extends ConsumerState<DatingChatScreen>
    with WidgetsBindingObserver {
  final _keys = DatingActionKeys();
  final _composer = TextEditingController();
  late final DatingChatGateway _gateway;
  String? _account;
  DatingChatPage? _page;
  final _older = <DatingChatMessage>[];
  bool _more = false;
  bool _loadingOlder = false;
  final _outgoing = <_Outgoing>[];
  String? _blockedName;
  bool _offlineTried = false;
  String? _actionReason;
  int _reads = 0;

  DateTime get _now => (widget.now ?? DateTime.now)();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addObserver(this);
    _gateway = ref.read(datingChatGatewayProvider);
    _account = ref.read(datingAccountProvider);
    // D8: another account starts from nothing; nothing typed is sent as it.
    ref.listenManual<String?>(datingAccountProvider, (previous, next) {
      if (previous == next || !mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _account = next;
        _page = null;
        _older.clear();
        _more = false;
        _outgoing.clear();
        _blockedName = null;
        _offlineTried = false;
        _actionReason = null;
        _composer.clear();
      });
      unawaited(_read());
    });
    unawaited(_read());
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    WidgetsBinding.instance.removeObserver(this);
    _composer.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  void didChangeAppLifecycleState(AppLifecycleState state) {
    if (state == AppLifecycleState.resumed) unawaited(_read());
  }

  /// The newest page, read again (a signal, resume, a sheet closing).
  Future<void> _read() async {
    final account = _account;
    final n = ++_reads;
    if (account == null) return;
    final page = await _gateway.messages(widget.matchId);
    if (!mounted || _account != account || n != _reads) return;
    setState(() {
      _page = page;
      if (page.ok && _older.isEmpty) _more = page.more;
      // A message sent and now listed is no longer on its way.
      final shown = {for (final m in page.messages) m.id};
      _outgoing.removeWhere((o) => shown.contains(o.sentId));
    });
  }

  Future<void> _loadOlder() async {
    final account = _account;
    final all = _messages;
    if (_loadingOlder || all.isEmpty) return;
    setState(() => _loadingOlder = true);
    final page = await _gateway.messages(widget.matchId, before: all.last.at);
    if (!mounted || _account != account) return;
    setState(() {
      _loadingOlder = false;
      if (page.ok) {
        _older.addAll(page.messages);
        _more = page.more;
      }
    });
  }

  /// Every message, newest first (the newest page, then older pages).
  List<DatingChatMessage> get _messages {
    final page = _page;
    if (page == null || !page.ok) return const [];
    final ids = <String>{};
    return [
      for (final m in [...page.messages, ..._older])
        if (ids.add(m.id)) m,
    ];
  }

  Future<void> _send(_Outgoing? retry) async {
    final online = ref.read(datingOnlineProvider).valueOrNull ?? true;
    final text = retry?.text ?? _composer.text;
    if (text.trim().isEmpty || text.length > kDatingMessageMax) return;
    if (!online) {
      // §3.6: nothing is queued; what was typed stays.
      setState(() => _offlineTried = true);
      return;
    }
    final account = _account;
    final out =
        retry ?? _Outgoing(_keys.keyFor('send', [widget.matchId, text]), text);
    setState(() {
      _offlineTried = false;
      out.failed = false;
      out.reason = null;
      if (retry == null) {
        _outgoing.add(out);
        _composer.clear();
      }
    });
    final r = await _gateway.send(out.key, widget.matchId, out.text);
    _keys.settle('send', reason: r.reason);
    if (!mounted || _account != account) return;
    setState(() {
      if (r.ok) {
        out.sentId = r.message?.id;
      } else {
        out.failed = true;
        out.reason = r.reason;
      }
    });
    if (r.ok || r.reason == 'closed') unawaited(_read());
  }

  Future<void> _swap(DatingSwap swap) async {
    final account = _account;
    if (swap.mine) {
      final r = await _gateway.withdrawSwap(widget.matchId);
      if (!mounted || _account != account) return;
      setState(() => _actionReason = r.ok ? null : r.reason);
      unawaited(_read());
      return;
    }
    if (!await showDatingSwapSheet(context, account: account)) return;
    if (!mounted || _account != account) return;
    final r = await _gateway.offerSwap(widget.matchId);
    if (!mounted || _account != account) return;
    setState(() => _actionReason = r.ok ? null : r.reason);
    unawaited(_read());
  }

  Future<void> _safety(DatingChatHeader header) async {
    final account = _account;
    final name = header.displayName;
    final choice = await showDatingSafetySheet(
      context,
      account: account,
      name: name,
      ended: header.ended,
    );
    if (!mounted || _account != account || choice == null) return;
    switch (choice) {
      case DatingSafetyChoice.block:
        if (!await showDatingBlockSheet(
          context,
          account: account,
          name: name,
        )) {
          return;
        }
        if (!mounted || _account != account) return;
        final r = await _gateway.block(
          _keys.keyFor('block', widget.matchId),
          widget.matchId,
        );
        _keys.settle('block', reason: r.reason);
        if (!mounted || _account != account) return;
        setState(() {
          _actionReason = r.ok ? null : r.reason;
          if (r.ok) {
            _blockedName = name;
            _composer.clear();
            _outgoing.clear();
          }
        });
        unawaited(_read());
      case DatingSafetyChoice.unmatch:
        if (!await showDatingUnmatchSheet(
          context,
          account: account,
          name: name,
        )) {
          return;
        }
        if (!mounted || _account != account) return;
        final r = await _gateway.unmatch(
          _keys.keyFor('unmatch', widget.matchId),
          widget.matchId,
        );
        _keys.settle('unmatch', reason: r.reason);
        if (!mounted || _account != account) return;
        setState(() => _actionReason = r.ok ? null : r.reason);
        unawaited(_read());
      case DatingSafetyChoice.resources:
        unawaited(context.push(RouteNames.safetyResources));
    }
  }

  String _reasonLine(AppLocalizations loc, String? reason) => switch (reason) {
    'closed' => loc.dating_chatEnded,
    'restricted' => loc.dating_chatRestricted,
    'rate_limited' => loc.dating_errorRateLimited,
    'busy' => loc.dating_busy,
    'match_unavailable' => loc.dating_cardGone,
    _ => loc.dating_errorGeneric,
  };

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    // A chat signal: read again (the other person wrote, swapped, or ended it).
    ref.listen(datingChatSignalsProvider(_account), (previous, next) {
      if (next.hasValue && next.value != previous?.value) unawaited(_read());
    });
    final online = ref.watch(datingOnlineProvider).valueOrNull ?? true;
    final page = _page;
    final header = page?.header;
    final name = header?.displayName ?? '';
    final title =
        header == null
            ? ''
            : name.isEmpty
            ? loc.dating_chatTitleNoName
            : header.age == null
            ? name
            : loc.dating_chatTitle(name, header.age!);
    return AllowLargeText(
      child: Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(
          title: Semantics(header: true, child: Text(title)),
          actions: [
            if (header != null)
              Semantics(
                button: true,
                label: loc.dating_chatSafetyLabel,
                excludeSemantics: true,
                onTap: () => _safety(header),
                child: IconButton(
                  key: const ValueKey('dating_chat_safety'),
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.shield_outlined),
                  tooltip: loc.dating_chatSafetyLabel,
                  onPressed: () => _safety(header),
                ),
              ),
          ],
        ),
        body: SafeArea(
          child: switch (page) {
            null => const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
            DatingChatPage(ok: false) => ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
              children: [_refusal(loc, page.reason, online)],
            ),
            _ => _chat(context, loc, header!, online),
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _refusal(AppLocalizations loc, String? reason, bool online) {
    final retry = FlowSecondaryButton(
      label: loc.dating_tryAgain,
      onPressed: () => unawaited(_read()),
    );
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: switch (reason) {
        _ when !online => [DatingCalmLine(loc.dating_offline), retry],
        'reverify_device' => [const DatingReverifyLine()],
        'match_unavailable' => [DatingCalmLine(loc.dating_cardGone)],
        'busy' => [DatingCalmLine(loc.dating_busy), retry],
        _ => [
          FlowErrorLine(text: loc.dating_errorGeneric, liveRegion: true),
          retry,
        ],
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _chat(
    BuildContext context,
    AppLocalizations loc,
    DatingChatHeader header,
    bool online,
  ) {
    final messages = _messages;
    final name = header.displayName;
    final ended = header.ended || _blockedName != null;
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        if (!ended) _swapRow(context, loc, header),
        Expanded(
          child: Semantics(
            container: true,
            explicitChildNodes: true,
            child: ListView(
              key: const ValueKey('dating_chat_messages'),
              reverse: true,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                horizontal: Spacing.md,
                vertical: Spacing.sm,
              ),
              children: [
                for (final o in _outgoing.reversed) _outgoingBubble(loc, o),
                for (final m in messages) _bubble(context, loc, m, name),
                if (_more)
                  Center(
                    child: FlowTextLink(
                      label: loc.dating_chatLoadOlder,
                      onPressed: () => unawaited(_loadOlder()),
                    ),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
        if (_actionReason != null)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: Spacing.md),
            child: DatingCalmLine(_reasonLine(loc, _actionReason)),
          ),
        if (_blockedName != null)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
            child: DatingCalmLine(
              _blockedName!.isEmpty
                  ? loc.dating_blockDoneNoName
                  : loc.dating_blockDone(_blockedName!),
            ),
          )
        else if (ended)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
            child: DatingCalmLine(loc.dating_chatEnded),
          )
        else
          _composerRow(context, loc, online),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _swapRow(
    BuildContext context,
    AppLocalizations loc,
    DatingChatHeader h,
  ) {
    final s = h.swap;
    final name = h.displayName;
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    Widget line(String text, [Widget? action]) => Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(Spacing.md, Spacing.xs, Spacing.md, 0),
      child: Wrap(
        crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
        spacing: Spacing.sm,
        children: [
          Semantics(
            liveRegion: true,
            child: Text(text, style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium),
          ),
          if (action != null) action,
        ],
      ),
    );
    Widget chip(String label, {required bool enabled}) => ActionChip(
      key: const ValueKey('dating_chat_swap'),
      avatar: const Icon(Icons.swap_horiz, size: 18),
      label: Text(label),
      onPressed: enabled ? () => _swap(s) : null,
    );
    if (s.both) return line(loc.dating_chatSwapDone);
    if (s.mine) {
      return line(
        name.isEmpty
            ? loc.dating_chatSwapOfferedNoName
            : loc.dating_chatSwapOffered(name),
        FlowTextLink(
          label: loc.dating_chatSwapCancel,
          onPressed: () => _swap(s),
        ),
      );
    }
    if (s.theirs) {
      return line(
        name.isEmpty
            ? loc.dating_chatSwapTheirsNoName
            : loc.dating_chatSwapTheirs(name),
        chip(loc.dating_chatSwap, enabled: true),
      );
    }
    final from = s.from?.toLocal();
    if (from != null && _now.isBefore(s.from!)) {
      return Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
          Spacing.md,
          Spacing.xs,
          Spacing.md,
          0,
        ),
        child: Align(
          alignment: AlignmentDirectional.centerStart,
          child: chip(
            loc.dating_chatSwapFrom(
              MyDateFormat.join([
                MyDateFormat.toDateShort(from),
                MyDateFormat.toTime(from),
              ]),
            ),
            enabled: false,
          ),
        ),
      );
    }
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(Spacing.md, Spacing.xs, Spacing.md, 0),
      child: Align(
        alignment: AlignmentDirectional.centerStart,
        child: chip(loc.dating_chatSwap, enabled: true),
      ),
    );
  }

  /// A message's words, each hidden span "•••" (tap: what it means; read
  /// as "hidden contact detail").
  Widget _words(
    BuildContext context,
    AppLocalizations loc,
    String text,
    TextStyle? style,
  ) {
    final parts = datingMaskedParts(text);
    if (parts.every((p) => !p.$2)) return Text(text, style: style);
    return Wrap(
      crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
      children: [
        for (final (part, masked) in parts)
          if (masked)
            InkWell(
              key: const ValueKey('dating_chat_masked'),
              onTap: () => showDatingMaskedSheet(context, account: _account),
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 2),
                child: Text(
                  kDatingMask,
                  style: style?.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
                ),
              ),
            )
          else
            Text(part, style: style),
      ],
    );
  }

  String _spoken(AppLocalizations loc, String text) =>
      [
        for (final (part, masked) in datingMaskedParts(text))
          masked ? ' ${loc.dating_chatMaskedLabel} ' : part,
      ].join().replaceAll(RegExp(' {2,}'), ' ').trim();

  Widget _bubble(
    BuildContext context,
    AppLocalizations loc,
    DatingChatMessage m,
    String name,
  ) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final sender =
        m.mine
            ? loc.dating_chatYou
            : (name.isEmpty ? loc.dating_chatThem : name);
    final color =
        m.mine
            ? theme.colorScheme.primaryContainer
            : theme.colorScheme.surfaceContainerHighest;
    final onColor =
        m.mine
            ? theme.colorScheme.onPrimaryContainer
            : theme.colorScheme.onSurface;
    final masked = datingMaskedParts(m.text).any((p) => p.$2);
    // Read as one line with its sender; a hidden span reads "hidden contact
    // detail", and the message, tapped, says what that means.
    return Semantics(
      container: true,
      label: '$sender: ${_spoken(loc, m.text)}',
      button: masked,
      excludeSemantics: true,
      onTap:
          masked
              ? () => showDatingMaskedSheet(context, account: _account)
              : null,
      child: Align(
        alignment:
            m.mine
                ? AlignmentDirectional.centerEnd
                : AlignmentDirectional.centerStart,
        child: Container(
          margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: Spacing.xs),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
            horizontal: Spacing.md,
            vertical: Spacing.sm,
          ),
          constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 320),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: color,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          ),
          child: _words(
            context,
            loc,
            m.text,
            theme.textTheme.bodyLarge?.copyWith(color: onColor),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _outgoingBubble(AppLocalizations loc, _Outgoing o) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    // §3.6: "Sending…", then nothing.
    final status =
        o.failed
            ? loc.dating_chatNotSent
            : o.sentId == null
            ? loc.dating_chatSending
            : '';
    final bubble = Align(
      alignment: AlignmentDirectional.centerEnd,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
        children: [
          Container(
            margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: Spacing.xs),
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
              horizontal: Spacing.md,
              vertical: Spacing.sm,
            ),
            constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 320),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: theme.colorScheme.primaryContainer,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
            ),
            child: Text(
              o.text,
              style: theme.textTheme.bodyLarge?.copyWith(
                color: theme.colorScheme.onPrimaryContainer,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: Spacing.xs, top: 2),
            child: Text(
              o.failed &&
                      o.reason != null &&
                      o.reason != 'failed' &&
                      o.reason != 'busy'
                  ? _reasonLine(loc, o.reason)
                  : status,
              style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                color:
                    o.failed
                        ? theme.colorScheme.error
                        : theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    final label = '${loc.dating_chatYou}: ${o.text}. $status';
    if (!o.failed) {
      return Semantics(
        container: true,
        liveRegion: true,
        label: label,
        child: ExcludeSemantics(child: bubble),
      );
    }
    // "Not sent. Tap to try again." (closed and restricted never retry).
    final retry = o.reason != 'closed' && o.reason != 'restricted';
    return Semantics(
      container: true,
      liveRegion: true,
      button: retry,
      label: label,
      excludeSemantics: true,
      onTap: retry ? () => _send(o) : null,
      child: InkWell(onTap: retry ? () => _send(o) : null, child: bubble),
    );
  }

  Widget _composerRow(BuildContext context, AppLocalizations loc, bool online) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
        Spacing.md,
        Spacing.xs,
        Spacing.xs,
        Spacing.sm,
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          if (_offlineTried && !online) DatingCalmLine(loc.dating_chatOffline),
          Row(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: TextField(
                  key: const ValueKey('dating_chat_composer'),
                  controller: _composer,
                  minLines: 1,
                  maxLines: 5,
                  maxLength: kDatingMessageMax,
                  textCapitalization: TextCapitalization.sentences,
                  enableSuggestions: false,
                  autocorrect: false,
                  enableIMEPersonalizedLearning: false,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    hintText: loc.dating_chatComposerHint,
                    counterText: '',
                    border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: Spacing.xs),
              SizedBox(
                width: 48,
                height: 48,
                child: IconButton(
                  key: const ValueKey('dating_chat_send'),
                  tooltip: loc.dating_chatSend,
                  color: theme.colorScheme.primary,
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.send),
                  onPressed: () => _send(null),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the failing sections**

Create `$D/contract_T5.sql` with exactly (sections M–Q; the lexicon is read from the file above; ends with the marker Task 8 replaces):

```sql
\set lexicon `cat supabase/functions/_shared/dating_chat_lexicon_v1.json`
SELECT length(set_config('w6.lexicon', :'lexicon', true)) AS lexicon_loaded \gset
-- The worker's claim, made to take [p_msg] (every other pending message
-- waits behind it).
CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.claim_only(p_msg uuid) RETURNS void LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
  UPDATE public.dating_message_safety_outbox SET created_at = clock_timestamp() + interval '1 day'
   WHERE state = 'pending' AND message_id <> p_msg;
  PERFORM 1 FROM public.claim_dating_safety_jobs(1);
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- M. (§6.1, §3.13; chat spec §2.3, §6, §9) A send queues its message for
--    the worker in the chat's own outbox (never the couples one) and a
--    neutral push for the recipient (me_update, no name, no text): at
--    most one per 15 minutes, none with "messages" off; without the push
--    key the message is still delivered and the push counted as failed.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  a constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('60');
  b constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('61', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"]}');
  v_m uuid;
  v_couples bigint := (SELECT count(*) FROM public.message_safety_outbox);
  v_failed bigint;
  v_ok text;
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (user_low_id, user_high_id, state) VALUES (least(a, b), greatest(a, b), 'active') RETURNING id INTO v_m;
  PERFORM pg_temp.j(a, '60', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''Hello Kofi'')', pg_temp.key('o1'), v_m));
  PERFORM pg_temp.j(a, '60', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''Are you there?'')', pg_temp.key('o2'), v_m));
  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.dating_message_safety_outbox AS o JOIN public.dating_messages AS d ON d.id = o.message_id
       WHERE d.match_id = v_m AND o.state = 'pending' AND o.attempts = 0) <> 2
     OR (SELECT count(*) FROM public.message_safety_outbox) <> v_couples THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a send did not queue its message for the chat''s worker, or reached the couples outbox';
  END IF;
  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.scheduled_notifications WHERE user_id = b) <> 1
     OR (SELECT metadata FROM public.scheduled_notifications WHERE user_id = b)
        IS DISTINCT FROM '{"type": "me_update", "route": "me", "title": "Attune", "body": "You have something new in Attune."}'::jsonb
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.scheduled_notifications WHERE user_id = a) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the message push is not one neutral me_update for the recipient (per 15 minutes)';
  END IF;
  DELETE FROM public.scheduled_notifications WHERE user_id = b;
  INSERT INTO public.dating_settings (user_id, chat_push) VALUES (b, false)
  ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO UPDATE SET chat_push = false;
  PERFORM pg_temp.j(a, '60', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''Still there?'')', pg_temp.key('o3'), v_m));
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.scheduled_notifications WHERE user_id = b) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a push was queued with "messages" off';
  END IF;
  UPDATE public.dating_settings SET chat_push = true WHERE user_id = b;
  v_failed := pg_temp.metric('dating.chat.push_failed');
  PERFORM set_config('app.settings.dating_push_key', '', true);
  v_ok := pg_temp.j(a, '60', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''No key'')', pg_temp.key('o4'), v_m)) ->> 'ok';
  PERFORM set_config('app.settings.dating_push_key', 'spec2-w6-push-key', true);
  IF v_ok IS DISTINCT FROM 'true' OR pg_temp.metric('dating.chat.push_failed') <> v_failed + 1 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: without the push key the message was not delivered, or the failed push not counted';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- N. (§6.1, §4.3; chat spec §3.2, §9) The worker's calls. A claim moves
--    pending messages to processing (one more attempt) and is the one way
--    a body leaves the database (the service role's alone); a completion
--    writes each hit as an event for the recipient only, once per message
--    and rule, never the text; tier 2 and 3 open a case about the sender;
--    malformed hits are refused; an error goes back to pending, then to
--    dead_letter after the 5th attempt (counted); with no config the
--    message is done with no events (counted); a stale claim is recovered.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  a constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('62');
  b constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('63', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"]}');
  v_m uuid;
  v_msg uuid;
  v_claim record;
  v jsonb;
  v_n bigint;
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (user_low_id, user_high_id, state, matched_at)
  VALUES (least(a, b), greatest(a, b), 'active', now() - interval '3 days') RETURNING id INTO v_m;
  v_msg := (pg_temp.j(b, '63', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''send me momo for transport'')', pg_temp.key('n1'), v_m))
            #>> '{message,id}')::uuid;
  UPDATE public.dating_message_safety_outbox SET created_at = clock_timestamp() + interval '1 day'
   WHERE state = 'pending' AND message_id <> v_msg;
  IF has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'public.claim_dating_safety_jobs(integer)', 'EXECUTE')
     OR has_function_privilege('anon', 'public.complete_dating_safety_job(uuid,text,jsonb,text)', 'EXECUTE')
     OR NOT has_function_privilege('service_role', 'public.claim_dating_safety_jobs(integer)', 'EXECUTE')
     OR has_table_privilege('service_role', 'public.dating_messages', 'SELECT') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the worker''s calls are not the service role''s alone, or it reads messages directly';
  END IF;
  SELECT * INTO v_claim FROM public.claim_dating_safety_jobs(1);
  IF v_claim.message_id IS DISTINCT FROM v_msg OR v_claim.body IS DISTINCT FROM 'send me momo for transport' OR NOT v_claim.new_match
     OR (SELECT state || ':' || attempts FROM public.dating_message_safety_outbox WHERE message_id = v_msg) IS DISTINCT FROM 'processing:1' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the claim did not hand over the oldest message as processing (%)', v_claim;
  END IF;
  IF public.complete_dating_safety_job(v_msg, 'dating_chat_v1', '[{"rule_id": "x", "family": "couples_control", "tier": 1}]') ->> 'reason'
     IS DISTINCT FROM 'invalid' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a hit of an unknown family was taken';
  END IF;
  v := public.complete_dating_safety_job(v_msg, 'dating_chat_v1',
         '[{"rule_id": "financial_request:send me momo", "family": "financial_request", "tier": 2},
           {"rule_id": "age_signal:i m 16", "family": "age_signal", "tier": 3}]');
  IF v IS DISTINCT FROM '{"ok": true, "state": "done", "events": 2}'::jsonb
     OR (SELECT array_agg(e.family || ':' || (e.at_risk_user_id = a)::text || ':' || (e.sender_user_id = b)::text ORDER BY e.family)
           FROM public.dating_safety_events AS e WHERE e.message_id = v_msg)
        IS DISTINCT FROM ARRAY['age_signal:true:true', 'financial_request:true:true']
     OR (SELECT array_agg(c.signal_kind || ':' || c.priority ORDER BY c.signal_kind) FROM public.dating_moderation_cases AS c
          WHERE c.target_user_id = b) IS DISTINCT FROM ARRAY['chat_age_signal:P1', 'chat_financial_request:P1'] THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the completion answered % (events for the recipient, cases about the sender)', v;
  END IF;
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_schema = 'public' AND table_name = 'dating_safety_events'
               AND column_name ~ '(body|text|message_text|content)') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the events can hold message text';
  END IF;
  -- An error: back to pending; the 5th attempt dead-letters, counted.
  v_msg := (pg_temp.j(b, '63', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''hello again'')', pg_temp.key('n2'), v_m))
            #>> '{message,id}')::uuid;
  v_n := pg_temp.metric('dating.safety.dead_letter');
  FOR i IN 1..5 LOOP
    PERFORM pg_temp.claim_only(v_msg);
    v := public.complete_dating_safety_job(v_msg, 'dating_chat_v1', '[]', 'engine_error');
  END LOOP;
  IF v IS DISTINCT FROM '{"ok": true, "state": "dead_letter"}'::jsonb OR pg_temp.metric('dating.safety.dead_letter') <> v_n + 1
     OR (SELECT state || ':' || last_error_code FROM public.dating_message_safety_outbox WHERE message_id = v_msg)
        IS DISTINCT FROM 'dead_letter:engine_error' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: five failed attempts did not dead-letter (counted): %', v;
  END IF;
  -- No config: done, no events, counted.
  v_msg := (pg_temp.j(b, '63', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''send me momo'')', pg_temp.key('n3'), v_m))
            #>> '{message,id}')::uuid;
  PERFORM pg_temp.claim_only(v_msg);
  v_n := pg_temp.metric('dating.safety.no_config');
  v := public.complete_dating_safety_job(v_msg, NULL, '[]');
  IF v IS DISTINCT FROM '{"ok": true, "state": "done", "events": 0}'::jsonb
     OR pg_temp.metric('dating.safety.no_config') <> v_n + 1 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: with no config the message was not done with no events (counted)';
  END IF;
  -- A stale claim comes back.
  v_msg := (pg_temp.j(b, '63', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''one more'')', pg_temp.key('n4'), v_m))
            #>> '{message,id}')::uuid;
  PERFORM pg_temp.claim_only(v_msg);
  UPDATE public.dating_message_safety_outbox SET processing_started_at = now() - interval '11 minutes' WHERE message_id = v_msg;
  v_n := public.recover_stale_dating_safety_jobs(); -- its own statement first (an IF's OR has no order)
  IF v_n < 1 OR (SELECT state FROM public.dating_message_safety_outbox WHERE message_id = v_msg) IS DISTINCT FROM 'pending' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a stale claim was not recovered';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- O. (§3.6 Safety cards, §6.1; chat spec §1.2) The cards are the
--    recipient's alone (never the sender's, nobody else's, not on another
--    phone), tier 1 and 2 only; a threat card names the country's
--    emergency resource; a dismissed card is gone and later detection is
--    unchanged.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  a constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('64');
  b constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('65', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"]}');
  o constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('66');
  v_m uuid;
  v_msg uuid;
  v jsonb;
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.support_resources (id, country_code, category, name, phone, verified_at, verified_by, catalogue_version)
  VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), 'GH', 'emergency', 'Ghana emergency', '112', current_date, 'contract', 'w6-contract');
  INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (user_low_id, user_high_id, state) VALUES (least(a, b), greatest(a, b), 'active') RETURNING id INTO v_m;
  v_msg := (pg_temp.j(b, '65', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''I know where you live'')', pg_temp.key('o1'), v_m))
            #>> '{message,id}')::uuid;
  PERFORM pg_temp.claim_only(v_msg);
  PERFORM public.complete_dating_safety_job(v_msg, 'dating_chat_v1',
    '[{"rule_id": "threat_intimidation:i know where you live", "family": "threat_intimidation", "tier": 2},
      {"rule_id": "identity_inconsistency:x", "family": "identity_inconsistency", "tier": 3}]');
  v := pg_temp.j(a, '64', format('SELECT public.get_my_dating_safety_cards(%L)', v_m));
  IF jsonb_array_length(v -> 'cards') <> 1 OR v #>> '{cards,0,family}' IS DISTINCT FROM 'threat_intimidation'
     OR v #>> '{cards,0,message_id}' IS DISTINCT FROM v_msg::text
     OR v #> '{cards,0,resource}' IS DISTINCT FROM '{"sms": null, "url": null, "name": "Ghana emergency", "phone": "112"}'::jsonb THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the recipient''s cards are %', v;
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.j(b, '65', format('SELECT public.get_my_dating_safety_cards(%L)', v_m)) IS DISTINCT FROM '{"ok": true, "cards": []}'::jsonb
     OR pg_temp.j(o, '66', format('SELECT public.get_my_dating_safety_cards(%L)', v_m)) IS DISTINCT FROM '{"ok": true, "cards": []}'::jsonb
     OR pg_temp.call(a, pg_temp.dev('99'), format('SELECT public.get_my_dating_safety_cards(%L)', v_m))::jsonb ->> 'reason'
        IS DISTINCT FROM 'reverify_device' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the sender, an outsider or another phone saw a card';
  END IF;
  PERFORM pg_temp.j(o, '66', format('SELECT public.dismiss_dating_safety_card(%L)', v #>> '{cards,0,id}'));
  IF jsonb_array_length(pg_temp.j(a, '64', format('SELECT public.get_my_dating_safety_cards(%L)', v_m)) -> 'cards') <> 1 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: someone else dismissed the recipient''s card';
  END IF;
  PERFORM pg_temp.j(a, '64', format('SELECT public.dismiss_dating_safety_card(%L)', v #>> '{cards,0,id}'));
  v_msg := (pg_temp.j(b, '65', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''watch your back'')', pg_temp.key('o2'), v_m))
            #>> '{message,id}')::uuid;
  PERFORM pg_temp.claim_only(v_msg);
  PERFORM public.complete_dating_safety_job(v_msg, 'dating_chat_v1',
    '[{"rule_id": "threat_intimidation:watch your back", "family": "threat_intimidation", "tier": 2}]');
  v := pg_temp.j(a, '64', format('SELECT public.get_my_dating_safety_cards(%L)', v_m));
  IF jsonb_array_length(v -> 'cards') <> 1 OR v #>> '{cards,0,message_id}' IS DISTINCT FROM v_msg::text THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a dismissal hid a later card, or kept the dismissed one: %', v;
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- P. (§5.10; carry 8) The sender's repeated patterns open signal cases:
--    money asks to two recipients in 7 days (P1), sexual pressure to two,
--    the same opener in three matches in 7 days; two matches alone don't.
--    Never a restriction (chat spec §5.1).
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  s constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('67', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"]}');
  r1 constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('68');
  r2 constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('69');
  r3 constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('70');
  v_m uuid[] := '{}';
  v_msg uuid;
  x uuid;
  i integer := 0;
BEGIN
  FOREACH x IN ARRAY ARRAY[r1, r2, r3] LOOP
    INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (user_low_id, user_high_id, state) VALUES (least(s, x), greatest(s, x), 'active');
    v_m := v_m || (SELECT id FROM public.dating_matches WHERE pair_key = public.dating_pair_key(s, x));
  END LOOP;
  FOREACH x IN ARRAY v_m LOOP
    i := i + 1;
    v_msg := (pg_temp.j(s, '67', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''Hello dear, how was your day?'')', pg_temp.key('p' || i), x))
              #>> '{message,id}')::uuid;
    PERFORM pg_temp.claim_only(v_msg);
    PERFORM public.complete_dating_safety_job(v_msg, 'dating_chat_v1', CASE WHEN i <= 2 THEN
      '[{"rule_id": "financial_request:gift card", "family": "financial_request", "tier": 2},
        {"rule_id": "sexual_pressure:send nudes", "family": "sexual_pressure", "tier": 1}]'::jsonb ELSE '[]'::jsonb END);
    IF i = 2 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_moderation_cases WHERE target_user_id = s AND signal_kind = 'duplicate_opener') THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the same opener in two matches opened a case';
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
  IF (SELECT array_agg(c.signal_kind || ':' || c.priority ORDER BY c.signal_kind) FROM public.dating_moderation_cases AS c
       WHERE c.target_user_id = s AND c.signal_kind IN ('money_asks', 'sexual_pressure', 'duplicate_opener'))
     IS DISTINCT FROM ARRAY['duplicate_opener:P2', 'money_asks:P1', 'sexual_pressure:P2']
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_account_restrictions WHERE user_id = s) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the repeated signals are % (or a restriction followed)',
      (SELECT array_agg(c.signal_kind || ':' || c.priority ORDER BY c.signal_kind) FROM public.dating_moderation_cases AS c WHERE c.target_user_id = s);
  END IF;
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_opener_hashes WHERE hash !~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$')
     OR (SELECT count(*) FROM public.dating_opener_hashes WHERE sender_user_id = s) <> 3 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the openers are not kept as one keyed hash per match';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- Q. (§6.1 lexicon, §4.3; W5 final M-7) The config: the v1 lexicon is the
--    reviewed file, every term in Spec 1's match form (age_signal in its
--    base form), seeded as a draft; one active version at a time; an
--    active version can't be edited; its hash is its config's; in
--    production a DEV- reference can't activate it.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  v_lex constant jsonb := current_setting('w6.lexicon')::jsonb;
  v_bad text;
BEGIN
  IF (SELECT config FROM public.dating_safety_configs WHERE version = 'dating_chat_v1') IS DISTINCT FROM v_lex THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the seeded lexicon is not supabase/functions/_shared/dating_chat_lexicon_v1.json';
  END IF;
  SELECT string_agg(f.key || ': ' || t.term, ', ') INTO v_bad
    FROM jsonb_each(v_lex -> 'families') AS f,
         LATERAL jsonb_array_elements_text((f.value -> 'terms') || coalesce(f.value -> 'new_match_terms', '[]')) AS t(term)
   WHERE t.term IS DISTINCT FROM CASE WHEN f.value ->> 'form' = 'base' THEN private.normalize_for_filter(t.term)
                                      ELSE private.match_form(t.term) END;
  IF v_bad IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: lexicon terms not in their stored form: %', v_bad;
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.probe(ARRAY['UPDATE public.dating_safety_configs SET state = ''retired'' WHERE state = ''active''',
                         'INSERT INTO public.dating_safety_configs (version, state, config, config_hash, review_ref, activated_at)
                          SELECT ''w6_a'', ''active'', c.config, c.config_hash, ''DEV-safety'', now() FROM public.dating_safety_configs AS c WHERE c.version = ''dating_chat_v1'''],
       'SELECT count(*)::text FROM public.dating_safety_configs WHERE state = ''active''') <> '1' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: activating a config did not leave exactly one active';
  END IF;
  BEGIN
    UPDATE public.dating_safety_configs SET state = 'retired' WHERE state = 'active';
    UPDATE public.dating_safety_configs SET state = 'active', review_ref = 'DEV-safety', activated_at = now() WHERE version = 'dating_chat_v1';
    UPDATE public.dating_safety_configs
       SET config = config || '{"x": 1}', config_hash = encode(extensions.digest((config || '{"x": 1}')::text, 'sha256'), 'hex')
     WHERE version = 'dating_chat_v1';
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an active config was edited';
  EXCEPTION WHEN check_violation THEN
    IF SQLERRM IS DISTINCT FROM 'config:active_frozen' THEN
      RAISE;
    END IF;
  END;
  BEGIN
    PERFORM set_config('app.settings.attune_environment', 'production', true);
    UPDATE public.dating_safety_configs SET state = 'retired' WHERE state = 'active';
    UPDATE public.dating_safety_configs SET state = 'active', review_ref = ' dev-safety', activated_at = now() WHERE version = 'dating_chat_v1';
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: production activated a config with a DEV- reference';
  EXCEPTION WHEN check_violation THEN
    PERFORM set_config('app.settings.attune_environment', '', true);
    IF SQLERRM IS DISTINCT FROM 'config:dev_review_ref' THEN
      RAISE;
    END IF;
  END;
  BEGIN
    INSERT INTO public.dating_safety_configs (version, config, config_hash) VALUES ('w6_bad', '{}', 'nope');
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a config was stored with a hash that is not its own';
  EXCEPTION WHEN check_violation THEN
    IF SQLERRM IS DISTINCT FROM 'config:hash_mismatch' THEN
      RAISE;
    END IF;
  END;
END $$;

-- @@W6C@@
```

Create `$D/mig_T5_head.sql` with exactly:

```sql
-- supabase/migrations/20261120110000_dating_v2_chat_safety.sql
-- Spec 2 W6b (§4.9 W6b, §6.1, §5.10, §3.13, §4.6, §7.1; chat spec §3, §4.1,
-- §6, §9, §10; carry 8): the dating chat's own safety pipeline. Each
-- message is queued (its own outbox, never the couples one); the worker
-- process-dating-safety-outbox claims it, runs the deterministic engine
-- (_shared/dating_chat_safety.ts on Spec 1's normaliser) against the active
-- dating_safety_configs row and completes it with the rules that fired;
-- events go to the recipient only (never message text); tier 2 and 3
-- families open a moderation case about the sender; repeated money asks,
-- repeated sexual pressure and a duplicate opener open signal cases (never
-- an automatic ban). Delivery never waits on any of it (fail-open); a
-- config that can't be read means no detection, never the couples config.
-- Message pushes: neutral me_update, at most one per 15 minutes. The
-- lexicon v1 is seeded as a draft (the dev script activates it); its
-- threat, sexual-pressure, harassment and identity sets are [DRAFT] until
-- the cultural reviewer's. No flag changes. Grants:
-- 20261120110010_dating_v2_chat_safety_grants.sql.
BEGIN;
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';
SET LOCAL client_min_messages = warning;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1. Tables.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.dating_message_safety_outbox (
  message_id uuid PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES public.dating_messages (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  state text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending'
    CONSTRAINT dating_message_safety_outbox_state_check CHECK (state IN ('pending', 'processing', 'done', 'dead_letter')),
  attempts smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  last_error_code text CONSTRAINT dating_message_safety_outbox_error_check CHECK (last_error_code IS NULL OR last_error_code ~ '^[a-z0-9_]{1,40}$'),
  processing_started_at timestamptz,
  completed_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT clock_timestamp()
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS dating_message_safety_outbox_pending_idx
  ON public.dating_message_safety_outbox (created_at) WHERE state = 'pending';
ALTER TABLE public.dating_message_safety_outbox ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.dating_safety_configs (
  version text PRIMARY KEY CONSTRAINT dating_safety_configs_version_check CHECK (version ~ '^[a-z0-9_]{1,40}$'),
  state text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft'
    CONSTRAINT dating_safety_configs_state_check CHECK (state IN ('draft', 'active', 'retired')),
  config jsonb NOT NULL,
  config_hash text NOT NULL,
  review_ref text,
  activated_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT dating_safety_configs_active_check CHECK (state <> 'active' OR (review_ref IS NOT NULL AND activated_at IS NOT NULL))
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS dating_safety_configs_one_active_idx ON public.dating_safety_configs ((true)) WHERE state = 'active';
ALTER TABLE public.dating_safety_configs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.dating_safety_events (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  match_id uuid REFERENCES public.dating_matches (id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  message_id uuid REFERENCES public.dating_messages (id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  at_risk_user_id uuid REFERENCES public.users (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  sender_user_id uuid REFERENCES public.users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  rule_id text NOT NULL CONSTRAINT dating_safety_events_rule_check CHECK (char_length(rule_id) BETWEEN 1 AND 120),
  family text NOT NULL CONSTRAINT dating_safety_events_family_check CHECK (family IN (
    'sexual_pressure', 'harassment_abuse', 'contact_migration_pressure', 'meet_private_pressure', 'threat_intimidation',
    'financial_request', 'sextortion', 'child_access', 'age_signal', 'identity_inconsistency')),
  tier smallint NOT NULL CONSTRAINT dating_safety_events_tier_check CHECK (tier BETWEEN 1 AND 3),
  config_version text NOT NULL,
  source_event_key text NOT NULL CONSTRAINT dating_safety_events_source_key UNIQUE,
  dismissed_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS dating_safety_events_match_idx ON public.dating_safety_events (match_id, at_risk_user_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS dating_safety_events_sender_idx ON public.dating_safety_events (sender_user_id, family, created_at);
ALTER TABLE public.dating_safety_events ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.dating_opener_hashes (
  sender_user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.users (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  match_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.dating_matches (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  hash text NOT NULL CONSTRAINT dating_opener_hashes_hash_check CHECK (hash ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  PRIMARY KEY (sender_user_id, match_id)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS dating_opener_hashes_hash_idx ON public.dating_opener_hashes (sender_user_id, hash, created_at);
ALTER TABLE public.dating_opener_hashes ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- The chat's tier 2 and 3 families open their own signal kinds (§6.1).
ALTER TABLE public.dating_moderation_cases DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS dating_moderation_cases_signal_kind_check;
ALTER TABLE public.dating_moderation_cases ADD CONSTRAINT dating_moderation_cases_signal_kind_check CHECK (
  signal_kind IS NULL OR signal_kind IN ('dragnet', 'duplicate_opener', 'money_asks', 'sexual_pressure', 'location_mismatch',
    'device_accounts', 'chat_threat_intimidation', 'chat_financial_request', 'chat_sextortion', 'chat_child_access',
    'chat_age_signal', 'chat_identity_inconsistency'));

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2. The config: one active version; a version is never edited while
--    active (a change is a new version, W5 final M-7); in production a
--    DEV- review reference can't activate (as introductions_v2).
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_safety_config_guard()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
BEGIN
  IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND OLD.state = 'active'
     AND (NEW.config IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.config OR NEW.config_hash IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.config_hash
          OR NEW.version IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.version) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'config:active_frozen' USING ERRCODE = '23514';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.config_hash IS DISTINCT FROM pg_catalog.encode(extensions.digest(NEW.config::text, 'sha256'), 'hex') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'config:hash_mismatch' USING ERRCODE = '23514';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.state = 'active' AND private.attune_environment() IS NOT DISTINCT FROM 'production'
     AND pg_catalog.upper(pg_catalog.btrim(coalesce(NEW.review_ref, ''), E' \t\r\n')) LIKE 'DEV-%' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'config:dev_review_ref' USING ERRCODE = '23514';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS dating_safety_config_guard ON public.dating_safety_configs;
CREATE TRIGGER dating_safety_config_guard BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON public.dating_safety_configs
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION private.dating_safety_config_guard();

INSERT INTO public.dating_safety_configs (version, state, config, config_hash)
SELECT 'dating_chat_v1', 'draft', c.config, pg_catalog.encode(extensions.digest(c.config::text, 'sha256'), 'hex')
  FROM (SELECT $lex${"version": "dating_chat_v1", "new_match_days": 14, "families": {"financial_request": {"tier": 2, "form": "match", "terms": ["mobile money", "momo number", "send me momo", "credit
```
