```sql
-- The active config, for the worker (the service role's): NULL when none
-- is active (no detection; never the couples config).
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_dating_safety_config()
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('version', c.version, 'config', c.config)
    FROM public.dating_safety_configs AS c WHERE c.state = 'active';
$$;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3. The worker's three calls (the service role's).
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Claims up to [p_limit] (1-100) pending messages: each moves to
-- processing with one more attempt. The only way a body leaves the
-- database (S-5); the match's age says whether its first 14 days apply.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.claim_dating_safety_jobs(p_limit integer DEFAULT 50)
RETURNS TABLE (message_id uuid, body text, new_match boolean)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
BEGIN
  RETURN QUERY
  WITH picked AS (
    SELECT o.message_id FROM public.dating_message_safety_outbox AS o
     WHERE o.state = 'pending'
     ORDER BY o.created_at
     LIMIT least(greatest(coalesce(p_limit, 50), 1), 100)
     FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
  ), claimed AS (
    UPDATE public.dating_message_safety_outbox AS o
       SET state = 'processing', attempts = o.attempts + 1, processing_started_at = clock_timestamp()
      FROM picked WHERE o.message_id = picked.message_id
    RETURNING o.message_id
  )
  SELECT d.id, d.body, d.created_at < m.matched_at + make_interval(days => 14)
    FROM claimed JOIN public.dating_messages AS d ON d.id = claimed.message_id
    JOIN public.dating_matches AS m ON m.id = d.match_id;
END;
$$;

-- Opens (or keeps) the sender's open case of [p_signal] about this match.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_open_chat_case(p_sender uuid, p_match uuid, p_signal text, p_priority text)
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_opened boolean;
BEGIN
  v_opened := private.dating_open_signal_case(p_sender, p_signal, p_priority);
  IF v_opened THEN
    UPDATE public.dating_moderation_cases AS c SET match_id = p_match
     WHERE c.target_user_id = p_sender AND c.kind = 'signal' AND c.signal_kind = p_signal
       AND c.state IN ('open', 'in_review') AND c.match_id IS NULL;
  END IF;
  RETURN v_opened;
END;
$$;

-- Completes a claimed message. With [p_error] (a code), it goes back to
-- pending, or to dead_letter after its 5th attempt (counted). Otherwise
-- each hit ({rule_id, family, tier}) becomes an event for the recipient
-- only (once per message and rule; never the text); tier 2 and 3 open a
-- case about the sender (§6.1); then the sender's repeated signals
-- (§5.10, carry 8). [p_version] NULL: no config was active (no detection,
-- counted). Never raises for a message that is gone (purged meanwhile).
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.complete_dating_safety_job(p_message_id uuid, p_version text, p_hits jsonb,
                                                            p_error text DEFAULT NULL)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_d public.dating_messages;
  v_m public.dating_matches;
  v_to uuid;
  v_from uuid;
  v_attempts smallint;
  h jsonb;
  v_family text;
  v_tier integer;
  v_events integer := 0;
  v_hash text;
BEGIN
  IF p_message_id IS NULL THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'invalid');
  END IF;
  SELECT o.attempts INTO v_attempts FROM public.dating_message_safety_outbox AS o
   WHERE o.message_id = p_message_id AND o.state = 'processing' FOR UPDATE;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'not_claimed');
  END IF;
  IF p_error IS NOT NULL THEN
    UPDATE public.dating_message_safety_outbox AS o
       SET state = CASE WHEN v_attempts >= 5 THEN 'dead_letter' ELSE 'pending' END,
           last_error_code = CASE WHEN p_error ~ '^[a-z0-9_]{1,40}$' THEN p_error ELSE 'worker_error' END,
           processing_started_at = NULL
     WHERE o.message_id = p_message_id;
    IF v_attempts >= 5 THEN
      PERFORM private.bump_metric_internal('dating.safety.dead_letter');
    END IF;
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'state', CASE WHEN v_attempts >= 5 THEN 'dead_letter' ELSE 'pending' END);
  END IF;
  IF jsonb_typeof(coalesce(p_hits, '[]'::jsonb)) <> 'array'
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM jsonb_array_elements(coalesce(p_hits, '[]'::jsonb)) AS x
                 WHERE jsonb_typeof(x) <> 'object' OR x ->> 'family' NOT IN ('sexual_pressure', 'harassment_abuse',
                         'contact_migration_pressure', 'meet_private_pressure', 'threat_intimidation', 'financial_request',
                         'sextortion', 'child_access', 'age_signal', 'identity_inconsistency')
                    OR coalesce(x ->> 'tier', '') NOT IN ('1', '2', '3')
                    OR coalesce(pg_catalog.char_length(x ->> 'rule_id'), 0) NOT BETWEEN 1 AND 120)
     OR (p_version IS NULL AND jsonb_array_length(coalesce(p_hits, '[]'::jsonb)) > 0) THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'invalid');
  END IF;
  SELECT * INTO v_d FROM public.dating_messages AS d WHERE d.id = p_message_id;
  SELECT * INTO v_m FROM public.dating_matches AS m WHERE m.id = v_d.match_id;
  v_from := v_d.sender_user_id;
  v_to := CASE WHEN v_d.sender_side = 'low' THEN v_m.user_high_id ELSE v_m.user_low_id END;
  IF p_version IS NULL THEN
    PERFORM private.bump_metric_internal('dating.safety.no_config');
  END IF;
  FOR h IN SELECT x FROM jsonb_array_elements(coalesce(p_hits, '[]'::jsonb)) AS x LOOP
    v_family := h ->> 'family';
    v_tier := (h ->> 'tier')::integer;
    CONTINUE WHEN v_to IS NULL;
    INSERT INTO public.dating_safety_events (match_id, message_id, at_risk_user_id, sender_user_id, rule_id, family, tier,
                                             config_version, source_event_key)
    VALUES (v_d.match_id, p_message_id, v_to, v_from, h ->> 'rule_id', v_family, v_tier, p_version,
            p_message_id::text || ':' || (h ->> 'rule_id'))
    ON CONFLICT (source_event_key) DO NOTHING;
    IF FOUND THEN
      v_events := v_events + 1;
      IF v_tier <= 2 THEN
        PERFORM private.bump_metric_internal('dating.card.' || v_family);
      END IF;
      IF v_tier >= 2 AND v_from IS NOT NULL THEN
        PERFORM private.dating_open_chat_case(v_from, v_d.match_id, 'chat_' || v_family,
          CASE WHEN v_family = 'identity_inconsistency' THEN 'P2' ELSE 'P1' END);
      END IF;
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
  -- §5.10 (carry 8): the sender's repeated patterns, each a signal case.
  IF v_from IS NOT NULL THEN
    IF (SELECT count(DISTINCT e.at_risk_user_id) FROM public.dating_safety_events AS e
         WHERE e.sender_user_id = v_from AND e.family = 'financial_request' AND e.created_at > now() - interval '7 days') >= 2 THEN
      PERFORM private.dating_open_chat_case(v_from, v_d.match_id, 'money_asks', 'P1');
    END IF;
    IF (SELECT count(DISTINCT e.at_risk_user_id) FROM public.dating_safety_events AS e
         WHERE e.sender_user_id = v_from AND e.family = 'sexual_pressure' AND e.created_at > now() - interval '7 days') >= 2 THEN
      PERFORM private.dating_open_chat_case(v_from, v_d.match_id, 'sexual_pressure', 'P2');
    END IF;
    SELECT k.hash INTO v_hash FROM public.dating_opener_hashes AS k WHERE k.sender_user_id = v_from AND k.match_id = v_d.match_id;
    IF v_hash IS NOT NULL
       AND (SELECT count(DISTINCT k.match_id) FROM public.dating_opener_hashes AS k
             WHERE k.sender_user_id = v_from AND k.hash = v_hash AND k.created_at > now() - interval '7 days') >= 3 THEN
      PERFORM private.dating_open_chat_case(v_from, v_d.match_id, 'duplicate_opener', 'P2');
    END IF;
  END IF;
  UPDATE public.dating_message_safety_outbox AS o
     SET state = 'done', completed_at = clock_timestamp(), processing_started_at = NULL, last_error_code = NULL
   WHERE o.message_id = p_message_id;
  RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'state', 'done', 'events', v_events);
END;
$$;

-- §4.6 recover-stale-dating-workers: a message held in processing for 10
-- minutes (a worker that died) goes back to pending.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.recover_stale_dating_safety_jobs()
RETURNS integer
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_n integer;
BEGIN
  UPDATE public.dating_message_safety_outbox AS o
     SET state = 'pending', processing_started_at = NULL
   WHERE o.state = 'processing' AND o.processing_started_at < now() - interval '10 minutes';
  GET DIAGNOSTICS v_n = ROW_COUNT;
  RETURN v_n;
END;
$$;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4. The recipient's cards (§3.6 Safety cards, §6.1): the caller's own
--    tier 1 and 2 events in their match, not dismissed, each with the
--    resource its copy names from S1 W7a's support_resources for the
--    caller's Dating country (emergency first; sextortion); dismissing
--    hides one card and never changes detection.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_card_resource(p_country text, p_family text)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('name', r.name, 'phone', r.phone, 'sms', r.sms, 'url', r.url)
    FROM public.support_resources AS r
   WHERE r.active AND r.country_code IN (p_country, 'XX')
     AND r.category = CASE p_family WHEN 'sextortion' THEN 'sextortion' ELSE 'emergency' END
   ORDER BY (r.country_code = 'XX'), r.sort_order, r.name, r.id
   LIMIT 1;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_my_dating_safety_cards(p_match_id uuid)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
VOLATILE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_user constant uuid := auth.uid();
  v_country text;
BEGIN
  IF v_user IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'not_authenticated' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;
  IF p_match_id IS NULL THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'invalid');
  END IF;
  IF public.dating_require_trusted_device() IS NOT TRUE THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'reverify_device');
  END IF;
  SELECT p.country_code INTO v_country FROM public.dating_profiles AS p WHERE p.user_id = v_user;
  RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'cards', coalesce((
    SELECT pg_catalog.jsonb_agg(pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
             'id', e.id, 'message_id', e.message_id, 'family', e.family,
             'resource', CASE WHEN e.family IN ('threat_intimidation', 'sextortion')
                              THEN private.dating_card_resource(v_country, e.family) END)
             ORDER BY e.created_at, e.id)
      FROM public.dating_safety_events AS e
     WHERE e.match_id = p_match_id AND e.at_risk_user_id = v_user AND e.tier <= 2 AND e.dismissed_at IS NULL
       AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_matches AS m WHERE m.id = e.match_id AND v_user IN (m.user_low_id, m.user_high_id))),
    '[]'::jsonb));
END;
$$;

-- Never device-gated (it only hides a card); one answer for any id.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.dismiss_dating_safety_card(p_event_id uuid)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
BEGIN
  IF auth.uid() IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'not_authenticated' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;
  UPDATE public.dating_safety_events AS e SET dismissed_at = coalesce(e.dismissed_at, now())
   WHERE e.id = p_event_id AND e.at_risk_user_id = auth.uid();
  RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
END;
$$;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 5. A message's push (§3.13): the neutral me_update, at most one per 15
--    minutes per person (the source key is keyed by the quarter hour),
--    only with "messages" on (dating_settings.chat_push, on by default)
--    and never while the person reads the chat (seen after the message).
--    Quiet hours fold it through the push queue. Never raises: a push
--    that can't be queued is counted and the message is delivered.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_queue_chat_push(p_user uuid)
RETURNS void
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
BEGIN
  IF p_user IS NULL OR coalesce((SELECT s.chat_push FROM public.dating_settings AS s WHERE s.user_id = p_user), true) IS NOT TRUE THEN
    RETURN;
  END IF;
  PERFORM private.dating_queue_me_update(p_user,
    'chat:' || pg_catalog.floor(extract(epoch FROM now()) / 900)::bigint::text, 'new');
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
  PERFORM private.bump_metric_internal('dating.chat.push_failed');
END;
$$;

-- §5.10: the sender's opener, keyed (the exclusion key over the match
-- form), never the words; the first message per side only.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_opener_hash(p_body text)
RETURNS text
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_key constant text := public.app_setting('dating_exclusion_key');
BEGIN
  IF coalesce(v_key, '') = '' OR p_body IS NULL THEN
    RETURN NULL;
  END IF;
  RETURN pg_catalog.encode(extensions.hmac('dating_opener|' || private.match_form(p_body), v_key, 'sha256'), 'hex');
END;
$$;

-- The crons (§4.6): the worker every minute, the stale-job recovery every
-- five.
DO $$
BEGIN
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_catalog.pg_namespace WHERE nspname = 'cron') THEN
    PERFORM cron.unschedule(j.jobid) FROM cron.job AS j
     WHERE j.jobname IN ('process-dating-safety-outbox', 'recover-stale-dating-workers');
    PERFORM cron.schedule('process-dating-safety-outbox', '* * * * *',
                          $c$SELECT public.invoke_edge_function('process-dating-safety-outbox')$c$);
    PERFORM cron.schedule('recover-stale-dating-workers', '*/5 * * * *',
                          $c$SELECT public.recover_stale_dating_safety_jobs()$c$);
  END IF;
END $$;
```

Create `$D/build_mig_T5.py` with exactly:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Spec 2 W6b: builds supabase/migrations/20261120110000_dating_v2_chat_safety.sql
# from mig_T5_head.sql, the functions below re-created from their LIVE
# bodies (saved to before_w6b/) with only the lines marked "-- Spec 2 W6b"
# changed, and COMMIT. Every anchor must occur exactly once.
import pathlib

D = pathlib.Path('.superpowers/sdd/2026-11-20-spec2-w6')
BEFORE = D / 'before_w6b'

EDITS = {
  # 1. The send queues the message for the worker (delivery never waits on
  #    it), keeps the side's first message as a keyed opener, and queues
  #    the recipient's neutral push.
  'public.send_dating_message(text,uuid,text,text,text)': [
    ("""      PERFORM private.dating_bump_chat(v_other, p_match_id);""",
     """      PERFORM private.dating_bump_chat(v_other, p_match_id);
      INSERT INTO public.dating_message_safety_outbox (message_id) VALUES (v_msg.id); -- Spec 2 W6b (§6.1)
      IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_opener_hashes AS k -- Spec 2 W6b (§5.10): the side's first message
                      WHERE k.sender_user_id = v_user AND k.match_id = p_match_id) -- Spec 2 W6b
         AND private.dating_opener_hash(p_body) IS NOT NULL THEN -- Spec 2 W6b
        INSERT INTO public.dating_opener_hashes (sender_user_id, match_id, hash) -- Spec 2 W6b
        VALUES (v_user, p_match_id, private.dating_opener_hash(p_body)) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING; -- Spec 2 W6b
      END IF; -- Spec 2 W6b
      PERFORM private.dating_queue_chat_push(v_other); -- Spec 2 W6b (§3.13)"""),
  ],
  # 2. Health: the chat's outbox and counters (chat spec §10).
  'public.get_dating_operational_health()': [
    ("""                        'dating.digest.push_key_missing', 'dating.digest.failed']) AS k(key)), -- Spec 2 W5 final (M-5, M-3): a digest that sends nothing is seen""",
     """                        'dating.digest.push_key_missing', 'dating.digest.failed', -- Spec 2 W5 final (M-5, M-3); Spec 2 W6b: the list goes on
                        'dating.pair_marker.unwritten', 'dating.safety.no_config', 'dating.safety.dead_letter', -- Spec 2 W6b
                        'dating.chat.push_failed']) AS k(key)), -- Spec 2 W6b
    -- Spec 2 W6b (chat spec §10): the chat's safety outbox and its events.
    'chat_safety_outbox',(SELECT jsonb_build_object( -- Spec 2 W6b
                            'pending', count(*) FILTER (WHERE o.state = 'pending'), -- Spec 2 W6b
                            'processing', count(*) FILTER (WHERE o.state = 'processing'), -- Spec 2 W6b
                            'dead_letter', count(*) FILTER (WHERE o.state = 'dead_letter'), -- Spec 2 W6b
                            'oldest_pending_seconds', coalesce(floor(extract(epoch FROM now() - min(o.created_at) FILTER (WHERE o.state = 'pending'))), 0)) -- Spec 2 W6b
                          FROM public.dating_message_safety_outbox AS o), -- Spec 2 W6b
    'chat_safety_config_active',(SELECT c.version FROM public.dating_safety_configs AS c WHERE c.state = 'active'), -- Spec 2 W6b
    'chat_safety_events_24h',(SELECT coalesce(jsonb_object_agg(x.family, x.n), '{}'::jsonb) FROM ( -- Spec 2 W6b
                                SELECT e.family, count(*) AS n FROM public.dating_safety_events AS e -- Spec 2 W6b
                                 WHERE e.created_at >= now() - interval '24 hours' GROUP BY e.family) AS x), -- Spec 2 W6b"""),
  ],
}

out = [(D / 'mig_T5_head.sql').read_text().rstrip('\n'), '',
       '-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------',
       '-- 6. Functions re-created from their live bodies; only the lines marked',
       '--    "-- Spec 2 W6b" change.',
       '-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------']
for sig, pairs in EDITS.items():
    body = (BEFORE / f'{sig}.sql').read_text().rstrip('\n')
    for old, new in pairs:
        n = body.count(old)
        assert n == 1, f'{sig}: anchor occurs {n} times: {old[:80]!r}'
        body = body.replace(old, new)
    out += ['', body + ';']
out += ['', 'COMMIT;', '']
pathlib.Path('supabase/migrations/20261120110000_dating_v2_chat_safety.sql').write_text('\n'.join(out))
print(f'20261120110000_dating_v2_chat_safety.sql: {len(EDITS)} functions re-created')
```

Create `supabase/migrations/20261120110010_dating_v2_chat_safety_grants.sql` with exactly:

```sql
-- Spec 2 W6b: grants for 20261120110000_dating_v2_chat_safety.sql. The
-- outbox, configs, events and opener hashes are no API role's (the
-- service role included: the worker reaches them only through its three
-- functions, S-5). The worker's calls are the service role's alone; the
-- cards and their dismissal are the app's; the rest is the database's own.
BEGIN;
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';
REVOKE ALL ON TABLE public.dating_message_safety_outbox FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON TABLE public.dating_safety_configs FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON TABLE public.dating_safety_events FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON TABLE public.dating_opener_hashes FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_safety_config_guard() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
```

Create `supabase/functions/_shared/dating_chat_safety.test.ts` with exactly:

```typescript
// Spec 2 W6b (§6.1, §7.2 S1, S8, S9, S21, S22, §7.3; I-17): the engine on
// the v1 lexicon. Golden positives per family (English, Ghanaian and
// Nigerian Pidgin, Twi typed with ɛ/ɔ and with e/o, leetspeak, a
// zero-width split) and near-miss negatives; age_signal in the base form
// only; the first-14-days terms only in a new match; one rule per family;
// a malformed config is no detection; the module has no fold table of its
// own (Spec 1's normaliser is the one).
import { assertEquals } from "https://deno.land/std@0.224.0/assert/mod.ts";
import lexicon from "./dating_chat_lexicon_v1.json" with { type: "json" };
import { baseForm, matchForm } from "./unicode_normaliser.ts";
import { detect, FAMILIES, parseConfig } from "./dating_chat_safety.ts";

const config = parseConfig({ version: "dating_chat_v1", config: lexicon })!;
const fams = (text: string, newMatch = false): string[] => detect(text, config, { newMatch }).map((h) => h.family);

Deno.test("the v1 lexicon reads, every family, every term in its stored form", () => {
  if (!config) throw new Error("the v1 lexicon did not parse");
  assertEquals([...config.families.keys()].sort(), [...FAMILIES].sort());
  for (const [family, f] of Object.entries(lexicon.families)) {
    for (const t of [...f.terms, ...(f.new_match_terms ?? [])]) {
      assertEquals(f.form === "base" ? baseForm(t) : matchForm(t), t, `${family}: ${t}`);
    }
  }
});

Deno.test("golden positives, one per family", () => {
  const cases: [string, string, boolean][] = [
    ["Please send me momo for transport", "financial_request", false],
    ["s3nd m3 m0m0 now", "financial_request", false],
    ["sɛnd me momo abeg", "financial_request", false],
    ["send me mo​mo today", "financial_request", false],
    ["I sent it by mistake, send it back", "financial_request", false],
    ["Abeg send small, I go pay you", "financial_request", false],
    ["Fa sika brɛ me", "financial_request", true],
    ["Buy me an iTunes card", "financial_request", false],
    ["W.E.S.T.E.R.N U.N.I.O.N is fine", "financial_request", false],
    ["I have your video. Pay or I post it", "sextortion", false],
    ["add me on WhatsApp instead", "contact_migration_pressure", false],
    ["call me on Sunday", "contact_migration_pressure", true],
    ["come to my place tonight", "meet_private_pressure", false],
    ["How old are your kids?", "child_access", false],
    ["I'm 16 but mature", "age_signal", false],
    ["I know where you live", "threat_intimidation", false],
    ["send nudes", "sexual_pressure", false],
    ["you are so stupid", "harassment_abuse", false],
    ["I am a US soldier on a peacekeeping mission", "identity_inconsistency", false],
  ];
  for (const [text, family, newMatch] of cases) {
    if (!fams(text, newMatch).includes(family)) throw new Error(`${JSON.stringify(text)} did not fire ${family}: ${fams(text, newMatch)}`);
  }
});

Deno.test("near-miss negatives stay silent", () => {
  const negatives: [string, boolean][] = [
    ["let's split the trotro fare", true],
    ["I paid my school fees myself", false],
    ["momo is down today", false],
    ["call me on Sunday", false],
    ["I'm 160 cm tall", false],
    ["I'm 1 6 years from retiring", false],
    ["Shall we meet at the mall?", true],
    ["What does a good Saturday look like for you?", true],
  ];
  for (const [text, newMatch] of negatives) {
    assertEquals(fams(text, newMatch), [], JSON.stringify(text));
  }
});

Deno.test("age_signal reads the base form only: no leetspeak folding", () => {
  assertEquals(fams("I'm 16"), ["age_signal"]);
  // In the match form "16" is "i6" (leetspeak), so "I'm i6" would fire;
  // the base form keeps digits as digits.
  assertEquals(fams("I'm i6"), []);
  assertEquals(fams("I'm l6"), []);
});

Deno.test("one rule per family; the rule names its term", () => {
  const hits = detect("send me momo, gift card, western union", config, { newMatch: false });
  assertEquals(hits.length, 1);
  assertEquals(hits[0], { rule_id: "financial_request:send me momo", family: "financial_request", tier: 2 });
});

Deno.test("a malformed config is no detection", () => {
  for (const raw of [null, {}, { version: "x" }, { version: "BAD!", config: lexicon },
                     { version: "v", config: { families: { couples_control: { tier: 1, terms: ["x"] } } } },
                     { version: "v", config: { families: { sextortion: { tier: 4, terms: ["x"] } } } },
                     { version: "v", config: { families: { sextortion: { tier: 2, terms: [1] } } } }]) {
    assertEquals(parseConfig(raw), null, JSON.stringify(raw));
  }
});

Deno.test("I-17: the engine has no fold table of its own", async () => {
  const src = await Deno.readTextFile(new URL("./dating_chat_safety.ts", import.meta.url));
  if (!src.includes('from "./unicode_normaliser.ts"')) throw new Error("the engine does not use Spec 1's normaliser");
  for (const own of ["new Map([", "fold_table", "charMap", "CONFUSABLES", "WEST_AFRICAN", "normalize("]) {
    if (src.includes(own)) throw new Error(`the engine folds on its own: ${own}`);
  }
});
```

Create `supabase/functions/process-dating-safety-outbox/handler.test.ts` with exactly:

```typescript
// Spec 2 W6b (§6.1, §7.3; chat spec §9, §11): the worker. Each claimed
// message is completed with its hits; no config (missing, malformed or
// failing to load) is no detection, never another config; one message's
// failure is its own; an engine error goes back with a code; nothing it
// logs holds a message's text.
import { assertEquals } from "https://deno.land/std@0.224.0/assert/mod.ts";
import lexicon from "../_shared/dating_chat_lexicon_v1.json" with { type: "json" };
import type { Hit } from "../_shared/dating_chat_safety.ts";
import { type Deps, type Job, runOutbox } from "./handler.ts";

const jobs: Job[] = [
  { message_id: "m1", body: "send me momo, I have your video", new_match: false },
  { message_id: "m2", body: "What does a good Saturday look like for you?", new_match: true },
];

function deps(over: Partial<Deps> = {}, list: Job[] = jobs) {
  const done: [string, string | null, Hit[], string | null][] = [];
  const logs: string[] = [];
  const d: Deps = {
    claim: async () => list,
    config: async () => ({ version: "dating_chat_v1", config: lexicon }),
    complete: async (id, version, hits, error) => {
      done.push([id, version, hits, error]);
    },
    log: (line) => logs.push(line),
    ...over,
  };
  return { d, done, logs };
}

Deno.test("each claimed message is completed with the rules it trips", async () => {
  const { d, done } = deps();
  const r = await runOutbox(d);
  assertEquals(r, { claimed: 2, completed: 2, failed: 0, detection: true });
  assertEquals(done[0][0], "m1");
  assertEquals(done[0][1], "dating_chat_v1");
  assertEquals(done[0][2].map((h) => h.family), ["financial_request", "sextortion"]);
  assertEquals(done[1], ["m2", "dating_chat_v1", [], null]);
});

Deno.test("no config, a malformed one or one that fails to load: no detection", async () => {
  for (const config of [async () => null, async () => ({ version: "v", config: { families: { nope: {} } } }),
                        async () => { throw new Error("config_failed"); }]) {
    const { d, done } = deps({ config });
    const r = await runOutbox(d);
    assertEquals(r.detection, false);
    assertEquals(done.map((x) => [x[0], x[1], x[2].length, x[3]]), [["m1", null, 0, null], ["m2", null, 0, null]]);
  }
});

Deno.test("one message's failure is its own; an engine error goes back with a code", async () => {
  const { d, done } = deps({}, [{ message_id: "bad", body: null as unknown as string, new_match: false }, ...jobs]);
  const r = await runOutbox(d);
  assertEquals(done[0], ["bad", "dating_chat_v1", [], "engine_error"]);
  assertEquals(r, { claimed: 3, completed: 2, failed: 1, detection: true });
  let calls = 0;
  const flaky = deps({
    complete: async () => {
      calls++;
      if (calls === 1) throw new Error("complete_failed");
    },
  });
  assertEquals(await runOutbox(flaky.d), { claimed: 2, completed: 1, failed: 1, detection: true });
});

Deno.test("nothing logged holds a message's text", async () => {
  const { d, logs } = deps();
  const spy: string[] = [];
  const original = console.log;
  console.log = (...a: unknown[]) => spy.push(a.join(" "));
  try {
    await runOutbox(d);
  } finally {
    console.log = original;
  }
  for (const line of [...logs, ...spy]) {
    if (/momo|video|saturday/i.test(line)) throw new Error(`a log line holds message text: ${line}`);
  }
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the engine and the worker**

Create `supabase/functions/_shared/dating_chat_safety.ts` with exactly:

```typescript
// supabase/functions/_shared/dating_chat_safety.ts
//
// Spec 2 W6b (§6.1; chat spec §3, §9; I-17): the dating chat's
// deterministic engine. No LLM; no fold table of its own: every text and
// every lexicon term is read through Spec 1's ./unicode_normaliser.ts
// (baseForm, matchForm, squashedForm), so a Twi or Pidgin term matches the
// same way here as in a D10 prompt. The couples chat_safety.ts is not
// touched. The config is the active dating_safety_configs row (the v1
// lexicon is ./dating_chat_lexicon_v1.json): one entry per family, its
// tier, its form ("match", or "base" for age_signal, where leetspeak would
// turn "16" into letters), its terms, and the terms that apply only in a
// match's first 14 days (chat spec §3.3: sensitivity may only increase
// with unfamiliarity). A config that can't be read is no detection.
import { baseForm, matchForm, squashedForm } from "./unicode_normaliser.ts";

export const FAMILIES = [
  "sexual_pressure",
  "harassment_abuse",
  "contact_migration_pressure",
  "meet_private_pressure",
  "threat_intimidation",
  "financial_request",
  "sextortion",
  "child_access",
  "age_signal",
  "identity_inconsistency",
] as const;
export type Family = typeof FAMILIES[number];
export type Tier = 1 | 2 | 3;

export interface FamilyConfig {
  tier: Tier;
  form: "match" | "base";
  terms: string[];
  newMatchTerms: string[];
}

export interface DatingSafetyConfig {
  version: string;
  families: Map<Family, FamilyConfig>;
}

export interface Hit {
  rule_id: string;
  family: Family;
  tier: Tier;
}

/// A distinctive term (at least this many letters without spaces) also
/// matches inside the squashed text, which catches a term split by
/// separators ("m.o.m.o n.u.m.b.e.r").
export const SQUASH_MIN = 8;

function strings(raw: unknown): string[] | null {
  if (raw === undefined) return [];
  if (!Array.isArray(raw) || raw.some((t) => typeof t !== "string" || t.trim() === "")) return null;
  return raw as string[];
}

/// The config as the database sends it ({version, config}), or null when
/// any part of it is malformed: no detection, never a guess.
export function parseConfig(raw: unknown): DatingSafetyConfig | null {
  if (!raw || typeof raw !== "object") return null;
  const { version, config } = raw as { version?: unknown; config?: unknown };
  if (typeof version !== "string" || !/^[a-z0-9_]{1,40}$/.test(version)) return null;
  const families = (config as { families?: unknown } | null)?.families;
  if (!families || typeof families !== "object") return null;
  const out = new Map<Family, FamilyConfig>();
  for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(families as Record<string, unknown>)) {
    if (!(FAMILIES as readonly string[]).includes(name) || !value || typeof value !== "object") return null;
    const v = value as Record<string, unknown>;
    const terms = strings(v.terms);
    const newMatchTerms = strings(v.new_match_terms);
    const form = v.form === undefined ? "match" : v.form;
    if (![1, 2, 3].includes(v.tier as number) || terms === null || newMatchTerms === null || (form !== "match" && form !== "base")) {
      return null;
    }
    out.set(name as Family, { tier: v.tier as Tier, form, terms, newMatchTerms });
  }
  return { version, families: out };
}

/// The families [text] trips (at most one rule per family), in FAMILIES
/// order. [newMatch]: the message was sent in the match's first 14 days.
export function detect(text: string, config: DatingSafetyConfig, opts: { newMatch: boolean }): Hit[] {
  const match = ` ${matchForm(text)} `;
  const base = ` ${baseForm(text)} `;
  const squashed = squashedForm(text);
  const hits: Hit[] = [];
  for (const family of FAMILIES) {
    const f = config.families.get(family);
    if (!f) continue;
    const terms = opts.newMatch ? [...f.terms, ...f.newMatchTerms] : f.terms;
    const padded = f.form === "base" ? base : match;
    for (const raw of terms) {
      const term = f.form === "base" ? baseForm(raw) : matchForm(raw);
      if (term === "") continue;
      const bare = term.replaceAll(" ", "");
      if (padded.includes(` ${term} `) || (f.form === "match" && bare.length >= SQUASH_MIN && squashed.includes(bare))) {
        hits.push({ rule_id: `${family}:${term}`, family, tier: f.tier });
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  return hits;
}
```

Create `supabase/functions/process-dating-safety-outbox/handler.ts` with exactly:

```typescript
// supabase/functions/process-dating-safety-outbox/handler.ts
//
// Spec 2 W6b (§6.1, §4.6, §4.7; chat spec §1.2, §6, §9): the worker. Each
// run claims queued messages, runs the deterministic engine on each and
// completes it with the rules that fired; the database writes the
// recipient's events and the cases. Fail-open: delivery never waited on
// this. A config that can't be read is no detection (each message is
// completed with none, and the database counts it), never another config.
// One message's failure is its own: it goes back to the queue with a code
// (dead-lettered after its 5th attempt by the database) and the rest run.
// No message text, rule or person is ever logged.
import { detect, parseConfig, type Hit } from "../_shared/dating_chat_safety.ts";

export interface Job {
  message_id: string;
  body: string;
  new_match: boolean;
}

export interface Deps {
  claim(limit: number): Promise<Job[]>;
  config(): Promise<unknown>;
  complete(messageId: string, version: string | null, hits: Hit[], error: string | null): Promise<void>;
  log?(line: string): void;
}

export const BATCH = 50;

export interface RunResult {
  claimed: number;
  completed: number;
  failed: number;
  detection: boolean;
}

export async function runOutbox(deps: Deps, limit = BATCH): Promise<RunResult> {
  const jobs = await deps.claim(limit);
  let config = null;
  try {
    config = parseConfig(await deps.config());
  } catch {
    config = null;
  }
  let completed = 0;
  let failed = 0;
  for (const job of jobs) {
    let hits: Hit[] = [];
    let error: string | null = null;
    if (config) {
      try {
        hits = detect(job.body, config, { newMatch: job.new_match === true });
      } catch {
        error = "engine_error";
      }
    }
    try {
      await deps.complete(job.message_id, config?.version ?? null, error ? [] : hits, error);
      if (error) failed++;
      else completed++;
    } catch {
      failed++;
    }
  }
  deps.log?.(`process-dating-safety-outbox: ${jobs.length} claimed, ${completed} completed, ${failed} failed${config ? "" : ", no config"}`);
  return { claimed: jobs.length, completed, failed, detection: config !== null };
}
```

Create `supabase/functions/process-dating-safety-outbox/index.ts` with exactly:

```typescript
// supabase/functions/process-dating-safety-outbox/index.ts
// Spec 2 W6b: the wiring for handler.ts. Called every minute by the cron
// (public.invoke_edge_function) with the service role; each database call
// is one RPC, awaited on its own; errors answer fixed codes only.
import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.177.0/http/server.ts";
import { HttpError, jsonResponse, requireServiceRole, serviceRoleClient } from "../_shared/attune_auth.ts";
import { runOutbox } from "./handler.ts";

serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return jsonResponse({ ok: true });
  try {
    requireServiceRole(req);
    const supabase = serviceRoleClient();
    const result = await runOutbox({
      claim: async (limit) => {
        const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("claim_dating_safety_jobs", { p_limit: limit });
        if (error) throw new Error("claim_failed");
        return data ?? [];
      },
      config: async () => {
        const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("get_dating_safety_config");
        if (error) throw new Error("config_failed");
        return data;
      },
      complete: async (id, version, hits, err) => {
        const { error } = await supabase.rpc("complete_dating_safety_job", {
          p_message_id: id,
          p_version: version,
          p_hits: hits,
          p_error: err,
        });
        if (error) throw new Error("complete_failed");
      },
      log: (line) => console.log(line),
    });
    return jsonResponse({ ok: true, ...result });
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof HttpError) return jsonResponse({ ok: false, error: e.message }, e.status);
    return jsonResponse({ ok: false, error: "worker_failed" }, 500);
  }
});
```

```dart
// Spec 2 W6b (§3.6 Safety cards, §6.1, §3.15 Chat; chat spec §4.1): the
// recipient's cards. A card shows under the message that raised it, in the
// spec's words with the person's name ("this person" without one) and the
// country's number or resource (a plain line without one); Dismiss hides
// it and asks the server once; Block confirms, then blocks; it slides in
// at 150 ms and not at all under reduce motion.
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat_screen.dart';

import '../../../helpers/flow_harness.dart';
import '../../../helpers/tap_in_reach.dart';
import '../support/dating_chat_fakes.dart';
import '../support/dating_shell.dart';

Future<void> tap(WidgetTester t, Finder finder) async {
  await tapInReach(t, finder);
  await t.pump();
  await t.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 300));
}

const _threat = DatingSafetyCard(
  id: 'e1',
  messageId: 'd2',
  family: 'threat_intimidation',
  resource: DatingCardResource(name: 'Ghana emergency', phone: '112'),
);

void main() {
  final loc = AppLocalizationsEn();

  testWidgets('a card shows under the message that raised it, with the '
      'country\'s number', (t) async {
    final fake = FakeDatingChat()..cardList = const [_threat];
    await pumpDatingShell(
      t,
      initial: '/dating/chat/m1',
      overrides: datingChatOverrides(fake),
    );
    final line = loc.dating_safetyThreat('Ghana emergency (112)');
    expect(find.text(line), findsOneWidget);
    final card = t.getTopLeft(find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_card_e1')));
    final message = t.getBottomLeft(find.text('Call me on '));
    expect(card.dy, greaterThan(message.dy - 1));
    expect(find.text('Hi Ama!'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_card_block')), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_card_dismiss')), findsOneWidget);
  });

  test('each family in the spec\'s words; no name is "this person"; no '
      'resource, a plain line', () {
    DatingSafetyCard c(String family, [DatingCardResource? r]) =>
        DatingSafetyCard(id: 'x', messageId: 'm', family: family, resource: r);
    const sos = DatingCardResource(name: 'CSA 292');
    expect(
      datingCardText(loc, c('sexual_pressure'), 'Kofi'),
      loc.dating_safetySexualPressure('Kofi'),
    );
    expect(
      datingCardText(loc, c('harassment_abuse'), ''),
      loc.dating_safetyHarassment('this person'),
    );
    expect(
      datingCardText(loc, c('contact_migration_pressure'), 'Kofi'),
      loc.dating_safetyContactMigration,
    );
    expect(
      datingCardText(loc, c('meet_private_pressure'), 'Kofi'),
      loc.dating_safetyMeetPrivate,
    );
    expect(
      datingCardText(loc, c('threat_intimidation'), 'Kofi'),
      loc.dating_safetyThreatNoNumber,
    );
    expect(
      datingCardText(loc, c('financial_request'), 'Kofi'),
      loc.dating_safetyFinancial,
    );
    expect(
      datingCardText(loc, c('sextortion', sos), 'Kofi'),
      loc.dating_safetySextortion('CSA 292'),
    );
    expect(
      datingCardText(loc, c('sextortion'), 'Kofi'),
      loc.dating_safetySextortionNoResource,
    );
    expect(
      datingCardText(loc, c('child_access'), 'Kofi'),
      loc.dating_safetyChildAccess,
    );
    expect(
      DatingSafetyCard.listFromJson({
        'ok': true,
        'cards': [
          {'id': 'e2', 'message_id': 'd1', 'family': 'age_signal'},
          {
            'id': 'e3',
            'message_id': 'd1',
            'family': 'financial_request',
            'resource': null,
          },
        ],
      }).map((c) => c.id),
      ['e3'],
    );
  });

  testWidgets('Dismiss hides it and asks once; Block confirms, then blocks', (
    t,
  ) async {
    final fake = FakeDatingChat()..cardList = const [_threat];
    await pumpDatingShell(
      t,
      initial: '/dating/chat/m1',
      overrides: datingChatOverrides(fake),
    );
    await tap(t, find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_card_dismiss')));
    await t.pump();
    expect(find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_card_e1')), findsNothing);
    expect(fake.dismissed, ['e1']);

    final again = FakeDatingChat()..cardList = const [_threat];
    await pumpDatingShell(
      t,
      initial: '/dating/chat/m1',
      overrides: datingChatOverrides(again),
    );
    await tap(t, find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_card_block')));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_blockTitle('Kofi')), findsOneWidget);
    await tap(
      t,
      find.descendant(
        of: find.byType(BottomSheet),
        matching: find.text(loc.dating_cardBlock),
      ),
    );
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(again.blocks.length, 1);
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_blockDone('Kofi')), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('a new card slides in at 150 ms; not at all under reduce '
      'motion', (t) async {
    for (final reduce in [false, true]) {
      if (reduce) {
        t.platformDispatcher.accessibilityFeaturesTestValue =
            const FakeAccessibilityFeatures(disableAnimations: true);
      }
      final fake = FakeDatingChat();
      await pumpDatingShell(
        t,
        initial: '/dating/chat/m1',
        overrides: datingChatOverrides(fake),
      );
      fake.cardList = const [_threat];
      fake.signalEvents.add(null);
      await t.pump();
      await t.pump();
      await t.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 50));
      final size =
          t
              .widget<SizeTransition>(
                find.descendant(
                  of: find.byType(DatingSafetyCardView),
                  matching: find.byType(SizeTransition),
                ),
              )
              .sizeFactor
              .value;
      if (reduce) {
        expect(size, 1.0, reason: 'reduce motion');
      } else {
        expect(size, inExclusiveRange(0.0, 1.0));
      }
      await t.pumpAndSettle();
      t.platformDispatcher.clearAccessibilityFeaturesTestValue();
    }
    expect(kDatingCardSlide, const Duration(milliseconds: 150));
  });

  testWidgets('goldens and guidelines with a card', (t) async {
    final fake = FakeDatingChat()..cardList = const [_threat];
    await expectFlowGoldens(
      t,
      'dating_chat_card',
      () => DatingChatScreen(matchId: 'm1', now: () => datingChatT0),
      overrides: [...datingShellOverrides(), ...datingChatOverrides(fake)],
    );
    await expectFlowGuidelines(t);
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The card, and the chat that shows it**

Create `lib/features/dating/chat/safety_card.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 2 W6b (§3.6 Safety cards, §6.1; chat spec §4.1): a card for the
// person who received the message, under it, never for the sender. Its
// words describe the pattern, never the person; the threat card names the
// country's emergency number and the sextortion card its resource (S1 W7a's
// support_resources), or a plain line when there is none. Every card has
// Block and Dismiss (Report arrives with W7a's report sheet; Plan a meet
// with W6c's meeting kit). Dismissing never weakens later detection. It
// slides in at 150 ms, and not at all under reduce motion.
import 'package:attune/app/theme/design_tokens.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// §3.15 Chat: cards slide in at 150 ms.
const kDatingCardSlide = Duration(milliseconds: 150);

/// The card's words for [card] (a person with no name is "this person").
String datingCardText(
  AppLocalizations loc,
  DatingSafetyCard card,
  String name,
) {
  final n = name.isEmpty ? loc.dating_cardThisPerson : name;
  final resource = card.resource?.spoken;
  return switch (card.family) {
    'sexual_pressure' => loc.dating_safetySexualPressure(n),
    'harassment_abuse' => loc.dating_safetyHarassment(n),
    'contact_migration_pressure' => loc.dating_safetyContactMigration,
    'meet_private_pressure' => loc.dating_safetyMeetPrivate,
    'threat_intimidation' =>
      resource == null
          ? loc.dating_safetyThreatNoNumber
          : loc.dating_safetyThreat(resource),
    'financial_request' => loc.dating_safetyFinancial,
    'sextortion' =>
      resource == null
          ? loc.dating_safetySextortionNoResource
          : loc.dating_safetySextortion(resource),
    'child_access' => loc.dating_safetyChildAccess,
    _ => '',
  };
}

class DatingSafetyCardView extends StatefulWidget {
  const DatingSafetyCardView({
    super.key,
    required this.card,
    required this.name,
    required this.onBlock,
    required this.onDismiss,
    this.ended = false,
  });

  final DatingSafetyCard card;
  final String name;
  final VoidCallback onBlock;
  final VoidCallback onDismiss;

  /// The conversation has ended (Block is already done or can't help).
  final bool ended;

  @override
  State<DatingSafetyCardView> createState() => _DatingSafetyCardViewState();
}

class _DatingSafetyCardViewState extends State<DatingSafetyCardView>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late final AnimationController _slide = AnimationController(
    vsync: this,
    duration: kDatingCardSlide,
  );

  @override
  void didChangeDependencies() {
    super.didChangeDependencies();
    if (MediaQuery.of(context).disableAnimations) {
      _slide.value = 1;
    } else if (_slide.status == AnimationStatus.dismissed) {
      _slide.forward();
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _slide.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final text = datingCardText(loc, widget.card, widget.name);
    return SizeTransition(
      sizeFactor: CurvedAnimation(parent: _slide, curve: Curves.easeOut),
      axisAlignment: -1,
      child: Semantics(
        container: true,
        liveRegion: true,
        child: Container(
          margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: Spacing.xs),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: theme.colorScheme.secondaryContainer,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          ),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              Row(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Icon(
                    Icons.shield_outlined,
                    color: theme.colorScheme.onSecondaryContainer,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: Spacing.sm),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(
                      text,
                      style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                        color: theme.colorScheme.onSecondaryContainer,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
              Wrap(
                alignment: WrapAlignment.end,
                spacing: Spacing.sm,
                children: [
                  if (!widget.ended)
                    TextButton(
                      key: const ValueKey('dating_card_block'),
                      style: TextButton.styleFrom(
                        minimumSize: const Size(48, 48),
                        foregroundColor: theme.colorScheme.onSecondaryContainer,
                      ),
                      onPressed: widget.onBlock,
                      child: Text(loc.dating_cardBlock),
                    ),
                  TextButton(
                    key: const ValueKey('dating_card_dismiss'),
                    style: TextButton.styleFrom(
                      minimumSize: const Size(48, 48),
                      foregroundColor: theme.colorScheme.onSecondaryContainer,
                    ),
                    onPressed: widget.onDismiss,
                    child: Text(loc.dating_safetyDismiss),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

```python
/// Spec 2 W6b (§3.6 Safety cards, §6.1): the families a card can show (tier
/// 1 and 2; the server never sends a tier 3 one).
const datingCardFamilies = {
  'sexual_pressure',
  'harassment_abuse',
  'contact_migration_pressure',
  'meet_private_pressure',
  'threat_intimidation',
  'financial_request',
  'sextortion',
  'child_access',
};

/// A resource a card names (S1 W7a's support_resources).
class DatingCardResource {
  const DatingCardResource({required this.name, this.phone});

  final String name;
  final String? phone;

  /// What the copy says: the number to call when there is one.
  String get spoken => phone == null ? name : '$name ($phone)';
}

/// One safety card for this person, under the message that raised it.
class DatingSafetyCard {
  const DatingSafetyCard({
    required this.id,
    required this.messageId,
    required this.family,
    this.resource,
  });

  final String id;
  final String messageId;
  final String family;
  final DatingCardResource? resource;

  static DatingSafetyCard? fromJson(Object? raw) {
    if (raw is! Map) return null;
    final id = raw['id'];
    final message = raw['message_id'];
    final family = raw['family'];
    if (id is! String ||
        message is! String ||
        family is! String ||
        !datingCardFamilies.contains(family)) {
      return null;
    }
    final r = raw['resource'];
    return DatingSafetyCard(
      id: id,
      messageId: message,
      family: family,
      resource:
          r is Map && r['name'] is String
              ? DatingCardResource(
                name: r['name'] as String,
                phone: r['phone'] is String ? r['phone'] as String : null,
              )
              : null,
    );
  }

  /// The cards in an answer; none for a refusal or anything malformed.
  static List<DatingSafetyCard> listFromJson(Object? raw) {
    if (raw is! Map || raw['ok'] != true || raw['cards'] is! List) {
      return const [];
    }
    return [
      for (final c in (raw['cards'] as List).map(fromJson))
        if (c != null) c,
    ];
  }
}
"""),

  /// §3.6 Block: confirmed, then sent once (from the Safety sheet or a
  /// card).
  Future<void> _block(String name) async {
    final account = _account;
    if (!await showDatingBlockSheet(context, account: account, name: name)) {
      return;
    }
    if (!mounted || _account != account) return;
    final r = await _gateway.block(
      _keys.keyFor('block', widget.matchId),
      widget.matchId,
    );
    _keys.settle('block', reason: r.reason);
    if (!mounted || _account != account) return;
    setState(() {
      _actionReason = r.ok ? null : r.reason;
      if (r.ok) {
        _blockedName = name;
        _composer.clear();
        _outgoing.clear();
      }
    });
    unawaited(_read());
  }

  /// §3.6: Dismiss hides the card (detection never changes).
  void _dismiss(DatingSafetyCard card) {
    setState(
      () =>
          _cards = [
            for (final c in _cards)
              if (c.id != card.id) c,
          ],
    );
    unawaited(_gateway.dismissCard(card.id));
  }
```

```sql
-- =====================================================================
-- R. (§3.6 guided first conversation) The questions in order; an answer
--    is seen by the other only once both have answered (and never lights
--    the list's dot before); then both appear together and the next
--    question is current; either may skip a question for both; only the
--    current question, once per side; nobody else skips.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  a constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('72');
  b constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('73', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"]}');
  o constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('74');
  v_m uuid;
  v jsonb;
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (user_low_id, user_high_id, state) VALUES (least(a, b), greatest(a, b), 'active') RETURNING id INTO v_m;
  IF pg_temp.j(a, '72', format('SELECT public.get_dating_guided_state(%L)', v_m))
     IS DISTINCT FROM '{"ok": true, "answered": false, "revealed": 0, "question_id": "good_saturday"}'::jsonb THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the first question is not current';
  END IF;
  v := pg_temp.j(a, '72', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''Market, then football'', ''guided_answer'', ''good_saturday'')', pg_temp.key('g1'), v_m));
  IF v ->> 'ok' IS DISTINCT FROM 'true'
     OR pg_temp.j(b, '73', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_m)) -> 'messages' IS DISTINCT FROM '[]'::jsonb
     OR pg_temp.j(b, '73', 'SELECT public.get_my_dating_matches()') #> '{conversations,0}' ->> 'new' IS DISTINCT FROM 'false'
     OR pg_temp.j(b, '73', 'SELECT public.get_my_dating_matches()') #> '{conversations,0,last}' IS DISTINCT FROM 'null'::jsonb
     OR pg_temp.j(a, '72', format('SELECT public.get_dating_guided_state(%L)', v_m)) ->> 'answered' IS DISTINCT FROM 'true'
     OR pg_temp.j(b, '73', format('SELECT public.get_dating_guided_state(%L)', v_m)) ->> 'answered' IS DISTINCT FROM 'false' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an answer was seen before both answered (%)', v;
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.j(a, '72', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''again'', ''guided_answer'', ''good_saturday'')', pg_temp.key('g2'), v_m)) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'invalid'
     OR pg_temp.j(b, '73', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''early'', ''guided_answer'', ''proud_of'')', pg_temp.key('g3'), v_m)) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'invalid'
     OR pg_temp.j(b, '73', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''x'', ''guided_answer'', NULL)', pg_temp.key('g4'), v_m)) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'invalid' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a second answer, a later question or an unnamed one was taken';
  END IF;
  PERFORM pg_temp.j(b, '73', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''Church, then jollof'', ''guided_answer'', ''good_saturday'')', pg_temp.key('g5'), v_m));
  IF (SELECT array_agg(x ->> 'text' ORDER BY x ->> 'text') FROM jsonb_array_elements(pg_temp.j(a, '72', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_m)) -> 'messages') AS x)
     IS DISTINCT FROM ARRAY['Church, then jollof', 'Market, then football']
     OR pg_temp.j(b, '73', format('SELECT public.get_dating_guided_state(%L)', v_m))
        IS DISTINCT FROM '{"ok": true, "answered": false, "revealed": 1, "question_id": "food_every_week"}'::jsonb THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: both answers did not appear together, or the next question is not current';
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.j(o, '74', format('SELECT public.skip_dating_guided_question(%L, ''food_every_week'')', v_m)) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'match_unavailable'
     OR pg_temp.j(a, '72', format('SELECT public.skip_dating_guided_question(%L, ''proud_of'')', v_m)) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'invalid' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an outsider, or a question not current, was skipped';
  END IF;
  v := pg_temp.j(a, '72', format('SELECT public.skip_dating_guided_question(%L, ''food_every_week'')', v_m));
  IF v ->> 'question_id' IS DISTINCT FROM 'looking_forward'
     OR pg_temp.j(b, '73', format('SELECT public.get_dating_guided_state(%L)', v_m)) ->> 'question_id' IS DISTINCT FROM 'looking_forward' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a skip was not for both (%)', v;
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- S. (§3.6 We met and the timebox cards; §5.3) Day 7: once to each
--    person; day 21: once, only while neither said We met; We met counts
--    once and takes the chat out of the open pre-date chats.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  a constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('75');
  b constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('76', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"]}');
  v_m uuid;
  v_met bigint := pg_temp.metric('dating.we_met');
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (user_low_id, user_high_id, state, matched_at)
  VALUES (least(a, b), greatest(a, b), 'active', now() - interval '6 days') RETURNING id INTO v_m;
  IF pg_temp.j(a, '75', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_m)) #>> '{chat,timebox}' IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a timebox card before day 7';
  END IF;
  UPDATE public.dating_matches SET matched_at = now() - interval '8 days' WHERE id = v_m;
  PERFORM pg_temp.j(a, '75', format('SELECT public.dismiss_dating_timebox(%L, ''day7'')', v_m));
  IF pg_temp.j(a, '75', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_m)) #>> '{chat,timebox}' IS NOT NULL
     OR pg_temp.j(b, '76', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_m)) #>> '{chat,timebox}' IS DISTINCT FROM 'day7' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the day-7 card is not once to each person';
  END IF;
  UPDATE public.dating_matches SET matched_at = now() - interval '22 days' WHERE id = v_m;
  IF pg_temp.j(a, '75', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_m)) #>> '{chat,timebox}' IS DISTINCT FROM 'day21' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: no day-21 card while neither said We met';
  END IF;
  PERFORM pg_temp.j(b, '76', format('SELECT public.send_dating_message(%L, %L, ''hello'')', pg_temp.key('s1'), v_m));
  IF private.dating_open_chats(a, now()) <> 1 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a chat with a recent message is not an open chat';
  END IF;
  PERFORM pg_temp.j(b, '76', format('SELECT public.mark_dating_we_met(%L)', v_m));
  PERFORM pg_temp.j(b, '76', format('SELECT public.mark_dating_we_met(%L)', v_m));
  IF pg_temp.j(a, '75', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_m)) #>> '{chat,timebox}' IS NOT NULL
     OR pg_temp.j(a, '75', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_m)) #> '{chat,we_met}' IS DISTINCT FROM '{"mine": false, "theirs": true}'::jsonb
     OR private.dating_open_chats(a, now()) <> 0 OR pg_temp.metric('dating.we_met') <> v_met + 1 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: after We met the day-21 card showed, the chat stayed open, or We met counted twice';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- T. (§3.6 Close kindly) The reviewed message is sent as the last one,
--    the match closes for both (closed_kindly, the 30-day window), the
--    pair is never introduced again; nobody else can; a replay is ok.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  a constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('77');
  b constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('78', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"]}');
  o constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('79');
  v_m uuid;
  k constant text := pg_temp.key('kind');
  v jsonb;
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (user_low_id, user_high_id, state) VALUES (least(a, b), greatest(a, b), 'active') RETURNING id INTO v_m;
  IF pg_temp.j(o, '79', format('SELECT public.close_dating_match_kindly(%L, %L)', k, v_m)) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'match_unavailable' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an outsider closed the chat';
  END IF;
  v := pg_temp.j(a, '77', format('SELECT public.close_dating_match_kindly(%L, %L)', k, v_m));
  IF v IS DISTINCT FROM '{"ok": true}'::jsonb
     OR (SELECT state || '/' || closed_reason || '/' || (evidence_until > now() + interval '29 days')::text FROM public.dating_matches WHERE id = v_m)
        IS DISTINCT FROM 'closed/closed_kindly/true'
     OR NOT pg_temp.marked(a, b)
     OR pg_temp.j(b, '78', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_m)) #>> '{messages,0,text}'
        IS DISTINCT FROM 'Thank you for chatting with me. I don''t think we''re a match, and I wish you well.'
     OR pg_temp.j(b, '78', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_m)) #>> '{messages,0,kind}' IS DISTINCT FROM 'close_kindly'
     OR pg_temp.j(b, '78', format('SELECT public.get_dating_messages(%L)', v_m)) #>> '{chat,state}' IS DISTINCT FROM 'ended' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: Close kindly answered % (the message, the close, the marker)', v;
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.j(a, '77', format('SELECT public.close_dating_match_kindly(%L, %L)', k, v_m)) IS DISTINCT FROM '{"ok": true}'::jsonb
     OR pg_temp.j(b, '78', format('SELECT public.close_dating_match_kindly(%L, %L)', pg_temp.key('kind2'), v_m)) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'closed'
     OR (SELECT count(*) FROM public.dating_messages WHERE match_id = v_m) <> 1 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a replay sent again, or a closed chat closed again';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- U. (§3.6 Check on me, §3.13; R17) One neutral me_checkin push at the
--    chosen time (no place, no match, no name); a new one replaces the
--    last; cancel removes it; a time under 5 minutes or past 7 days is
--    refused; a check-in at 23:30 local is not folded by quiet hours,
--    while a me_update at the same time is.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  a constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('80');
  v_at timestamptz;
  v jsonb;
BEGIN
  UPDATE public.users SET timezone = 'Africa/Accra' WHERE id = a;
  v_at := (date_trunc('day', now() AT TIME ZONE 'Africa/Accra') + interval '23 hours 30 minutes') AT TIME ZONE 'Africa/Accra';
  IF v_at < now() + interval '5 minutes' THEN
    v_at := v_at + interval '1 day';
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.j(a, '80', format('SELECT public.schedule_dating_checkin(%L)', now() + interval '1 minute')) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'invalid'
     OR pg_temp.j(a, '80', format('SELECT public.schedule_dating_checkin(%L)', now() + interval '8 days')) ->> 'reason' IS DISTINCT FROM 'invalid' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a check-in too soon or too far was taken';
  END IF;
  PERFORM pg_temp.j(a, '80', format('SELECT public.schedule_dating_checkin(%L)', v_at - interval '1 hour'));
  v := pg_temp.j(a, '80', format('SELECT public.schedule_dating_checkin(%L)', v_at));
  IF v ->> 'ok' IS DISTINCT FROM 'true'
     OR (SELECT count(*) FROM public.scheduled_notifications WHERE user_id = a AND status = 'pending') <> 1
     OR (SELECT metadata FROM public.scheduled_notifications WHERE user_id = a AND status = 'pending')
        IS DISTINCT FROM '{"type": "me_checkin", "route": "me_checkin", "title": "Attune", "body": "A quick check-in from Attune."}'::jsonb
     OR (SELECT scheduled_for FROM public.scheduled_notifications WHERE user_id = a AND status = 'pending') IS DISTINCT FROM v_at THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the check-in is not one neutral push at its time (folded by quiet hours?) (%)', v;
  END IF;
  INSERT INTO public.scheduled_notifications (user_id, notification_type, scheduled_for, status, metadata)
  VALUES (a, 'immediate', v_at, 'pending', '{"type": "me_update", "route": "me", "title": "Attune", "body": "You have something new in Attune."}');
  IF (SELECT scheduled_for FROM public.scheduled_notifications WHERE user_id = a AND metadata ->> 'type' = 'me_update') = v_at THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the control me_update at 23:30 was not folded by quiet hours (the case proves nothing)';
  END IF;
  PERFORM pg_temp.j(a, '80', 'SELECT public.cancel_dating_checkin()');
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.scheduled_notifications WHERE user_id = a AND status = 'pending' AND metadata ->> 'type' = 'me_checkin') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: cancel left a check-in';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- V. (§4.8; carry 9) The purge: a closed match past its window goes with
--    its messages unless an open case holds it; an active one stays; a
--    resolved introduction keeps only its ids, pair, state and dates; pair
--    markers and moderation cases go at 2 years; openers at 30 days;
--    safety events lose their people at 90 days; one failing step never
--    stops the others (counted).
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  a constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('81');
  b constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('82', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"]}');
  c constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('83', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"]}');
  e constant uuid := pg_temp.ready('84', '{"identity": "man", "seeking": ["woman"]}');
  v_gone uuid;
  v_held uuid;
  v_live uuid;
  v_i uuid;
  v jsonb;
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (user_low_id, user_high_id, state, closed_reason, evidence_until)
  VALUES (least(a, b), greatest(a, b), 'unmatched', 'unmatched', now() - interval '1 day') RETURNING id INTO v_gone;
  INSERT INTO public.dating_messages (match_id, sender_side, sender_user_id, body, body_masked, mask_version, idempotency_key)
  VALUES (v_gone, 'low', least(a, b), 'old', 'old', 1, 'w6-purge-1');
  INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (user_low_id, user_high_id, state, closed_reason, evidence_until)
  VALUES (least(a, c), greatest(a, c), 'blocked', 'blocked', now() - interval '1 day') RETURNING id INTO v_held;
  INSERT INTO public.dating_moderation_cases (kind, priority, target_user_id, match_id, signal_kind)
  VALUES ('signal', 'P1', c, v_held, 'money_asks');
  INSERT INTO public.dating_matches (user_low_id, user_high_id, state) VALUES (least(a, e), greatest(a, e), 'active') RETURNING id INTO v_live;
  v_i := pg_temp.intro(b, e);
  UPDATE public.dating_introductions SET state = 'passed', low_action = 'passed', reasons = ARRAY['same_region'], rank_detail = '{"f": 1}',
         created_at = now() - interval '40 days', expires_at = now() - interval '33 days' WHERE id = v_i;
  INSERT INTO public.dating_pair_markers (pair_code, created_at, purge_after)
  VALUES (repeat('a', 64), now() - interval '2 years 1 day', now() - interval '1 day'), (repeat('b', 64), now(), now() + interval '2 years');
  INSERT INTO public.dating_moderation_cases (kind, priority, target_user_id, signal_kind, state, created_at)
  VALUES ('signal', 'P2', e, 'dragnet', 'decided', now() - interval '2 years 1 day');
  INSERT INTO public.dating_opener_hashes (sender_user_id, match_id, hash, created_at) VALUES (a, v_live, repeat('c', 64), now() - interval '31 days');
  INSERT INTO public.dating_safety_events (match_id, at_risk_user_id, sender_user_id, rule_id, family, tier, config_version, source_event_key, created_at)
  VALUES (v_live, a, e, 'financial_request:x', 'financial_request', 2, 'dating_chat_v1', 'w6-purge-event', now() - interval '91 days');
  v := public.purge_dating_retention();
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_matches WHERE id = v_gone) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_messages WHERE idempotency_key = 'w6-purge-1')
     OR NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_matches WHERE id = v_held) OR NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_matches WHERE id = v_live)
     OR (SELECT coalesce(low_action, '') || cardinality(reasons) || coalesce(rank_detail::text, '') || state FROM public.dating_introductions WHERE id = v_i)
        IS DISTINCT FROM '0passed'
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_pair_markers WHERE pair_code = repeat('a', 64))
     OR NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_pair_markers WHERE pair_code = repeat('b', 64))
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_moderation_cases WHERE target_user_id = e AND signal_kind = 'dragnet')
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_opener_hashes WHERE hash = repeat('c', 64))
     OR (SELECT coalesce(at_risk_user_id::text, '') || coalesce(sender_user_id::text, '') || coalesce(match_id::text, '') || family
           FROM public.dating_safety_events WHERE source_event_key = 'w6-purge-event') IS DISTINCT FROM 'financial_request' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the purge answered % and left the wrong rows', v;
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.probe(ARRAY['CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.w6_boom() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $f$ BEGIN RAISE EXCEPTION ''w6:boom''; END $f$',
                         'CREATE TRIGGER w6_boom BEFORE DELETE ON public.dating_opener_hashes FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION pg_temp.w6_boom()',
                         format('INSERT INTO public.dating_opener_hashes (sender_user_id, match_id, hash, created_at) VALUES (%L, %L, %L, now() - interval ''31 days'')',
                                a, v_live, repeat('e', 64)),
                         format('INSERT INTO public.dating_pair_markers (pair_code, purge_after) VALUES (%L, now() - interval ''1 day'')', repeat('d', 64)),
                         -- health is STABLE: it reads the counter in a statement after the purge's
                         'CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.w6_purge_then_health() RETURNS text LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $f$
                          DECLARE r jsonb; BEGIN
                            r := public.purge_dating_retention();
                            RETURN (r ->> ''opener_hashes'') || '':'' || (r ->> ''pair_markers'') || '':'' || pg_temp.metric(''dating.purge.failed.opener_hashes'')
                                   || '':'' || coalesce(public.get_dating_operational_health() -> ''purge_failed_7d'' ->> ''opener_hashes'', ''0'');
                          END $f$'],
       $p$SELECT pg_temp.w6_purge_then_health()$p$)
     IS DISTINCT FROM 'failed:P0001:1:' || (pg_temp.metric('dating.purge.failed.opener_hashes') + 1)
                      || ':' || (coalesce((public.get_dating_operational_health() -> 'purge_failed_7d' ->> 'opener_hashes')::bigint, 0) + 1) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: one failing purge step stopped the others, or was not counted (or health does not show it)';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- W. (§2.3; W5 final review I-1) W6c's read refreshes the phone's last
--    use as PostgREST calls it (VOLATILE).
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
BEGIN
  IF (SELECT p.provolatile FROM pg_proc AS p WHERE p.oid = 'public.get_dating_guided_state(uuid)'::regprocedure) <> 'v'
     OR (SELECT p.provolatile FROM pg_proc AS p WHERE p.oid = 'public.get_my_dating_safety_cards(uuid)'::regprocedure) <> 'v' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a device-checked chat read is not VOLATILE (PostgREST would run it read-only)';
  END IF;
END $$;
```
