Create `$D/mig_T8_head.sql` with exactly:

```sql
-- supabase/migrations/20261120120000_dating_v2_chat_guided.sql
-- Spec 2 W6c (§4.9 W6c, §3.6 guided conversation, timebox cards, We met,
-- meeting kit, Close kindly, §3.13 check-in, §4.6, §4.8, §5.3, §6.9; R17;
-- carry 9): the guided first conversation (nine reviewed questions in
-- order; an answer is seen once both have answered; either may skip a
-- question for both); "We met" (which also ends the chat's count as an
-- open pre-date chat, §5.3); the day-7 and day-21 cards, once each per
-- side; Close kindly (the reviewed message, then the match closes for
-- both, never to be introduced again); the check-in (one neutral
-- me_checkin push at the time the person chose, not folded by quiet hours:
-- scheduled_notifications_quiet_guard re-created from its live body with
-- me_checkin beside safety_resources, R17); and the daily retention purge
-- (§4.8: closed matches and their messages after the 30-day evidence
-- window unless an open case holds them, resolved introductions' details
-- after 30 days, moderation cases and pair markers at 2 years, the rest
-- per §4.8), each step its own error block counted by SQLSTATE, no lock
-- held across steps. No flag changes. Grants:
-- 20261120120010_dating_v2_chat_guided_grants.sql.
BEGIN;
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';
SET LOCAL client_min_messages = warning;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1. Columns and the guided questions.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
ALTER TABLE public.dating_matches
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS low_we_met_at timestamptz,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS high_we_met_at timestamptz,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS low_day7_at timestamptz,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS high_day7_at timestamptz,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS low_day21_at timestamptz,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS high_day21_at timestamptz;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.dating_guided_skips (
  match_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.dating_matches (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  question_id text NOT NULL CONSTRAINT dating_guided_skips_question_check CHECK (question_id ~ '^[a-z0-9_]{1,40}$'),
  skipped_by_side text NOT NULL CONSTRAINT dating_guided_skips_side_check CHECK (skipped_by_side IN ('low', 'high')),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  PRIMARY KEY (match_id, question_id)
);
ALTER TABLE public.dating_guided_skips ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- §3.6: the reviewed questions, in order (light, middle, deeper). The app
-- holds their words under the same ids.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_guided_questions()
RETURNS text[]
LANGUAGE sql
IMMUTABLE
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT ARRAY['good_saturday', 'food_every_week', 'looking_forward', 'proud_of', 'laugh_with', 'where_you_grew_up',
               'good_relationship', 'time_with_family', 'before_first_meeting'];
$$;

-- Whether [p_side] has answered [p_question] in [p_match].
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_guided_answered(p_match uuid, p_question text, p_side text)
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_messages AS d
                  WHERE d.match_id = p_match AND d.kind = 'guided_answer' AND d.question_id = p_question
                    AND d.sender_side = p_side);
$$;

-- The guided state as [p_side] sees it: the current question (the first
-- neither skipped nor answered by both; NULL once all are done), whether
-- this side answered it, and how many answers both have shown (the tip
-- follows the first). Never whether the other side answered.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_guided_state(p_match uuid, p_side text)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  q text;
  v_current text;
  v_revealed integer := 0;
BEGIN
  FOREACH q IN ARRAY private.dating_guided_questions() LOOP
    IF private.dating_guided_answered(p_match, q, 'low') AND private.dating_guided_answered(p_match, q, 'high') THEN
      v_revealed := v_revealed + 1;
    ELSIF v_current IS NULL AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_guided_skips AS s
                                             WHERE s.match_id = p_match AND s.question_id = q) THEN
      v_current := q;
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
  RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('question_id', v_current,
    'answered', v_current IS NOT NULL AND private.dating_guided_answered(p_match, v_current, p_side),
    'revealed', v_revealed);
END;
$$;

-- A message [p_side] may read: a guided answer of the other side only once
-- this side has answered the same question (§3.6: both appear together).
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.dating_message_visible(p_m public.dating_messages, p_side text)
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT p_m.kind <> 'guided_answer' OR p_m.sender_side = p_side
      OR private.dating_guided_answered(p_m.match_id, p_m.question_id, p_side);
$$;

-- §3.6 the RPC: {ok, question_id, answered, revealed} | {ok:false,
-- reason: invalid | reverify_device | match_unavailable}.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_dating_guided_state(p_match_id uuid)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
VOLATILE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_user constant uuid := auth.uid();
  v_m public.dating_matches;
BEGIN
  IF v_user IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'not_authenticated' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;
  IF p_match_id IS NULL THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'invalid');
  END IF;
  IF public.dating_require_trusted_device() IS NOT TRUE THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'reverify_device');
  END IF;
  SELECT * INTO v_m FROM public.dating_matches AS m WHERE m.id = p_match_id AND v_user IN (m.user_low_id, m.user_high_id);
  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'match_unavailable');
  END IF;
  RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true)
      || private.dating_guided_state(p_match_id, CASE WHEN v_m.user_low_id = v_user THEN 'low' ELSE 'high' END);
END;
$$;

-- §3.6: either may skip the current question; it is skipped for both.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.skip_dating_guided_question(p_match_id uuid, p_question_id text)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_user constant uuid := auth.uid();
  v_m public.dating_matches;
  v_side text;
BEGIN
  IF v_user IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'not_authenticated' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;
  IF p_match_id IS NULL OR p_question_id IS NULL THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'invalid');
  END IF;
  IF public.dating_require_trusted_device() IS NOT TRUE THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'reverify_device');
  END IF;
  SELECT * INTO v_m FROM public.dating_matches AS m
   WHERE m.id = p_match_id AND v_user IN (m.user_low_id, m.user_high_id) FOR NO KEY UPDATE;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'match_unavailable');
  END IF;
  IF v_m.state <> 'active' THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'closed');
  END IF;
  v_side := CASE WHEN v_m.user_low_id = v_user THEN 'low' ELSE 'high' END;
  IF private.dating_guided_state(p_match_id, v_side) ->> 'question_id' IS DISTINCT FROM p_question_id THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'invalid');
  END IF;
  INSERT INTO public.dating_guided_skips (match_id, question_id, skipped_by_side)
  VALUES (p_match_id, p_question_id, v_side) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
  PERFORM private.dating_bump_chat(CASE WHEN v_side = 'low' THEN v_m.user_high_id ELSE v_m.user_low_id END, p_match_id);
  RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true) || private.dating_guided_state(p_match_id, v_side);
END;
$$;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2. We met, the timebox cards, Close kindly.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- §3.6 "We met": this side's mark (once); counted.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.mark_dating_we_met(p_match_id uuid)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_user constant uuid := auth.uid();
  v_m public.dating_matches;
  v_side text;
BEGIN
  IF v_user IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'not_authenticated' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;
  IF p_match_id IS NULL THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'invalid');
  END IF;
  IF public.dating_require_trusted_device() IS NOT TRUE THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'reverify_device');
  END IF;
  SELECT * INTO v_m FROM public.dating_matches AS m
   WHERE m.id = p_match_id AND v_user IN (m.user_low_id, m.user_high_id) FOR NO KEY UPDATE;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'match_unavailable');
  END IF;
  IF v_m.state <> 'active' THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'closed');
  END IF;
  v_side := CASE WHEN v_m.user_low_id = v_user THEN 'low' ELSE 'high' END;
  IF (CASE WHEN v_side = 'low' THEN v_m.low_we_met_at ELSE v_m.high_we_met_at END) IS NULL THEN
    UPDATE public.dating_matches AS m
       SET low_we_met_at = CASE WHEN v_side = 'low' THEN now() ELSE m.low_we_met_at END,
           high_we_met_at = CASE WHEN v_side = 'high' THEN now() ELSE m.high_we_met_at END
     WHERE m.id = p_match_id;
    PERFORM private.bump_metric_internal('dating.we_met');
  END IF;
  RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
END;
$$;

-- §3.6 the timebox cards, once to each person: 'day7' or 'day21' seen.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.dismiss_dating_timebox(p_match_id uuid, p_card text)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_user constant uuid := auth.uid();
BEGIN
  IF v_user IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'not_authenticated' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;
  IF p_match_id IS NULL OR p_card IS NULL OR p_card NOT IN ('day7', 'day21') THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'invalid');
  END IF;
  UPDATE public.dating_matches AS m
     SET low_day7_at = CASE WHEN p_card = 'day7' AND m.user_low_id = v_user THEN coalesce(m.low_day7_at, now()) ELSE m.low_day7_at END,
         high_day7_at = CASE WHEN p_card = 'day7' AND m.user_high_id = v_user THEN coalesce(m.high_day7_at, now()) ELSE m.high_day7_at END,
         low_day21_at = CASE WHEN p_card = 'day21' AND m.user_low_id = v_user THEN coalesce(m.low_day21_at, now()) ELSE m.low_day21_at END,
         high_day21_at = CASE WHEN p_card = 'day21' AND m.user_high_id = v_user THEN coalesce(m.high_day21_at, now()) ELSE m.high_day21_at END
   WHERE m.id = p_match_id AND v_user IN (m.user_low_id, m.user_high_id);
  RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
END;
$$;

-- §3.6 Close kindly: the reviewed message, sent as this side's last, then
-- the match closes for both (closed_kindly, sealed for the 30-day
-- evidence window) and the pair is never introduced again. Never gated by
-- the phone (it reduces exposure). {ok} | {ok:false, reason: invalid |
-- match_unavailable | closed | busy}. The lock order of block and exit.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.close_dating_match_kindly(p_idempotency_key text, p_match_id uuid)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
SET lock_timeout = '2s'
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_user constant uuid := auth.uid();
  v_text constant text := 'Thank you for chatting with me. I don''t think we''re a match, and I wish you well.';
  v_m public.dating_matches;
  v_side text;
  v_try integer;
BEGIN
  IF v_user IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'not_authenticated' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;
  IF p_match_id IS NULL OR NOT private.dating_key_ok(p_idempotency_key) THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'invalid');
  END IF;
  FOR v_try IN 1..3 LOOP
    BEGIN
      PERFORM private.dating_lock_setup(v_user);
      SELECT * INTO v_m FROM public.dating_matches AS m
       WHERE m.id = p_match_id AND v_user IN (m.user_low_id, m.user_high_id) FOR UPDATE;
      IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'match_unavailable');
      END IF;
      v_side := CASE WHEN v_m.user_low_id = v_user THEN 'low' ELSE 'high' END;
      IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_messages AS d
                  WHERE d.match_id = p_match_id AND d.sender_side = v_side AND d.idempotency_key = p_idempotency_key) THEN
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
      END IF;
      IF v_m.state <> 'active' THEN
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'closed');
      END IF;
      PERFORM 1 FROM public.dating_introductions AS i WHERE i.id = v_m.introduction_id FOR UPDATE;
      INSERT INTO public.dating_messages (match_id, sender_side, sender_user_id, kind, body, body_masked, mask_version, idempotency_key)
      VALUES (p_match_id, v_side, v_user, 'close_kindly', v_text, v_text, 1, p_idempotency_key);
      UPDATE public.dating_matches AS m
         SET state = 'closed', closed_reason = 'closed_kindly', closed_at = coalesce(m.closed_at, now()),
             evidence_until = now() + interval '30 days'
       WHERE m.id = p_match_id;
      PERFORM private.dating_mark_pair(v_m.user_low_id, v_m.user_high_id);
      PERFORM private.dating_bump_chat(CASE WHEN v_side = 'low' THEN v_m.user_high_id ELSE v_m.user_low_id END, p_match_id);
      PERFORM private.bump_metric_internal('dating.close_kindly');
      RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
    EXCEPTION WHEN deadlock_detected OR lock_not_available OR serialization_failure THEN
      IF v_try = 3 THEN
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'busy');
      END IF;
    END;
  END LOOP;
  RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'busy');
END;
$$;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3. The check-in (§3.6 meeting kit, §3.13): one neutral push at the time
--    the person chose (from 5 minutes to 7 days ahead): no place, no
--    match, no name; a new one replaces the last; cancel removes it. Not
--    gated by the phone, never cancelled by quick exit (a safety tool).
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.schedule_dating_checkin(p_at timestamptz)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_user constant uuid := auth.uid();
BEGIN
  IF v_user IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'not_authenticated' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;
  IF p_at IS NULL OR p_at < now() + interval '5 minutes' OR p_at > now() + interval '7 days' THEN
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'invalid');
  END IF;
  BEGIN
    PERFORM public.check_dating_rate_limit('dating_checkin', 20, interval '1 day');
  EXCEPTION WHEN invalid_parameter_value THEN
    IF SQLERRM IS DISTINCT FROM 'rate_limited' THEN
      RAISE;
    END IF;
    RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'rate_limited');
  END;
  UPDATE public.scheduled_notifications AS n SET status = 'cancelled', updated_at = now()
   WHERE n.user_id = v_user AND n.status = 'pending' AND n.metadata ->> 'type' = 'me_checkin';
  INSERT INTO public.scheduled_notifications (user_id, notification_type, scheduled_for, status, metadata, created_at, updated_at)
  VALUES (v_user, 'immediate', p_at, 'pending',
          pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('type', 'me_checkin', 'route', 'me_checkin', 'title', 'Attune',
                                        'body', 'A quick check-in from Attune.'),
          now(), now());
  RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'at', p_at);
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.cancel_dating_checkin()
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
BEGIN
  IF auth.uid() IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'not_authenticated' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;
  UPDATE public.scheduled_notifications AS n SET status = 'cancelled', updated_at = now()
   WHERE n.user_id = auth.uid() AND n.status = 'pending' AND n.metadata ->> 'type' = 'me_checkin';
  RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
END;
$$;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4. The retention purge (§4.8; carry 9), daily at 02:47 UTC. Each step is
--    one bounded statement in its own error block (counted by SQLSTATE as
--    dating.purge.failed.<step>), so one failing step never stops the
--    others and no lock is held across steps; answers each step's count.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.purge_dating_retention(p_now timestamptz DEFAULT now())
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_out jsonb := '{}'::jsonb;
  v_n integer;
  v_step text;
BEGIN
  FOREACH v_step IN ARRAY ARRAY['matches', 'introductions', 'pair_markers', 'moderation_cases', 'opener_hashes',
                                'safety_events', 'safety_outbox', 'rate_limits', 'block_hmacs', 'enforcement_records',
                                'session_context'] LOOP
    BEGIN
      CASE v_step
      -- A closed match after its 30-day window, with its messages (cascade),
      -- unless an open case holds it (§4.8 "unless a report holds it").
      WHEN 'matches' THEN
        DELETE FROM public.dating_matches AS m
         WHERE m.id IN (SELECT x.id FROM public.dating_matches AS x
                         WHERE x.state <> 'active' AND x.evidence_until < p_now
                           AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_moderation_cases AS c
                                            WHERE c.match_id = x.id AND c.state IN ('open', 'in_review'))
                         LIMIT 5000);
      -- A resolved introduction keeps only its ids, pair key, state and
      -- dates 30 days on (its interest actions go).
      WHEN 'introductions' THEN
        WITH old AS (
          SELECT i.id FROM public.dating_introductions AS i
           WHERE i.state IN ('passed', 'matched', 'expired', 'invalidated')
             AND greatest(i.expires_at, i.created_at) < p_now - interval '30 days'
             AND (i.low_action IS NOT NULL OR i.high_action IS NOT NULL OR cardinality(i.reasons) > 0 OR i.rank_detail IS NOT NULL
                  OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_interest_actions AS a WHERE a.introduction_id = i.id))
           LIMIT 5000
        ), gone AS (
          DELETE FROM public.dating_interest_actions AS a USING old WHERE a.introduction_id = old.id
        )
        UPDATE public.dating_introductions AS i SET low_action = NULL, high_action = NULL, reasons = '{}', rank_detail = NULL
          FROM old WHERE i.id = old.id;
      WHEN 'pair_markers' THEN
        DELETE FROM public.dating_pair_markers AS k WHERE k.purge_after < p_now;
      WHEN 'moderation_cases' THEN
        DELETE FROM public.dating_moderation_cases AS c WHERE c.created_at < p_now - interval '2 years';
      WHEN 'opener_hashes' THEN
        DELETE FROM public.dating_opener_hashes AS k WHERE k.created_at < p_now - interval '30 days';
      -- Safety events after 90 days keep only their family, tier, rule and
      -- version (nobody, no match, no message).
      WHEN 'safety_events' THEN
        UPDATE public.dating_safety_events AS e
           SET at_risk_user_id = NULL, sender_user_id = NULL, match_id = NULL, message_id = NULL
         WHERE e.created_at < p_now - interval '90 days'
           AND (e.at_risk_user_id IS NOT NULL OR e.sender_user_id IS NOT NULL OR e.match_id IS NOT NULL OR e.message_id IS NOT NULL);
      WHEN 'safety_outbox' THEN
        DELETE FROM public.dating_message_safety_outbox AS o
         WHERE o.state = 'done' AND o.completed_at < p_now - interval '7 days';
      WHEN 'rate_limits' THEN
        DELETE FROM public.dating_rate_limits AS r WHERE r.window_started_at < p_now - interval '2 days';
      WHEN 'block_hmacs' THEN
        DELETE FROM public.dating_block_hmacs AS h WHERE h.purge_after < p_now;
      WHEN 'enforcement_records' THEN
        DELETE FROM public.enforcement_records AS r WHERE r.scope = 'dating' AND r.purge_after < p_now;
      WHEN 'session_context' THEN
        DELETE FROM public.dating_session_context AS c WHERE c.checked_at < p_now - interval '30 days';
      END CASE;
      GET DIAGNOSTICS v_n = ROW_COUNT;
      v_out := v_out || pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(v_step, v_n);
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
      PERFORM private.bump_metric_internal('dating.purge.failed.' || v_step);
      v_out := v_out || pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(v_step, 'failed:' || SQLSTATE);
    END;
  END LOOP;
  RETURN v_out;
END;
$$;

DO $$
BEGIN
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_catalog.pg_namespace WHERE nspname = 'cron') THEN
    PERFORM cron.unschedule(j.jobid) FROM cron.job AS j WHERE j.jobname = 'purge-dating-retention';
    PERFORM cron.schedule('purge-dating-retention', '47 2 * * *', $c$SELECT public.purge_dating_retention()$c$);
  END IF;
END $$;
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Spec 2 W6c: builds supabase/migrations/20261120120000_dating_v2_chat_guided.sql

BEFORE = D / 'before_w6c'

  'public.send_dating_message(text,uuid,text,text,text)': [
    ("""     OR p_kind IS DISTINCT FROM 'text' OR p_question_id IS NOT NULL""",
     """     OR p_kind IS NULL OR p_kind NOT IN ('text', 'guided_answer') -- Spec 2 W6c: text, or a guided answer
     OR (p_kind = 'text') <> (p_question_id IS NULL) -- Spec 2 W6c: a guided answer names its question"""),
    ("""      BEGIN
        PERFORM public.check_dating_rate_limit('dating_message', 500, interval '1 day');""",
     """      IF p_kind = 'guided_answer' -- Spec 2 W6c: the current question, not answered yet by this side
         AND (private.dating_guided_state(p_match_id, v_side) ->> 'question_id' IS DISTINCT FROM p_question_id -- Spec 2 W6c
              OR private.dating_guided_answered(p_match_id, p_question_id, v_side)) THEN -- Spec 2 W6c
        RETURN pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'invalid'); -- Spec 2 W6c
      END IF; -- Spec 2 W6c
      BEGIN
        PERFORM public.check_dating_rate_limit('dating_message', 500, interval '1 day');"""),
    ("""      VALUES (p_match_id, v_side, v_user, 'text', NULL, p_body, v_masked, 1, p_idempotency_key)""",
     """      VALUES (p_match_id, v_side, v_user, p_kind, p_question_id, p_body, v_masked, 1, p_idempotency_key) -- Spec 2 W6c"""),
  ],
  # 2. The read shows the other side's guided answer only once this side
  #    has answered the same question; a hidden one never lights the dot.
  'public.get_dating_messages(uuid,timestamp with time zone,integer)': [
    ("""           WHERE d.match_id = p_match_id AND (p_before IS NULL OR d.created_at < p_before)""",
     """           WHERE d.match_id = p_match_id AND (p_before IS NULL OR d.created_at < p_before)
             AND private.dating_message_visible(d, v_side) -- Spec 2 W6c (§3.6): answers appear together"""),
    ("""                    WHERE d.match_id = m.id AND d.sender_side <> v_side
                      AND d.created_at > coalesce(""",
     """                    WHERE d.match_id = m.id AND d.sender_side <> v_side
                      AND private.dating_message_visible(d, v_side) -- Spec 2 W6c
                      AND d.created_at > coalesce("""),
  ],
  'public.get_my_dating_matches(boolean)': [
    ("""                                   WHERE d.match_id = m.id AND d.sender_side <> s.side
                                     AND d.created_at > coalesce(s.seen, '-infinity'))) AS row""",
     """                                   WHERE d.match_id = m.id AND d.sender_side <> s.side
                                     AND private.dating_message_visible(d, s.side) -- Spec 2 W6c
                                     AND d.created_at > coalesce(s.seen, '-infinity'))) AS row"""),
    ("""            LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT d.* FROM public.dating_messages AS d WHERE d.match_id = m.id
                                ORDER BY""",
     """            LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT d.* FROM public.dating_messages AS d WHERE d.match_id = m.id
                                  AND private.dating_message_visible(d, s.side) -- Spec 2 W6c
                                ORDER BY"""),
  ],
  # 3. The header: each side's We met, and the timebox card due for this
  #    side (day 21 once, if neither met; else day 7 once), never a date.
  'private.dating_chat_header(dating_matches,text)': [
    ("""                                          'from', p_m.matched_at + interval '72 hours'));""",
     """                                          'from', p_m.matched_at + interval '72 hours'), -- Spec 2 W6c
    'we_met', pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object( -- Spec 2 W6c
      'mine', (CASE WHEN p_side = 'low' THEN p_m.low_we_met_at ELSE p_m.high_we_met_at END) IS NOT NULL, -- Spec 2 W6c
      'theirs', (CASE WHEN p_side = 'low' THEN p_m.high_we_met_at ELSE p_m.low_we_met_at END) IS NOT NULL), -- Spec 2 W6c
    'timebox', CASE WHEN p_m.state <> 'active' OR v_other IS NULL THEN NULL -- Spec 2 W6c (§3.6)
                    WHEN now() >= p_m.matched_at + interval '21 days' -- Spec 2 W6c
                         AND p_m.low_we_met_at IS NULL AND p_m.high_we_met_at IS NULL -- Spec 2 W6c
                         AND (CASE WHEN p_side = 'low' THEN p_m.low_day21_at ELSE p_m.high_day21_at END) IS NULL THEN 'day21' -- Spec 2 W6c
                    WHEN now() >= p_m.matched_at + interval '7 days' AND now() < p_m.matched_at + interval '21 days' -- Spec 2 W6c
                         AND (CASE WHEN p_side = 'low' THEN p_m.low_day7_at ELSE p_m.high_day7_at END) IS NULL THEN 'day7' -- Spec 2 W6c
               END); -- Spec 2 W6c"""),
  ],
  # 4. §5.3 open chats: and neither said "We met".
  'private.dating_open_chats(uuid,timestamp with time zone)': [
    ("""   WHERE m.state = 'active' AND p_user IN (m.user_low_id, m.user_high_id)""",

     AND m.low_we_met_at IS NULL AND m.high_we_met_at IS NULL -- Spec 2 W6c (§5.3): neither said We met"""),
  ],
  # 5. R17: the check-in the person chose is never folded by quiet hours.
  # 7. Health shows the purge's failed steps (each step its own counter).
  'public.get_dating_operational_health()': [
    ("""                                 WHERE e.created_at >= now() - interval '24 hours' GROUP BY e.family) AS x), -- Spec 2 W6b
""",
     """                                 WHERE e.created_at >= now() - interval '24 hours' GROUP BY e.family) AS x), -- Spec 2 W6b
    -- Spec 2 W6c (§4.8): the retention purge's failed steps, per step, 7 days
    'purge_failed_7d',(SELECT coalesce(jsonb_object_agg(x.step, x.n), '{}'::jsonb) FROM ( -- Spec 2 W6c
                         SELECT substr(c.key, char_length('dating.purge.failed.') + 1) AS step, sum(c.count) AS n -- Spec 2 W6c
                           FROM public.metric_counters AS c -- Spec 2 W6c
                          WHERE left(c.key, char_length('dating.purge.failed.')) = 'dating.purge.failed.' -- Spec 2 W6c
                            AND c.day >= (now() AT TIME ZONE 'utc')::date - 6 -- Spec 2 W6c
                          GROUP BY 1) AS x), -- Spec 2 W6c
"""),
  ],
  'public.scheduled_notifications_quiet_guard()': [
    ("""  IF NEW.delivery_channel IS DISTINCT FROM 'push' OR v_type = 'safety_resources' THEN""",
     """  IF NEW.delivery_channel IS DISTINCT FROM 'push' OR v_type IN ('safety_resources', 'me_checkin') THEN -- Spec 2 W6c (R17): a check-in the person timed"""),
  ],
}

out = [(D / 'mig_T8_head.sql').read_text().rstrip('\n'), '',
       '-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------',
       '-- 5. Functions re-created from their live bodies; only the lines marked',
       '--    "-- Spec 2 W6c" change.',
       '-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------']
for sig, pairs in EDITS.items():
    body = (BEFORE / f'{sig}.sql').read_text().rstrip('\n')
    for old, new in pairs:
        n = body.count(old)
        assert n == 1, f'{sig}: anchor occurs {n} times: {old[:80]!r}'
        body = body.replace(old, new)
    out += ['', body + ';']
out += ['', 'COMMIT;', '']
pathlib.Path('supabase/migrations/20261120120000_dating_v2_chat_guided.sql').write_text('\n'.join(out))
print(f'20261120120000_dating_v2_chat_guided.sql: {len(EDITS)} functions re-created')
```

```sql
-- Spec 2 W6c: grants for 20261120120000_dating_v2_chat_guided.sql. The
-- guided skips are no API role's; the guided state, the skip, We met, the
-- timebox cards, Close kindly and the check-in are the app's; the purge is
-- the cron's (postgres) alone; the helpers are the database's own.
BEGIN;
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';
REVOKE ALL ON TABLE public.dating_guided_skips FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_guided_questions() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_guided_answered(uuid, text, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_guided_state(uuid, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.dating_message_visible(public.dating_messages, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.purge_dating_retention(timestamptz) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.get_dating_guided_state(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.get_dating_guided_state(uuid) TO authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.skip_dating_guided_question(uuid, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.skip_dating_guided_question(uuid, text) TO authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.mark_dating_we_met(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.mark_dating_we_met(uuid) TO authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.dismiss_dating_timebox(uuid, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.dismiss_dating_timebox(uuid, text) TO authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.close_dating_match_kindly(text, uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.close_dating_match_kindly(text, uuid) TO authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.schedule_dating_checkin(timestamptz) FROM PUBLIC, anon, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.schedule_dating_checkin(timestamptz) TO authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.cancel_dating_checkin() FROM PUBLIC, anon, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.cancel_dating_checkin() TO authenticated;
COMMIT;
```

```bash
D=.superpowers/sdd/2026-11-20-spec2-w6
python3 -I $D/build_mig_T8.py
for f in supabase/migrations/20261120120000_dating_v2_chat_guided.sql supabase/migrations/20261120120010_dating_v2_chat_guided_grants.sql; do
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `test/features/dating/chat/guided_test.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 2 W6c (§3.6): the guided first conversation (the current question;
// Answer sends it as a guided answer; then "{Name} will see your answer
// once {Name} answers too."; Skip this one; the tip after the first
// reveal), the day-7 and day-21 cards (each answered once), Close kindly
// (the reviewed message, confirmed) and We met from the Safety sheet; none
// of it on an ended chat.
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat_screen.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations_en.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

import '../../../helpers/flow_harness.dart';
import '../../../helpers/tap_in_reach.dart';
import '../support/dating_chat_fakes.dart';
import '../support/dating_shell.dart';

Future<void> tap(WidgetTester t, Finder finder) async {
  await tapInReach(t, finder);
  await t.pump();
  await t.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 300));
}

Map<String, Object?> page({
  String? timebox,
  bool weMet = false,
  bool ended = false,
}) {
  final p = datingPageJson(ended: ended);
  final chat = Map<String, Object?>.from(p['chat']! as Map);
  chat['timebox'] = timebox;
  chat['we_met'] = {'mine': weMet, 'theirs': false};
  return {...p, 'chat': chat};
}

void main() {
  final loc = AppLocalizationsEn();

  Future<void> open(WidgetTester t, FakeDatingChat fake) {
    if (fake.guidedState.questionId == null && fake.guidedState.revealed == 0) {
      fake.guidedState = const DatingGuided(questionId: 'good_saturday');
    }
    return pumpDatingShell(
      t,
      initial: '/dating/chat/m1',
      overrides: datingChatOverrides(fake),
    );
  }

  testWidgets('the current question; Answer sends it as this side\'s answer; '
      'then the waiting line', (t) async {
    final fake = FakeDatingChat(page: page());
    await open(t, fake);
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_guidedTitle), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_guided_good_saturday), findsOneWidget);
    await tap(t, find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_guided_answer_button')));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    await t.enterText(
      find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_guided_answer')),
      'Market, then football',
    );
    await t.pump();
    fake.guidedState = const DatingGuided(
      questionId: 'good_saturday',
      answered: true,
    );
    await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_chatSend));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(fake.answers.single.$2, 'good_saturday');
    expect(fake.answers.single.$3, 'Market, then football');
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_guidedWaiting('Kofi')), findsOneWidget);
    expect(
      find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_guided_answer_button')),
      findsNothing,
    );
  });

  testWidgets('Skip this one skips the current question; the tip follows '
      'the first reveal', (t) async {
    final fake = FakeDatingChat(page: page());
    await open(t, fake);
    fake.guidedState = const DatingGuided(
      questionId: 'food_every_week',
      revealed: 1,
    );
    await tap(t, find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_guided_skip')));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(fake.skips, ['good_saturday']);
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_guided_food_every_week), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_guidedTip('Kofi')), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('day 7: Not yet answers it once; day 21: Close kindly asks '
      'first, then closes', (t) async {
    final fake = FakeDatingChat(page: page(timebox: 'day7'));
    await open(t, fake);
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_timeboxDay7), findsOneWidget);
    await tap(t, find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_timebox_not_yet')));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(fake.timeboxes, ['day7']);
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_timeboxDay7), findsNothing);

    final late = FakeDatingChat(page: page(timebox: 'day21'));
    await open(t, late);
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_timeboxDay21), findsOneWidget);
    await tap(t, find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_timebox_close')));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_closeKindlyTitle), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_closeKindlyMessage), findsOneWidget);
    await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_closeKindlySend));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(late.kindCloses.length, 1);
    expect(late.timeboxes, ['day21']);
  });

  testWidgets('the Safety sheet offers Close kindly and We met (once)', (
    t,
  ) async {
    final fake = FakeDatingChat(page: page());
    await open(t, fake);
    await tap(t, find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_chat_safety')));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_closeKindly), findsOneWidget);
    await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_weMet));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(fake.weMets, 1);

    final met = FakeDatingChat(page: page(weMet: true));
    await open(t, met);
    await tap(t, find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_chat_safety')));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_weMet), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('an ended chat shows no guided or timebox card', (t) async {
    final fake = FakeDatingChat(page: page(timebox: 'day21', ended: true));
    await open(t, fake);
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_guidedTitle), findsNothing);
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_timeboxDay21), findsNothing);
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_chatEnded), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('goldens and guidelines with the guided and day-7 cards', (
    t,
  ) async {
    final fake = FakeDatingChat(page: page(timebox: 'day7'))
      ..guidedState = const DatingGuided(questionId: 'good_saturday');
    await expectFlowGoldens(
      t,
      'dating_chat_guided',
      () => DatingChatScreen(matchId: 'm1', now: () => datingChatT0),
      overrides: [...datingShellOverrides(), ...datingChatOverrides(fake)],
    );
    await expectFlowGuidelines(t);
  });
}
```

```dart
// Spec 2 W6c (§3.6): the guided first conversation's reviewed questions,
// by the server's ids (nothing written by AI).
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';

String datingGuidedQuestion(AppLocalizations loc, String id) => switch (id) {
  'good_saturday' => loc.dating_guided_good_saturday,
  'food_every_week' => loc.dating_guided_food_every_week,
  'looking_forward' => loc.dating_guided_looking_forward,
  'proud_of' => loc.dating_guided_proud_of,
  'laugh_with' => loc.dating_guided_laugh_with,
  'where_you_grew_up' => loc.dating_guided_where_you_grew_up,
  'good_relationship' => loc.dating_guided_good_relationship,
  'time_with_family' => loc.dating_guided_time_with_family,
  'before_first_meeting' => loc.dating_guided_before_first_meeting,
  _ => '',
};
```

```python
   r"""    FlowSecondaryButton(label: loc.dating_chatOk, onPressed: () => close(null)),
  ]);
});

/// Spec 2 W6c (§3.6) Close kindly's confirm: the reviewed message, then
/// [Send and end] or [Cancel]. True when the person chose to send. Offline,
/// it waits (nothing is queued).
Future<bool> showDatingCloseKindlySheet(
  BuildContext context, {
  required String? account,
}) async =>
    await _show<bool>(context, 'chat-close-kindly', account, (context, close) {
      return Consumer(
        builder: (context, ref, _) {
          final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
          final theme = Theme.of(context);
          final online = ref.watch(datingOnlineProvider).valueOrNull ?? true;
          return _frame(context, loc.dating_closeKindlyTitle, [
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: theme.colorScheme.surfaceContainerHighest,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              ),
              child: Text(
                loc.dating_closeKindlyMessage,
                style: theme.textTheme.bodyLarge,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: Spacing.md),
            if (!online) DatingCalmLine(loc.dating_offline),
            FlowPrimaryButton(
              label: loc.dating_closeKindlySend,
              onPressed: online ? () => close(true) : null,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
            FlowSecondaryButton(
              label: loc.dating_blockCancel,
              onPressed: () => close(false),
            ),
          ]);
        },
      );
    }) ==
    true;

/// Spec 2 W6c (§3.6): this side's answer to the guided question; the text,
/// or null when the person went back.
Future<String?> showDatingGuidedAnswerSheet(
  BuildContext context, {
  required String? account,
  required String question,
}) => _show<String>(context, 'chat-guided', account, (context, close) {
  return _GuidedAnswer(question: question, close: close);
});

class _GuidedAnswer extends StatefulWidget {
  const _GuidedAnswer({required this.question, required this.close});

  final String question;
  final void Function(String?) close;

  @override
  State<_GuidedAnswer> createState() => _GuidedAnswerState();
}

class _GuidedAnswerState extends State<_GuidedAnswer> {
  final _text = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _text.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    return Padding(
      padding: EdgeInsets.only(
        bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom,
      ),
      child: _frame(context, widget.question, [
        TextField(
          key: const ValueKey('dating_guided_answer'),
          controller: _text,
          minLines: 2,
          maxLines: 6,
          maxLength: 1000,
          enableSuggestions: false,
          autocorrect: false,
          enableIMEPersonalizedLearning: false,
          decoration: InputDecoration(
            hintText: loc.dating_chatComposerHint,
            border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
        ValueListenableBuilder<TextEditingValue>(
          valueListenable: _text,
          builder:
              (context, value, _) => FlowPrimaryButton(
                label: loc.dating_chatSend,
                onPressed:
                    value.text.trim().isEmpty
                        ? null
                        : () => widget.close(_text.text),
              ),
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }
}
"""),

/// Spec 2 W6c (§3.6): the reviewed questions, in order.
const datingGuidedQuestions = [
  'good_saturday',
  'food_every_week',
  'looking_forward',
  'proud_of',
  'laugh_with',
  'where_you_grew_up',
  'good_relationship',
  'time_with_family',
  'before_first_meeting',
];

/// Spec 2 W6c (§3.6): the guided first conversation as this side sees it.
class DatingGuided {
  const DatingGuided({
    this.questionId,
    this.answered = false,
    this.revealed = 0,
  });

  static const none = DatingGuided();

  /// The current question; null once every one is answered or skipped.
  final String? questionId;

  /// This side answered it (the other side's answer waits for both).
  final bool answered;

  /// How many questions both have answered (the tip follows the first).
  final int revealed;

  factory DatingGuided.fromJson(Object? raw) {
    if (raw is! Map || raw['ok'] != true) return none;
    final q = raw['question_id'];
    return DatingGuided(
      questionId: q is String && datingGuidedQuestions.contains(q) ? q : null,
      answered: raw['answered'] == true,
      revealed: raw['revealed'] is int ? raw['revealed'] as int : 0,
    );
  }
}
"""),
], 'app: Task 9')

      'p_body': text,
      'p_kind': 'guided_answer',
      'p_question_id': questionId,
    }),

   r"""    }
  }

  /// §3.6 Close kindly: the reviewed message, then the chat ends for both.
  Future<void> _closeKindly() async {
    final account = _account;
    if (!await showDatingCloseKindlySheet(context, account: account)) return;
    if (!mounted || _account != account) return;
    final r = await _gateway.closeKindly(
      _keys.keyFor('close', widget.matchId),
      widget.matchId,
    );
    _keys.settle('close', reason: r.reason);
    if (!mounted || _account != account) return;
    setState(() => _actionReason = r.ok ? null : r.reason);
    unawaited(_read());
  }

  /// §3.6: the timebox card is answered once.
  void _timebox(String card) {
    setState(() => _timeboxDone = card);
    unawaited(_gateway.dismissTimebox(widget.matchId, card));
  }

  Future<void> _answer(String questionId) async {
    final account = _account;
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final text = await showDatingGuidedAnswerSheet(
      context,
      account: account,
      question: datingGuidedQuestion(loc, questionId),
    );
    if (text == null || !mounted || _account != account) return;
    final r = await _gateway.answerGuided(
      _keys.keyFor('guided', [widget.matchId, questionId, text]),
      widget.matchId,
      questionId,
      text,
    );
    _keys.settle('guided', reason: r.reason);
    if (!mounted || _account != account) return;
    setState(() => _actionReason = r.ok ? null : r.reason);
    unawaited(_read());
  }

  Future<void> _skip(String questionId) async {
    final account = _account;
    await _gateway.skipGuided(widget.matchId, questionId);
    if (mounted && _account == account) unawaited(_read());
  }

  /// §3.6: the timebox card, then the guided card (and its tip), at the top.
  List<Widget> _topCards(
    BuildContext context,
    AppLocalizations loc,
    DatingChatHeader h,
  ) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final name =
        h.displayName.isEmpty ? loc.dating_cardThisPerson : h.displayName;
    Widget card(String key, List<Widget> children) => Container(
      key: ValueKey(key),
      margin: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(Spacing.md, Spacing.sm, Spacing.md, 0),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: theme.colorScheme.surfaceContainerHighest,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
      ),
      child: Semantics(
        container: true,
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: children,
        ),
      ),
    );
    Widget actions(List<Widget> buttons) => Wrap(
      alignment: WrapAlignment.end,
      spacing: Spacing.sm,
      children: buttons,
    );
    Widget button(String key, String label, VoidCallback onPressed) =>
        TextButton(
          key: ValueKey(key),
          style: TextButton.styleFrom(
            minimumSize: const Size(48, 48),
            foregroundColor: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
          ),
          onPressed: onPressed,
          child: Text(label),
        );
    final out = <Widget>[];
    final timebox = h.timebox == _timeboxDone ? null : h.timebox;
    if (timebox == 'day7') {
      out.add(
        card('dating_timebox_day7', [
          Text(loc.dating_timeboxDay7, style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium),
          actions([
            button(
              'dating_timebox_not_yet',
              loc.dating_timeboxNotYet,
              () => _timebox('day7'),
            ),
          ]),
        ]),
      );
    } else if (timebox == 'day21') {
      out.add(
        card('dating_timebox_day21', [
          Text(loc.dating_timeboxDay21, style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium),
          actions([
            button(
              'dating_timebox_keep',
              loc.dating_timeboxKeep,
              () => _timebox('day21'),
            ),
            button('dating_timebox_close', loc.dating_closeKindly, () {
              _timebox('day21');
              unawaited(_closeKindly());
            }),
          ]),
        ]),
      );
    }
    final q = _guided.questionId;
    if (q != null) {
      out.add(
        card('dating_guided', [
          Text(loc.dating_guidedTitle, style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium),
          const SizedBox(height: Spacing.xs),
          Text(datingGuidedQuestion(loc, q), style: theme.textTheme.bodyLarge),
          if (_guided.answered)
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: Spacing.sm),
              child: Text(
                loc.dating_guidedWaiting(name),
                style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium,
              ),
            )
          else
            actions([
              button(
                'dating_guided_skip',
                loc.dating_guidedSkip,
                () => _skip(q),
              ),
              button(
                'dating_guided_answer_button',
                loc.dating_guidedAnswer,
                () => _answer(q),
              ),
            ]),
        ]),
      );
    }
    // §3.6: after the first reveal, once (until the next).
    if (_guided.revealed == 1) {
      out.add(
        Padding(
          key: const ValueKey('dating_guided_tip'),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
            Spacing.md,
            Spacing.sm,
            Spacing.md,
            0,
          ),
          child: Text(
            loc.dating_guidedTip(name),
            style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium,
          ),
        ),
      );
    }
    return out;
  }
"""),
  (r"""        if (!ended) _swapRow(context, loc, header),
""",
   r"""        // Spec 2 W6c: the swap row and the top cards scroll within at most
        // two fifths of the screen (large text never pushes the chat away).
        if (!ended)
          ConstrainedBox(
            constraints: BoxConstraints(
              maxHeight: MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).height * 0.4,
            ),
            child: SingleChildScrollView(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  _swapRow(context, loc, header),
                  ..._topCards(context, loc, header),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
"""),

    weMets++;
    return endAnswer;

      timeboxes.add(card);

  Future<DatingChatAnswer> closeKindly(String key, String matchId) async {

    return endAnswer;
```

```dart
  await DatingPlanMemory.clear();
  DatingPlanMemory.account = 'ama';
  DatingPlanMemory.place = place;
  DatingPlanMemory.at = _meet;
}

void main() {
  final loc = AppLocalizationsEn();
  final shared = <String>[];
  final texts = <String>[];
  List<Override> kit(FakeDatingChat fake) => [
    ...datingChatOverrides(fake),
    datingShareProvider.overrideWithValue((text) async => shared.add(text)),
    datingSmsProvider.overrideWithValue((body) async => texts.add(body)),
  ];
  final resources = GoRoute(
    path: SafetyResourcesScreen.routeName,
    builder: (_, __) => const Scaffold(body: Text('RESOURCES')),
  );

  setUp(() async {
    shared.clear();
    texts.clear();
    await DatingPlanMemory.clear();
  });

  testWidgets('the checklist; Share and Send wait for When and Where; the '
      'words carry the plan and never the name', (t) async {
    final fake = FakeDatingChat();
    await pumpDatingShell(
      t,
      initial: '/dating/chat/m1/plan',
      overrides: kit(fake),
    );
    for (final line in [
      loc.dating_planCheck1,
      loc.dating_planCheck2,
      loc.dating_planCheck3,
      loc.dating_planCheck4,
      loc.dating_planCheck5,
    ]) {
      expect(find.text(line), findsOneWidget);
    }
    await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_planShare));
    expect(shared, isEmpty);

    await _remember('Accra Mall food court');
    await pumpDatingShell(
      t,
      initial: '/dating/chat/m1/plan',
      overrides: kit(fake),
    );
    expect(find.text('Accra Mall food court'), findsOneWidget);
    await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_planShare));
    await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_planSms));
    final words = loc.dating_planShareText(
      MyDateFormat.toDate(_meet),
      MyDateFormat.toTime(_meet),
      'Accra Mall food court',
      MyDateFormat.toTime(_meet.add(const Duration(hours: 3))),
    );
    expect(shared, [words]);
    expect(texts, [words]);
    expect(words, isNot(contains('Kofi')));
  });

  testWidgets('Check on me asks for one push three hours after the meet; '
      'Cancel takes it back', (t) async {
    await _remember('Accra Mall food court');
    final fake = FakeDatingChat();
    await pumpDatingShell(
      t,
      initial: '/dating/chat/m1/plan',
      overrides: kit(fake),
    );
    await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_planCheckOnMe));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(fake.checkins, [_meet.add(kDatingCheckinAfter)]);
    expect(kDatingCheckinAfter, const Duration(hours: 3));
    expect(
      find.text(
        loc.dating_planCheckinSet(
          MyDateFormat.toTime(_meet.add(kDatingCheckinAfter)),
        ),
      ),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_planCheckinCancel));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(fake.checkins.last, isNull);
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_planCheckOnMe), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('another account sees no plan; quick exit and sign-out forget '
      'it', (t) async {
    await _remember('Accra Mall food court');
    final s = await pumpDatingShell(
      t,
      initial: '/dating/chat/m1/plan',
      overrides: kit(FakeDatingChat()),
    );
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_planTitle), findsWidgets);
    expect(find.text('Accra Mall food court'), findsOneWidget);
    s.container.read(datingTestAccount.notifier).state = 'kwame';
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.byType(DatingPlanMeetScreen), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_planCheck1), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Accra Mall food court'), findsNothing);
    expect(DatingPlanMemory.place, isNull);

    for (final moment in [WipeMoment.quickExit, WipeMoment.signOut]) {
      await _remember('Osu');
      final registry = DeviceWipeRegistry();
      registerDatingDeviceWipes(registry);
      await registry.run(moment);
      expect(DatingPlanMemory.place, isNull, reason: '$moment');
      expect(DatingPlanMemory.at, isNull, reason: '$moment');
    }
  });

  testWidgets('Plan a meet from the Safety sheet, the day-7 card and the '
      'meet-in-private card (not the threat card)', (t) async {
    await pumpDatingShell(
      t,
      initial: '/dating/chat/m1',
      overrides: kit(FakeDatingChat()),
    );
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_planCheck1), findsNothing);
    await tap(t, find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_chat_safety')));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_planTitle));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_planCheck1), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.byType(DatingPlanMeetScreen), findsOneWidget);

    final page = datingPageJson();
    final chat = Map<String, Object?>.from(page['chat']! as Map)
      ..['timebox'] = 'day7';
    final day7 = FakeDatingChat(page: {...page, 'chat': chat});
    await pumpDatingShell(t, initial: '/dating/chat/m1', overrides: kit(day7));
    await tap(t, find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_timebox_plan')));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_planCheck1), findsOneWidget);
    expect(day7.timeboxes, ['day7']);

    final cards =
        FakeDatingChat()
          ..cardList = const [
            DatingSafetyCard(
              id: 'e1',
              messageId: 'd2',
              family: 'threat_intimidation',
            ),
            DatingSafetyCard(
              id: 'e2',
              messageId: 'd1',
              family: 'meet_private_pressure',
            ),
          ];
    await pumpDatingShell(t, initial: '/dating/chat/m1', overrides: kit(cards));
    expect(find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_card_plan')), findsOneWidget);
    expect(
      find.descendant(
        of: find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_card_e2')),
        matching: find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_card_plan')),
      ),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    await tap(t, find.byKey(const ValueKey('dating_card_plan')));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_planCheck1), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('the check-in: Get help asks first, then the support '
      'directory and a text the person sends', (t) async {
    await _remember('Osu');
    final s = await pumpDatingShell(
      t,
      initial: '/dating/checkin',
      extraRoutes: [resources],
      overrides: kit(FakeDatingChat()),
    );
    for (final label in [
      loc.dating_checkinOk,
      loc.dating_checkinHelp,
      loc.dating_weMet,
      loc.dating_checkinNotNow,
    ]) {
      expect(find.text(label), findsOneWidget);
    }
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_checkinMessageSomeone), findsNothing);
    await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_checkinHelp));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_checkinHelpTitle), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('RESOURCES'), findsNothing);
    await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_checkinHelpShow));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('RESOURCES'), findsOneWidget);
    s.router.pop();
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_checkinMessageSomeone));
    expect(texts, [loc.dating_checkinHelpText('Osu')]);
    expect(texts.single, 'I need help. I\'m at Osu. Please call me.');

    await DatingPlanMemory.clear();
    await pumpDatingShell(
      t,
      initial: '/dating/checkin',
      extraRoutes: [resources],
      overrides: kit(FakeDatingChat()),
    );
    await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_checkinHelp));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_blockCancel));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('RESOURCES'), findsNothing);
    expect(find.text(loc.dating_checkinMessageSomeone), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('We met opens the conversations; Not now leaves', (t) async {
    final s = await pumpDatingShell(
      t,
      initial: '/dating/checkin',
      overrides: kit(FakeDatingChat()),
    );
    await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_weMet));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(s.location, '/dating/chats');
    final n = await pumpDatingShell(
      t,
      initial: '/dating/checkin',
      overrides: kit(FakeDatingChat()),
    );
    await tap(t, find.text(loc.dating_checkinNotNow));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(n.location, startsWith('/home'));
  });

  testWidgets('goldens and guidelines: the meeting kit and the check-in', (
    t,
  ) async {
    await _remember('Accra Mall food court');
    await expectFlowGoldens(
      t,
      'dating_plan_meet',
      () => const DatingPlanMeetScreen(matchId: 'm1'),
      overrides: [...datingShellOverrides(), ...kit(FakeDatingChat())],
    );
    await expectFlowGuidelines(t);
    await expectFlowGoldens(
      t,
      'dating_checkin',
      () => const DatingCheckinScreen(),
      overrides: [...datingShellOverrides(), ...kit(FakeDatingChat())],
    );
    await expectFlowGuidelines(t);
  });
}
```

Create `test/core/notifications/me_checkin_routing_test.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 2 W6c (§3.6 Check on me, §3.13): the neutral check-in push opens
// the check-in behind the Dating lock: locked, the Safety PIN comes first.
import 'package:attune/core/notifications/config/notification_config.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/notifications/domain/entities/app_notification.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations_en.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../features/dating/support/dating_shell.dart';

void main() {
  final loc = AppLocalizationsEn();
  for (final locked in [true, false]) {
    testWidgets('me_checkin opens the check-in (locked: $locked)', (t) async {
      late BuildContext tapContext;
      final s = await pumpDatingShell(
        t,
        initial: '/somewhere',
        locked: locked,
        pinSet: true,
        extraRoutes: [
          GoRoute(
            path: '/somewhere',
            builder:
                (context, _) => Builder(
                  builder: (c) {
                    tapContext = c;
                    return const Scaffold(body: Text('ELSEWHERE'));
                  },
                ),
          ),
        ],
      );
      buildNanoEmbryoNotificationConfig().onNotificationTap!(
        AppNotification(
          id: 'n1',
          title: 'Attune',
          body: 'A quick check-in from Attune.',
          data: const {'type': 'me_checkin', 'route': 'me_checkin'},
          createdAt: DateTime.utc(2026, 11, 21, 18, 30),
        ),
        tapContext,
      );
      await t.pumpAndSettle();
      if (locked) {
        expect(s.location, startsWith('/dating/unlock?from='));
        expect(find.text(loc.dating_checkinTitle), findsNothing);
        expect(find.text('Enter your Safety PIN'), findsOneWidget);
      } else {
        expect(s.location, '/dating/checkin');
        expect(find.text(loc.dating_checkinOk), findsOneWidget);
      }
    });
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The meeting kit and the check-in**

Create `lib/features/dating/chat/plan_meet_screen.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 2 W6c (§3.6 meeting kit, §3.15 Meeting kit; S21): Plan a meet. A
// reviewed checklist; "When?" and "Where?" held in memory only (never on
// the server, never on disk: quick exit and sign-out forget them); Share
// my plan through the system share sheet and Send as text message, both
// with the plan's words and never the match's name or photo; Check on me
// schedules one neutral push three hours after the meet (the only server
// row: a time and a route). Nothing here moves; Share works offline,
// Check on me needs a connection.
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/app/theme/design_tokens.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/ui/text/allow_large_text.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/utils/date_formatter.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_buttons.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/dating_chat_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/data/dating_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/gentle_wait/gentle_wait_providers.dart'
    show datingOnlineProvider;
import 'package:attune/features/dating/section/dating_calm_line.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:share_plus/share_plus.dart';
import 'package:url_launcher/url_launcher.dart';

/// §3.6: the check-in comes three hours after the meet.
const kDatingCheckinAfter = Duration(hours: 3);

/// The plan in memory only (D20): read by Get help's message, forgotten by
/// quick exit and sign-out (Dating's wipe entry) and by another account.
abstract final class DatingPlanMemory {
  static String? place;
  static DateTime? at;
  static String? account;

  static Future<void> clear() async {
    place = null;
    at = null;
    account = null;
  }
}

/// The system share sheet (tests replace it).
final datingShareProvider = Provider<Future<void> Function(String text)>(
  (ref) => (text) async => Share.share(text),
);

/// A text message the person sends themselves (tests replace it).
final datingSmsProvider = Provider<Future<void> Function(String body)>(
  (ref) =>
      (body) async =>
          launchUrl(Uri(scheme: 'sms', queryParameters: {'body': body})),
);

/// The plan's words: never the match's name or photo.
String datingPlanText(AppLocalizations loc, DateTime at, String place) =>
    loc.dating_planShareText(
      MyDateFormat.toDate(at),
      MyDateFormat.toTime(at),
      place,
      MyDateFormat.toTime(at.add(kDatingCheckinAfter)),
    );

class DatingPlanMeetScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const DatingPlanMeetScreen({super.key, required this.matchId, this.now});

  final String matchId;
  final DateTime Function()? now;

  @override
  ConsumerState<DatingPlanMeetScreen> createState() =>
      _DatingPlanMeetScreenState();
}

class _DatingPlanMeetScreenState extends ConsumerState<DatingPlanMeetScreen> {
  final _place = TextEditingController();
  DateTime? _at;
  DateTime? _checkinAt;
  String? _reason;
  bool _scheduling = false;

  DateTime get _now => (widget.now ?? DateTime.now)();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final account = ref.read(datingAccountProvider);
    if (DatingPlanMemory.account == account) {
      _place.text = DatingPlanMemory.place ?? '';
      _at = DatingPlanMemory.at;
    } else {
      unawaited(DatingPlanMemory.clear());
    }
    _place.addListener(_remember);
    ref.listenManual<String?>(datingAccountProvider, (previous, next) {
      if (previous == next || !mounted) return;
      unawaited(DatingPlanMemory.clear());
      setState(() {
        _place.text = '';
        _at = null;
        _checkinAt = null;
        _reason = null;
      });
    });
  }

  void _remember() {
    DatingPlanMemory.account = ref.read(datingAccountProvider);
    DatingPlanMemory.place =
        _place.text.trim().isEmpty ? null : _place.text.trim();
    DatingPlanMemory.at = _at;
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _place.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _pickWhen() async {
    final now = _now;
    final day = await showDatePicker(
      context: context,
      firstDate: DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day),
      lastDate: now.add(const Duration(days: 7)),
      initialDate: _at ?? now,
    );
    if (day == null || !mounted) return;
    final time = await showTimePicker(
      context: context,
      initialTime: TimeOfDay.fromDateTime(_at ?? now),
    );
    if (time == null || !mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _at = DateTime(day.year, day.month, day.day, time.hour, time.minute);
      _remember();
    });
  }

  String? get _text {
    final at = _at;
    final place = _place.text.trim();
    if (at == null || place.isEmpty) return null;
    return datingPlanText(AppLocalizations.of(context)!, at, place);
  }

  Future<void> _checkOnMe() async {
    final at = _at;
    if (at == null || _scheduling) return;
    final account = ref.read(datingAccountProvider);
    setState(() => _scheduling = true);
    final when = at.add(kDatingCheckinAfter);
    final r = await ref.read(datingChatGatewayProvider).scheduleCheckin(when);
    if (!mounted || ref.read(datingAccountProvider) != account) return;
    setState(() {
      _scheduling = false;
      _checkinAt = r.ok ? when : null;
      _reason = r.ok ? null : r.reason;
    });
  }

  Future<void> _cancelCheckin() async {
    final r = await ref.read(datingChatGatewayProvider).cancelCheckin();
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      if (r.ok) _checkinAt = null;
      _reason = r.ok ? null : r.reason;
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final online = ref.watch(datingOnlineProvider).valueOrNull ?? true;
    final text = _text;
    final at = _at;
    final checkin = _checkinAt;
    final items = [
      loc.dating_planCheck1,
      loc.dating_planCheck2,
      loc.dating_planCheck3,
      loc.dating_planCheck4,
      loc.dating_planCheck5,
    ];
    return AllowLargeText(
      child: Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(title: Text(loc.dating_planTitle)),
        body: SafeArea(
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                Semantics(
                  container: true,
                  explicitChildNodes: true,
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                    children: [
                      for (final item in items)
                        Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: Spacing.sm),
                          child: Row(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              const ExcludeSemantics(
                                child: Icon(Icons.check_circle_outline),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(width: Spacing.sm),
                              Expanded(
                                child: Text(
                                  item,
                                  style: theme.textTheme.bodyLarge,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: Spacing.md),
                Semantics(
                  header: true,
                  child: Text(
                    loc.dating_planWhen,
                    style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: Spacing.xs),
                FlowSecondaryButton(
                  label:
                      at == null
                          ? loc.dating_planPickWhen
                          : MyDateFormat.join([
                            MyDateFormat.toDate(at),
                            MyDateFormat.toTime(at),
                          ]),
                  onPressed: _pickWhen,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: Spacing.md),
                Semantics(
                  header: true,
                  child: Text(
                    loc.dating_planWhere,
                    style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: Spacing.xs),
                TextField(
                  key: const ValueKey('dating_plan_place'),
                  controller: _place,
                  maxLength: 120,
                  enableSuggestions: false,
                  autocorrect: false,
                  enableIMEPersonalizedLearning: false,
                  onChanged: (_) => setState(() {}),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    hintText: loc.dating_planWhereHint,
                    border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: Spacing.md),
                FlowPrimaryButton(
                  label: loc.dating_planShare,
                  onPressed:
                      text == null
                          ? null
                          : () =>
                              unawaited(ref.read(datingShareProvider)(text)),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
                FlowSecondaryButton(
                  label: loc.dating_planSms,
                  onPressed:
                      text == null
                          ? null
                          : () => unawaited(ref.read(datingSmsProvider)(text)),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: Spacing.md),
                if (!online) DatingCalmLine(loc.dating_offline),
                if (checkin == null)
                  FlowSecondaryButton(
                    label: loc.dating_planCheckOnMe,
                    onPressed:
                        at == null || !online || _scheduling
                            ? null
                            : _checkOnMe,
                  )
                else ...[
                  DatingCalmLine(
                    loc.dating_planCheckinSet(MyDateFormat.toTime(checkin)),
                  ),
                  FlowTextLink(
                    label: loc.dating_planCheckinCancel,
                    onPressed: () => unawaited(_cancelCheckin()),
                  ),
                ],
                if (_reason != null)
                  DatingCalmLine(
                    _reason == 'rate_limited'
                        ? loc.dating_errorRateLimited
                        : _reason == 'invalid'
                        ? loc.dating_planCheckinInvalid
                        : loc.dating_errorGeneric,
                  ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

Create `lib/features/dating/chat/checkin_screen.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 2 W6c (§3.6 Check-in, Get help; §3.13): where the neutral
// "A quick check-in from Attune." push opens (behind the Dating lock).
// "How did it go?" [I'm OK] [Get help] [We met] [Not now]. Get help asks
// first ("Show emergency numbers?"), then opens the country's support
// directory (S1 W7a, emergency first), and offers a text the person sends
// themselves: "I need help. I'm at {place}. Please call me." Nothing is
// ever sent automatically. Nothing here moves.
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/app/routing/app_router.dart' show RouteNames;
import 'package:attune/app/theme/design_tokens.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/ui/text/allow_large_text.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_buttons.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/chat/plan_meet_screen.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/data/dating_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/lock/dating_sheets.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/dating/routes/dating_routes.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

class DatingCheckinScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const DatingCheckinScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<DatingCheckinScreen> createState() =>
      _DatingCheckinScreenState();
}

class _DatingCheckinScreenState extends ConsumerState<DatingCheckinScreen> {
  bool _help = false;

  void _leave() =>
      context.canPop() ? context.pop() : context.go(RouteNames.home);

  Future<void> _getHelp() async {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final show = await showModalBottomSheet<bool>(
      context: context,
      routeSettings: datingSheetSettings('checkin-help'),
      builder:
          (sheet) => DatingSheetTracker(
            child: AllowLargeText(
              child: SafeArea(
                child: SingleChildScrollView(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    children: [
                      Semantics(
                        header: true,
                        child: Text(
                          loc.dating_checkinHelpTitle,
                          style: Theme.of(sheet).textTheme.titleLarge,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: Spacing.md),
                      FlowPrimaryButton(
                        label: loc.dating_checkinHelpShow,
                        onPressed: () => Navigator.of(sheet).pop(true),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
                      FlowSecondaryButton(
                        label: loc.dating_blockCancel,
                        onPressed: () => Navigator.of(sheet).pop(false),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
    );
    if (show != true || !mounted) return;
    setState(() => _help = true);
    unawaited(context.push(RouteNames.safetyResources));
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final account = ref.watch(datingAccountProvider);
    final place =
        DatingPlanMemory.account == account ? DatingPlanMemory.place : null;
    return AllowLargeText(
      child: Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(title: Text(loc.dating_checkinTitle)),
        body: SafeArea(
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                Semantics(
                  header: true,
                  child: Text(
                    loc.dating_checkinTitle,
                    style: theme.textTheme.headlineSmall,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: Spacing.lg),
                FlowPrimaryButton(
                  label: loc.dating_checkinOk,
                  onPressed: _leave,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
                FlowSecondaryButton(
                  label: loc.dating_checkinHelp,
                  onPressed: () => unawaited(_getHelp()),
                ),
                if (_help) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
                  FlowSecondaryButton(
                    label: loc.dating_checkinMessageSomeone,
                    onPressed:
                        () => unawaited(
                          ref.read(datingSmsProvider)(
                            place == null
                                ? loc.dating_checkinHelpTextNoPlace
                                : loc.dating_checkinHelpText(place),
                          ),
                        ),
                  ),
                ],
                const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
                // We met names no match here (the push carries none): the
                // conversations, where each chat's Safety sheet says it.
                FlowSecondaryButton(
                  label: loc.dating_weMet,
                  onPressed: () => context.go(DatingPaths.chats),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
                FlowTextLink(
                  label: loc.dating_checkinNotNow,
                  onPressed: _leave,
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```
