edit('lib/architecture/DATING_CHAT_SAFETY_SPEC.md', [
  (r"""**Status:** Draft for safety-owner review. Nothing in this document is
approved. Dating chat may not ship until Section 12's blockers are checked.
""",
   r"""**Status:** Version 2.0 (2026-11-20, Spec 2 W6c). The v2.0 amendments
below were approved by the product owner under Q20 of
`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-24-dating-mode-design.md` (§1.5). The
safety owner's review is still required: Dating chat may not ship to
production until Section 12's blockers are checked.
"""),
  (r"""12 is the release gate.
""",
   r"""12 is the release gate.

---

## Version 2.0 amendments (they override the sections they name)

- **§1.4 and §2.4 — own surface.** Dating chat has its own screen, widgets,
  table (`dating_messages`), outbox (`dating_message_safety_outbox`) and
  worker (`process-dating-safety-outbox`). It shares no transport, widget or
  table with couples chat: dating messages never enter `public.messages`,
  `relationship_message_counters` or the couples safety outbox. There are no
  read receipts, typing indicators or presence, and no in-chat Games.
- **§3.1 — new families.** `sextortion` (tier 2, the country's support
  resource), `meet_private_pressure` (tier 1, "Plan a meet") and
  `child_access` (tier 3 to moderation, with a recipient card) join the v1
  families, with `duplicate_opener` (the same first message to three or
  more matches) and cross-match `money_asks` as moderation signals. The
  lexicon is `dating_chat_v1` in `dating_safety_configs`; the Twi, Ga, Ewe
  and Pidgin entries come from the cultural reviewer.
- **§3.4 — own normaliser.** Dating uses Spec 1's Unicode-safe normaliser
  (`_shared/unicode_normaliser.ts`, its fold table and goldens) through
  `_shared/dating_chat_safety.ts`; the couples `chat_safety.ts` is untouched.
- **§4.1 — presentation rules.** Contact masking (phone numbers, emails,
  handles and links shown as "•••" until both people tap Swap contacts,
  offered from 72 hours after the match) and the swap card are decided by
  the server when it reads a message. Delivery never waits on safety: the
  message is stored and delivered first, and detection runs after it
  (fail-open). Masking never lifts by itself.
- **§5.1 — holds.** A report can lead to an interim hold only for a
  corroborated P0 (design spec §6.2, built in W7a). Phrase triggers still
  never restrict an account.
- **§5.2 — categories.** The report categories are the design spec's
  §3.7 categories (built in W7a), replacing the list below.
- **§7 — 30-day evidence window.** When a match closes (block, unmatch,
  close kindly, leaving or account deletion), its messages stay sealed for
  30 days, then `purge_dating_retention` deletes them, unless an open
  moderation case holds the match. Safety events are anonymised at 90 days;
  moderation cases are kept 2 years; one-way pair markers (no names, no
  ids) are kept 2 years so a pair is never introduced again.
"""),
  (r"""
- **2026-09-04** — Initial draft. Written against
""",
   r"""
- **2026-11-20** — Version 2.0: the amendments approved under Q20 of the
  Dating Mode design (§1.5), recorded at the top of this document (Spec 2
  W6c). The safety owner's §12 review is still pending.

- **2026-09-04** — Initial draft. Written against
"""),
], 'app: Task 10')

## Owner checklist (nothing here is done by an implementer)

1. **W6's production gate (spec §8 W6; recorded in `private.flag_production_gates` before any Dating chat flag may turn on in production).**
   - The safety owner checks every box in `lib/architecture/DATING_CHAT_SAFETY_SPEC.md` §12 (v2.0) and approves the v2.0 amendments recorded at its top.
   - The DV specialist signs the sextortion card, the Get help copy and the meeting kit (Task 10's `dating_plan*` and `dating_checkin*` keys: the checklist, "I'm meeting someone from Attune on {date} at {time} at {place}. I'll check in by {checkin}.", "Show emergency numbers?", "I need help. I'm at {place}. Please call me.", and the plan copy "I need help. Please call me." for no plan), Task 7's card lines, and Close kindly's message.
   - The cultural reviewer's Twi, Ga, Ewe and Pidgin lexicon entries (stored in Spec 1's match form) replace the `[DRAFT]` sets of `dating_chat_v1`, the golden fixtures pass, and the config is activated in production with a real `review_ref` (the safety owner's sign-off), not starting `DEV-` (the trigger refuses `DEV-` there; an active config is frozen: a change is a new version).
   - W5's gate (carry 10) stays as recorded.
2. **DPIA lines (owner decision 1; Rulings R-39, R-40).**
   - Pair markers: a keyed one-way code of two phone HMACs (no names, no ids, no phone), written when a person taps Not for me, blocks, unmatches or closes kindly; kept 2 years so a pair is never introduced again, even after leaving Dating or deleting the account; purged daily after 2 years. Purpose: safety and the person's own choice. Lawful basis and the Leave screen's wording (it already says blocks persist) for counsel.
   - Held identity: after a Dating-country change, the last identity answer stays encrypted (`held_identity_enc`) until the person answers again, read only by the same-gender switch; cleared on a new answer, on a restricting country and by Leave's hard delete.
   - Dating messages: kept while the match is open; sealed for 30 days after it closes (block, unmatch, close kindly, leaving, account deletion), then deleted unless an open moderation case holds the match (Q15, Q17 R-2). Safety events hold rule ids, never text; they lose their people at 90 days.
3. **§7.7 device checks for W6 (release builds, the development project, `supabase/sql/dev_enable_spec2_dating_flags.sql` run).**
   - TalkBack and VoiceOver through a full chat: the list (name, age, last line, the new dot), the header and Safety button label, messages with sender names, a hidden span read as "hidden contact detail" and its sheet, Sending… and the failure retry, Swap's chip (disabled with its time, then the sheet and states), a safety card and its buttons, the guided card, the day-7 and day-21 cards, Close kindly, the meeting kit and the check-in.
   - 200% text and RTL on the list, the chat, every sheet, the meeting kit and the check-in; the composer's send button at least 48 dp.
   - Two phones, two test accounts matched by the dev generation: messages arrive through the chat signal without a refresh; a number typed by one shows "•••" to the other until both swap (and never before 72 hours); Block on one while the other types: the next send answers "This conversation has ended." and nothing arrives; Unmatch shows the ended banner on both.
   - A message containing a seeded money ask: the recipient's card appears under it within a minute (the worker's cron), the sender sees nothing; the neutral "You have something new in Attune." push arrives at most once per 15 minutes and names no one.
   - Check on me: the neutral "A quick check-in from Attune." push arrives at the chosen time even inside quiet hours; with the Dating lock on, tapping it asks for the Safety PIN first, then shows "How did it go?"; Get help asks first, then shows the country's emergency number; Message someone you trust opens the SMS app with the text and sends nothing by itself.
   - Quick exit from a chat and from the meeting kit: back in the app nothing of the chat or the plan shows; the Dating lock, the Safety PIN and the plain icon unchanged. A second account on the same phone sees nothing of the first's chats or plan.
   - Android screenshots and screen recording blocked on the chat; iOS app switcher covered.
4. **Deploy (owner only; Dating stays off in production).**
   - `supabase db push` after every earlier undeployed wave (Spec 1 W1–W10, Spec 2 W1–W5), in order: `20261120100000_dating_v2_chat.sql`, `20261120100010_dating_v2_chat_grants.sql`, `20261120110000_dating_v2_chat_safety.sql`, `20261120110010_dating_v2_chat_safety_grants.sql`, `20261120120000_dating_v2_chat_guided.sql`, `20261120120010_dating_v2_chat_guided_grants.sql`.
   - Deploy `process-dating-safety-outbox` (default `verify_jwt`; it needs no new secret: the cron calls it through `public.invoke_edge_function` with the service role).
   - Crons after the push: `process-dating-safety-outbox` every minute, `recover-stale-dating-workers` every 5 minutes, `purge-dating-retention` daily at 02:47. Alerts on `get_dating_operational_health()`'s chat counters (`dating.safety.dead_letter`, `dating.safety.no_config`, `dating.chat.push_failed`, `dating.pair_marker.unwritten`), `purge_failed_7d` and the `chat_safety_outbox` backlog.
   - `dating_push_key` and `dating_exclusion_key` present in the production vault (without the exclusion key no pair marker is written and it is counted).
   - Re-run `supabase/sql/dev_enable_spec2_dating_flags.sql` on the development project (it activates `dating_chat_v1` under `DEV-` references).
   - Ship the W6 app build with or after the W6a push (the build calls the new chat RPCs; with Dating off nothing reaches them).
5. **Plan copy for the reviewers** (each marked "Plan copy" in its ARB description; the full list is Tasks 4, 7, 9 and 10's fragments): the chat's error and calm lines, the Safety sheet's row labels, Unmatch's done line, the masked sheet's title, the card's "this person" fallback and no-number lines, the guided waiting and tip lines' name fallbacks, the meeting kit's "Choose a date and time", "Attune will check on you at {time}.", "Cancel the check-in", "Choose a time from today to a week from now.", "Show", and "I need help. Please call me.".
6. **Owner decisions (one line each).** R-41: 500 messages a day per sender — confirm or set the number. R-43: Report waits for W7a and "Become a couple on Attune" for W9 — confirm (or ask for an interim Report row). R-46: Check on me from 5 minutes to 7 days ahead, and the check-in's We met opening Conversations — confirm. R-47: the purge's plan windows (outbox 7 days, rate limits 2 days, openers 30 days, session context 30 days, events anonymised at 90 days) — confirm with counsel. R-39/R-40: the DPIA lines above. Visible for a nod: R-37 (file location), R-38 (mask once at insert; a later masker doesn't re-mask history), R-42 (push collapse), R-44 (draft lexicon), R-45 (guided answers as messages), R-48 (dragnet input), the §6.1 platform-list amendment.
7. **Still open from W2–W5 (unchanged by W6):** W5's production gate and carries 10 and 12 (field-key rotation must not end every open introduction); W4's vendor contracts and DPIA biometric section; W3's cultural, LGBTQ+, Ghana legal and counsel reviews and the CSAM matcher; W2's clinician's note and DV reads.
8. **Carried to later waves (recorded, not built here):** W7a: Report from the Safety sheet, each card and the ended list (30 days), corroborated P0 holds, the report categories (chat spec v2 §5.1, §5.2); W8: the chat's caches in the device wipe registry beyond the plan (none kept today); W9: "Become a couple on Attune" after We met, the export of a person's own messages; W10: the old `dating_mutual_match` push case removed.

The plan was built in a scratch copy of the repo at `b8340ae5` on a clone of `attune_spec3a`, then replayed from this text: a fresh scratch copy reset to `b8340ae5` (the W6 tool folder holding only `carries.md`), a fresh clone of `attune_spec3a` (`createdb -T`), and a script that wrote every "Create … with exactly" block and ran every `bash` block in order (Tasks 1–10, in four runs: 1–2, 3–4, 5–7, 8–10, on the same copy and clone), with the database name swapped for the clone's. The replay found five things the plan now fixes: Task 4 Step 4 must also update `today_final_fix_test.dart`'s goldens (`match_moment.dark` and `.reduce` carry Say hello; the first W6a gate failed those two tests, the step and gate were re-run by hand after the fix and printed the Expected lines below); `build_mig_T5.py` changed a W5-tagged line in health without the W6b tag (`marked_diff` said `UNMARKED`; the line now carries both tags, the migration was rebuilt and re-applied on the clone, `marked_diff` then `OK` and Task 5's 12 mutants re-run RED); the dev-script check named a column that doesn't exist (`review_ref`, re-run by hand: `chat=dating_chat_v1:active:DEV-safety`); and the Deno and Dart "see it fail" filters for Tasks 1 and 9 (re-run by hand against a tree without the new file or at Task 7's state: the Expected lines below).
